## This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (resolved from this file's directory; in monorepos the `next` package may not be visible from the repo root) before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.

This block is written and re-added by `next dev` — verify at `node_modules/next/dist/server/lib/generate-agent-files.js`. Removing it from a diff only re-creates the uncommitted change; committing it with your work keeps the tree clean.

<!-- END:nextjs-agent-rules -->

===== \prisma.config.ts =====
import "dotenv/config";
import { defineConfig, env } from "prisma/config";

function databaseUrl(database: string) {
  const user = encodeURIComponent(env("PGUSER"));
  const password = encodeURIComponent(env("PGPASSWORD"));
  const host = env("PGHOST");
  const port = env("PGPORT");
  const name = encodeURIComponent(database);
  const ssl = process.env.PGSSLMODE === "require" ? "&sslmode=require" : "";

  return `postgresql://${user}:${password}@${host}:${port}/${name}?schema=public${ssl}`;
}

export default defineConfig({
  schema: "prisma/schema.prisma",
  migrations: {
    path: "prisma/migrations",
  },
  datasource: {
    url: databaseUrl(env("PGDATABASE")),
    ...(process.env.PGSHADOWDATABASE
      ? {
          shadowDatabaseUrl: databaseUrl(
            process.env.PGSHADOWDATABASE,
          ),
        }
      : {}),
  },
});

This is a [Next.js](https://nextjs.org) project bootstrapped with [`create-next-app`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/cli/create-next-app).

## Getting Started

First, run the development server:

```bash
npm run dev
# or
yarn dev
# or
pnpm dev
# or
bun dev
```

Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) with your browser to see the result.

You can start editing the page by modifying `app/page.tsx`. The page auto-updates as you edit the file.

This project uses [`next/font`](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/building-your-application/optimizing/fonts) to automatically optimize and load [Geist](https://vercel.com/font), a new font family for Vercel.

## Learn More

- [Next.js Documentation](https://nextjs.org/docs) - learn about Next.js features and API.
- [Learn Next.js](https://nextjs.org/learn) - an interactive Next.js tutorial.

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client"
  output   = "../src/generated/prisma"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"

enum ProductStatus {
  DRAFT
  ACTIVE
  ARCHIVED
}

model Category {
  id          String   @id @default(cuid())
  name        String
  slug        String   @unique
  description String?
  imageUrl    String?
  sortOrder   Int      @default(0)
  isActive    Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime @updatedAt

  parentId String?
  parent   Category?  @relation("CategoryTree", fields: [parentId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  children Category[] @relation("CategoryTree")

  products Product[]

  @@index([parentId, sortOrder])
}

model Product {
  id               String        @id @default(cuid())
  name             String
  slug             String        @unique
  shortDescription String?
  description      String        @default("")
  status           ProductStatus @default(DRAFT)
  isFeatured       Boolean       @default(false)
  seoTitle         String?
  seoDescription   String?
  createdAt        DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt        DateTime      @updatedAt

  categoryId String
  category   Category @relation(fields: [categoryId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  variants   ProductVariant[]
  images     ProductImage[]
  orderItems OrderItem[]
  reviews Review[]

  @@index([categoryId, status])
  @@index([status, isFeatured])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

model ProductVariant {
  id             String   @id @default(cuid())
  sku            String   @unique
  size           String?
  color          String?
  colorHex       String?
  price          Decimal  @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  compareAtPrice Decimal? @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  flashPrice      Decimal?    @db.Decimal(10, 2) 
  flashSaleEnd    DateTime?   

  stock          Int      @default(0)
  isActive       Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt      DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime @updatedAt

  productId  String
  product    Product     @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  orderItems OrderItem[]

  @@index([productId, isActive])
}

model ProductImage {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  url       String
  alt       String
  sortOrder Int      @default(0)
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  productId String
  product   Product @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([productId, sortOrder])
}

enum AdminRole {
  OWNER
  STAFF
}

model AdminUser {
  id           String    @id @default(cuid())
  name         String
  email        String    @unique
  passwordHash String
  role         AdminRole @default(STAFF)
  isActive     Boolean   @default(true)
  createdAt    DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime  @updatedAt

  sessions    AdminSession[]
  orderAudits OrderAudit[]
}

model AdminSession {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  tokenHash String   @unique
  expiresAt DateTime
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  adminId String
  admin   AdminUser @relation(fields: [adminId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([adminId])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model AdminLoginLimit {
  id        String   @id
  attempts  Int      @default(0)
  expiresAt DateTime

  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model StoreSetting {
  id                    String   @id @default("main")
  storeName             String   @default("BIMLAY")
  supportPhone          String?
  whatsappNumber        String?
  facebookPageUrl       String?
  supportEmail          String?
  dhakaDeliveryCharge   Decimal  @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  outsideDeliveryCharge Decimal  @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  codEnabled            Boolean  @default(true)
  updatedAt             DateTime @updatedAt
}

enum OrderStatus {
  PENDING
  CONFIRMED
  PROCESSING
  SHIPPED
  DELIVERED
  CANCELLED
  RETURNED
}

enum PaymentStatus {
  UNPAID
  PAID
  REFUNDED
}

enum DeliveryZone {
  DHAKA
  OUTSIDE_DHAKA
}

model Order {
  id               String @id @default(cuid())
  orderNumber      String @unique
  checkoutKey      String @unique
  receiptTokenHash String

  customerName String
  phone        String
  email        String?
  district     String
  address      String
  notes        String?

  deliveryZone DeliveryZone
  subtotal     Decimal      @db.Decimal(16, 2)
  deliveryFee  Decimal      @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  total        Decimal      @db.Decimal(16, 2)
  discount     Decimal      @default(0) @db.Decimal(16, 2)
  couponCode   String?
  couponId     String?
  coupon       Coupon?      @relation(fields: [couponId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  currency     String       @default("BDT")

  paymentMethod String        @default("COD")
  paymentStatus PaymentStatus @default(UNPAID)
  status        OrderStatus   @default(PENDING)

  stockReleasedAt DateTime?
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt

  items      OrderItem[]
  audits     OrderAudit[]
  customerId String?
  customer   Customer?    @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@index([phone])
  @@index([customerId, createdAt])
  @@index([couponId])
}

model OrderItem {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  orderId String
  order   Order  @relation(fields: [orderId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  productId String?
  product   Product? @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  variantId String?
  variant   ProductVariant? @relation(fields: [variantId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  productName String
  sku         String
  size        String?
  color       String?
  imageUrl    String?
  quantity    Int
  unitPrice   Decimal @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  lineTotal   Decimal @db.Decimal(16, 2)

  @@index([orderId])
  @@index([productId])
  @@index([variantId])
}

model CheckoutLimit {
  id        String   @id
  attempts  Int      @default(0)
  expiresAt DateTime

  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model OrderAudit {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  orderId String
  order   Order  @relation(fields: [orderId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  adminId String?
  admin   AdminUser? @relation(fields: [adminId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  actorEmail String
  event      String
  fromValue  String
  toValue    String
  note       String?
  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([orderId, createdAt])
  @@index([adminId])
}

model Customer {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  name            String
  email           String    @unique
  passwordHash    String
  isActive        Boolean   @default(true)
  emailVerifiedAt DateTime?
  createdAt       DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt

  sessions CustomerSession[]
  orders   Order[]
}

model CustomerSession {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  tokenHash String   @unique
  expiresAt DateTime
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  customerId String
  customer   Customer @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([customerId])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model CustomerAuthLimit {
  id        String   @id
  attempts  Int      @default(0)
  expiresAt DateTime

  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model HeroSlide {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  eyebrow      String?
  title        String
  subtitle     String?
  desktopImage String
  mobileImage  String?
  altText      String
  buttonText   String   @default("Shop Collection")
  buttonHref   String   @default("/shop")
  sortOrder    Int      @default(0)
  isActive     Boolean  @default(true)
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([isActive, sortOrder])
}

enum HomeSectionKey {
  FOR_HIM
  FOR_HER
  STORY
}

model HomeSection {
  key            HomeSectionKey @id
  eyebrow        String?
  title          String
  description    String?
  imageUrl       String?
  mobileImageUrl String?
  altText        String         @default("")
  buttonText     String
  buttonHref     String
  isActive       Boolean        @default(false)
  updatedAt      DateTime       @updatedAt
}

enum CouponKind {
  PERCENT
  FIXED
}

model Coupon {
  id              String     @id @default(cuid())
  code            String     @unique
  kind            CouponKind
  value           Decimal    @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  minimumSubtotal Decimal    @default(0) @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  maximumDiscount Decimal?   @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  usageLimit      Int?
  usedCount       Int        @default(0)
  startsAt        DateTime?
  expiresAt       DateTime?
  isActive        Boolean    @default(true)
  createdAt       DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime   @updatedAt

  orders Order[]
}

model Review {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  productId String
  product   Product  @relation(fields: [productId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  userId    String?
  userName  String
  rating    Int      // 1 theke 5 er moddhe rating
  comment   String?
  imageUrl  String?  // Photo ba media er jonno
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
}



===== \scripts\create-owner.ts =====
import "dotenv/config";

import { PrismaPg } from "@prisma/adapter-pg";
import { PrismaClient } from "../src/generated/prisma/client";
import { hashPassword } from "../src/lib/password";

function requiredEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name];

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(`Missing environment variable: ${name}`);
  }

  return value;
}

async function main() {
  const email = requiredEnv("BIMLAY_SETUP_EMAIL")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  const password = requiredEnv("BIMLAY_SETUP_PASSWORD");

  if (
    email.length > 254 ||
    !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Enter a valid admin email address.");
  }

  if (password.length < 12 || password.length > 128) {
    throw new Error("Password must contain 12 to 128 characters.");
  }

  const adapter = new PrismaPg({
    host: requiredEnv("PGHOST"),
    port: Number(requiredEnv("PGPORT")),
    user: requiredEnv("PGUSER"),
    password: requiredEnv("PGPASSWORD"),
    database: requiredEnv("PGDATABASE"),
    max: 1,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 5000,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 1000,
  });

  const prisma = new PrismaClient({ adapter });

  try {
    const existingAdminCount = await prisma.adminUser.count();

    if (existingAdminCount > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        "An admin account already exists. No account was changed.",
      );
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

    const owner = await prisma.adminUser.create({
      data: {
        name: "BIMLAY Owner",
        email,
        passwordHash,
        role: "OWNER",
      },
      select: {
        email: true,
        role: true,
      },
    });

    console.log("OWNER_CREATED_OK");
    console.log(`Email: ${owner.email}`);
    console.log(`Role: ${owner.role}`);
  } finally {
    await prisma.$disconnect();
  }
}

main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  if (error instanceof Error) {
    console.error(error.message);
  } else {
    console.error("Owner account creation failed.");
  }

  process.exitCode = 1;
});


===== \scripts\upload-media-to-cloudinary.ts =====
import "dotenv/config";

import { readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { v2 as cloudinary } from "cloudinary";

const MEDIA_DIR = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "storage",
  "product-images",
);

const FILENAME_PATTERN =
  /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}\.webp$/;

function required(name: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name];

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(`${name} is missing.`);
  }

  return value;
}

function errorParts(error: unknown): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  if (!error || typeof error !== "object") {
    return [];
  }

  const outer = error as Record<string, unknown>;
  const parts = [outer];

  if (outer.error && typeof outer.error === "object") {
    parts.push(outer.error as Record<string, unknown>);
  }

  return parts;
}

function isNotFound(error: unknown): boolean {
  return errorParts(error).some((part) => {
    if (part.http_code === 404) return true;

    return (
      typeof part.message === "string" &&
      /^Resource not found\b/i.test(part.message)
    );
  });
}

async function main() {
  cloudinary.config({
    cloud_name: required("CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME"),
    api_key: required("CLOUDINARY_API_KEY"),
    api_secret: required("CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET"),
    secure: true,
  });

  const filenames = (await readdir(MEDIA_DIR))
    .filter((filename) => FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename))
    .sort();

  console.log(`Found ${filenames.length} local images.`);

  let uploaded = 0;
  let alreadyPresent = 0;

  for (const filename of filenames) {
    const publicId =
      `bimlay/${filename.slice(0, -".webp".length)}`;

    try {
      await cloudinary.api.resource(publicId, {
        resource_type: "image",
      });

      alreadyPresent++;
      console.log(`Already uploaded: ${filename}`);
      continue;
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isNotFound(error)) {
        throw error;
      }
    }

    const image = await readFile(
      path.join(MEDIA_DIR, filename),
    );

    const dataUri =
      `data:image/webp;base64,${image.toString("base64")}`;

    const result = await cloudinary.uploader.upload(dataUri, {
      resource_type: "image",
      public_id: publicId,
      overwrite: false,
      unique_filename: false,
      format: "webp",
    });

    if (result.public_id !== publicId) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected Cloudinary ID for ${filename}.`,
      );
    }

    uploaded++;
    console.log(`Uploaded: ${filename}`);
  }

  console.log(
    `Complete. Uploaded: ${uploaded}; already present: ${alreadyPresent}.`,
  );
}

void main().catch((error: unknown) => {
  const parts = errorParts(error);

  const code = parts.find(
    (part) => typeof part.http_code === "number",
  )?.http_code;

  const message = parts.find(
    (part) => typeof part.message === "string",
  )?.message;

  const safeMessage =
    typeof message === "string"
      ? message
          .replaceAll(
            process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET ?? "\u0000",
            "[REDACTED]",
          )
          .replaceAll(
            process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_KEY ?? "\u0000",
            "[REDACTED]",
          )
          .replace(/[\r\n]/g, " ")
          .slice(0, 200)
      : "No error message returned.";

  console.error("Cloudinary HTTP status:", code ?? "unknown");
  console.error("Reason:", safeMessage);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

===== \src\app\layout.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Cormorant_Garamond, Manrope } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";

const manrope = Manrope({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-manrope",
});

const cormorant = Cormorant_Garamond({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  weight: ["400", "500", "600", "700"],
  variable: "--font-cormorant",
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  metadataBase: new URL("https://bimlay.com"),
  title: {
    default: "BIMLAY — Fashion & Lifestyle",
    template: "%s | BIMLAY",
  },
  description:
    "Discover men's and women's fashion, accessories and everyday lifestyle essentials at BIMLAY.",
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body className={`${manrope.variable} ${cormorant.variable}`}>
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}


===== \src\app\page.tsx =====


import Storefront from "@/components/storefront";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { getHeroSlides } from "@/lib/hero-slides";
import { getHomeSections } from "@/lib/home-sections";

// লাইভ ডেটা ফেচিংয়ের জন্য Next.js-এর নতুন নিয়ম
export const instant = false;

export default async function HomePage() {
  const [heroSlides, homeSections, rawCategories, featuredProducts] = await Promise.all([
    getHeroSlides(),
    getHomeSections(),
    db.category.findMany({
      orderBy: { name: "asc" },
      include: {
        products: {
          orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
          include: {
            images: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" }, take: 1 },
            variants: { orderBy: { price: "asc" } },
          },
        },
      },
    }),
    db.product.findMany({
      where: { isFeatured: true },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      take: 8,
      include: {
        images: { orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" }, take: 1 },
        variants: { orderBy: { price: "asc" } },
      },
    })
  ]);

  const categories = rawCategories.map((cat) => ({
    id: cat.id,
    name: cat.name,
    slug: cat.slug,
    parentId: cat.parentId,
    label: cat.name,
    imageUrl: cat.imageUrl ?? null,
  }));

  const categoryProducts = rawCategories
    .filter((cat) => cat.products && cat.products.length > 0)
    .map((cat) => ({
      category: {
        id: cat.id,
        name: cat.name,
        slug: cat.slug,
        parentId: cat.parentId,
        label: cat.name,
        imageUrl: cat.imageUrl ?? null,
      },
      products: cat.products.slice(0, 4).map((p) => {
        const defaultVariant = p.variants?.[0];
        const inStock = Boolean(p.variants?.some((v) => v.stock > 0));

        return {
          id: p.id,
          name: p.name,
          slug: p.slug,
          shortDescription: p.description ?? "",
          price: defaultVariant?.price ? String(defaultVariant.price) : "0",
          regularPrice: defaultVariant?.compareAtPrice ? String(defaultVariant.compareAtPrice) : null,
          image: p.images?.[0]?.url || "/branding/bimlay-logo.png",
          imageAlt: p.images?.[0]?.alt || p.name,
          inStock,
          hasMultipleVariants: (p.variants?.length ?? 0) > 1,
        };
      }),
    }));

  const allProducts = featuredProducts.map((p) => {
    const defaultVariant = p.variants?.[0];
    const inStock = Boolean(p.variants?.some((v) => v.stock > 0));

    return {
      id: p.id,
      name: p.name,
      slug: p.slug,
      shortDescription: p.description ?? "",
      price: defaultVariant?.price ? String(defaultVariant.price) : "0",
      regularPrice: defaultVariant?.compareAtPrice ? String(defaultVariant.compareAtPrice) : null,
      image: p.images?.[0]?.url || "/branding/bimlay-logo.png",
      imageAlt: p.images?.[0]?.alt || p.name,
      inStock,
      hasMultipleVariants: (p.variants?.length ?? 0) > 1,
    };
  });

  return (
    <Storefront
      mode="home"
      categories={categories}
      products={allProducts}
      categoryProducts={categoryProducts}
      heroSlides={heroSlides}
      homeSections={homeSections}
    />
  );
}


===== \src\app\robots.ts =====
import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";

export default function robots(): MetadataRoute.Robots {
  return {
    rules: {
      userAgent: "*",
      allow: "/",
      disallow: [
        "/admin",
        "/account",
        "/cart",
        "/checkout",
        "/order-confirmation",
        "/wishlist",
        "/api",
      ],
    },
    sitemap: "https://bimlay.com/sitemap.xml",
    host: "https://bimlay.com",
  };
}


===== \src\app\sitemap.ts =====
import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

const SITE_URL = "https://bimlay.com";

export default async function sitemap(): Promise<MetadataRoute.Sitemap> {
  await connection();

  const products = await db.product.findMany({
    where: {
      status: "ACTIVE",
      category: {
        isActive: true,
        OR: [
          { parentId: null },
          { parent: { isActive: true } },
        ],
      },
      images: {
        some: {},
      },
      variants: {
        some: {
          isActive: true,
        },
      },
    },
    select: {
      slug: true,
      updatedAt: true,
    },
    orderBy: {
      updatedAt: "desc",
    },
    take: 49000,
  });

  return [
    {
      url: SITE_URL,
      changeFrequency: "weekly",
      priority: 1,
    },
    {
      url: `${SITE_URL}/shop`,
      changeFrequency: "daily",
      priority: 0.9,
    },
    ...products.map((product) => ({
      url: `${SITE_URL}/products/${encodeURIComponent(product.slug)}`,
      lastModified: product.updatedAt,
      changeFrequency: "weekly" as const,
      priority: 0.7,
    })),
  ];
}

===== \src\app\account\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

import {
  hashPassword,
  verifyPassword,
} from "@/lib/password";
import {
  createCustomerSession,
  deleteCustomerSession,
  requireCustomer,
} from "@/lib/customer-auth";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export type CustomerAuthState = {
  error: string;
};

function text(formData: FormData, key: string) {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function passwordValue(formData: FormData, key: string) {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : "";
}

function validEmail(email: string) {
  return (
    email.length <= 254 &&
    /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)
  );
}

async function allowAttempt(
  email: string,
  purpose: "login" | "register",
) {
  const windowMs = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  const windowNumber = Math.floor(Date.now() / windowMs);

  const id = createHash("sha256")
    .update(`customer:${purpose}:${email}:${windowNumber}`)
    .digest("hex");

  const limit = await db.customerAuthLimit.upsert({
    where: { id },
    create: {
      id,
      attempts: 1,
      expiresAt: new Date((windowNumber + 1) * windowMs),
    },
    update: {
      attempts: { increment: 1 },
    },
  });

  return limit.attempts <= (purpose === "login" ? 8 : 5);
}

export async function registerCustomer(
  _previousState: CustomerAuthState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CustomerAuthState> {
  const name = text(formData, "name");
  const email = text(formData, "email").toLowerCase();
  const password = passwordValue(formData, "password");
  const confirmation = passwordValue(
    formData,
    "confirmPassword",
  );

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 100) {
    return { error: "Enter a name containing 2 to 100 characters." };
  }

  if (!validEmail(email)) {
    return { error: "Enter a valid email address." };
  }

  if (password.length < 12 || password.length > 128) {
    return { error: "Password must contain 12 to 128 characters." };
  }

  if (password !== confirmation) {
    return { error: "Passwords do not match." };
  }

  try {
    if (!(await allowAttempt(email, "register"))) {
      return {
        error: "Too many attempts. Try again after 15 minutes.",
      };
    }

    const existing = await db.customer.findUnique({
      where: { email },
      select: { id: true },
    });

    if (existing) {
      return {
        error: "Registration is unavailable for this email. Try signing in.",
      };
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

    const customer = await db.customer.create({
      data: {
        name,
        email,
        passwordHash,
      },
      select: { id: true },
    });

    await createCustomerSession(customer.id);
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Could not finish registration. If you already registered, try signing in.",
    };
  }

  redirect("/account");
}

const DUMMY_HASH = [
  "scrypt",
  "131072",
  "8",
  "1",
  "0".repeat(32),
  "0".repeat(128),
].join("$");

export async function loginCustomer(
  _previousState: CustomerAuthState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CustomerAuthState> {
  const email = text(formData, "email").toLowerCase();
  const password = passwordValue(formData, "password");

  if (
    !validEmail(email) ||
    password.length < 1 ||
    password.length > 128
  ) {
    return { error: "Email or password is incorrect." };
  }

  try {
    if (!(await allowAttempt(email, "login"))) {
      return {
        error: "Too many attempts. Try again after 15 minutes.",
      };
    }

    const customer = await db.customer.findUnique({
      where: { email },
      select: {
        id: true,
        passwordHash: true,
        isActive: true,
      },
    });

    const valid = await verifyPassword(
      password,
      customer?.passwordHash ?? DUMMY_HASH,
    );

    if (!customer || !customer.isActive || !valid) {
      return { error: "Email or password is incorrect." };
    }

    await createCustomerSession(customer.id);

    await db.customerAuthLimit.deleteMany({
      where: {
        expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
      },
    });
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Sign in is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
    };
  }

  redirect("/account");
}

export async function logoutCustomer(): Promise<void> {
  await deleteCustomerSession();
  redirect("/account/login");
}

export type CustomerProfileState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

export async function updateCustomerProfile(
  _previousState: CustomerProfileState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CustomerProfileState> {
  const customer = await requireCustomer();
  const name = text(formData, "name");

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 100) {
    return {
      error: "Name must contain 2 to 100 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    const result = await db.customer.updateMany({
      where: {
        id: customer.id,
        isActive: true,
      },
      data: { name },
    });

    if (result.count !== 1) {
      return {
        error: "Your account is unavailable. Please sign in again.",
        success: "",
      };
    }
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Could not save your profile. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/account");
  revalidatePath("/account/profile");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Your profile has been updated.",
  };
}

export async function changeCustomerPassword(
  _previousState: CustomerAuthState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CustomerAuthState> {
  const customer = await requireCustomer();

  const currentPassword = passwordValue(
    formData,
    "currentPassword",
  );

  const newPassword = passwordValue(
    formData,
    "newPassword",
  );

  const confirmation = passwordValue(
    formData,
    "confirmPassword",
  );

  if (
    currentPassword.length < 1 ||
    currentPassword.length > 128
  ) {
    return { error: "Enter your current password." };
  }

  if (newPassword.length < 12 || newPassword.length > 128) {
    return {
      error: "New password must contain 12 to 128 characters.",
    };
  }

  if (newPassword !== confirmation) {
    return { error: "New passwords do not match." };
  }

  if (newPassword === currentPassword) {
    return {
      error: "Choose a password different from your current password.",
    };
  }

  try {
    const windowMs = 15 * 60 * 1000;
    const windowNumber = Math.floor(Date.now() / windowMs);

    const limitId = createHash("sha256")
      .update(`customer:password:${customer.id}:${windowNumber}`)
      .digest("hex");

    const limit = await db.customerAuthLimit.upsert({
      where: { id: limitId },
      create: {
        id: limitId,
        attempts: 1,
        expiresAt: new Date((windowNumber + 1) * windowMs),
      },
      update: {
        attempts: { increment: 1 },
      },
    });

    if (limit.attempts > 5) {
      return {
        error: "Too many attempts. Try again after 15 minutes.",
      };
    }

    const account = await db.customer.findUnique({
      where: { id: customer.id },
      select: {
        passwordHash: true,
        isActive: true,
      },
    });

    if (!account?.isActive) {
      return {
        error: "Your account is unavailable. Please sign in again.",
      };
    }

    const valid = await verifyPassword(
      currentPassword,
      account.passwordHash,
    );

    if (!valid) {
      return { error: "Your current password is incorrect." };
    }

    const newHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);

    await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const changed = await tx.customer.updateMany({
        where: {
          id: customer.id,
          isActive: true,
          passwordHash: account.passwordHash,
        },
        data: {
          passwordHash: newHash,
        },
      });

      if (changed.count !== 1) {
        throw new Error("Account changed during password update.");
      }

      await tx.customerSession.deleteMany({
        where: { customerId: customer.id },
      });
    });
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Could not change your password. Please sign in again or retry.",
    };
  }

  await deleteCustomerSession();
  redirect("/account/login");
}

===== \src\app\account\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { connection } from "next/server";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { requireCustomer } from "@/lib/customer-auth";
import { getStoreCategories } from "@/lib/catalog";
import { label } from "@/lib/order-rules";
import Storefront from "@/components/storefront";
import { logoutCustomer } from "./actions";

type Props = {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
};

async function AccountContent({ searchParams }: Props) {
  await connection();

  const customer = await requireCustomer();
  const params = await searchParams;

  const rawPage = Number(typeof params.page === "string" ? params.page : "1");

  const requestedPage =
    Number.isSafeInteger(rawPage) && rawPage > 0 ? rawPage : 1;

  // Never query orders by an email supplied by the browser.
  const where = { customerId: customer.id };

  const [categories, total] = await Promise.all([
    getStoreCategories(),
    db.order.count({ where }),
  ]);

  const pageSize = 10;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const page = Math.min(requestedPage, totalPages);

  const orders = await db.order.findMany({
    where,
    skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
    take: pageSize,
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    select: {
      id: true,
      orderNumber: true,
      status: true,
      paymentStatus: true,
      total: true,
      createdAt: true,
    },
  });

  return (
    <Storefront mode="account" categories={categories} products={[]}>
      <section className="shell customer-account">
        <header className="customer-account-heading">
          <div>
            <p className="eyebrow">Your personal space</p>
            <h1>Hello, {customer.name}.</h1>
            <p>{customer.email}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="customer-account-actions">
            <Link href="/account/profile" className="product-small-button">
              Account settings
            </Link>

            <form action={logoutCustomer}>
              <button type="submit" className="product-small-button">
                Sign out
              </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="customer-orders">
          <div className="section-heading">
            <h2>Your orders</h2>
            <span className="category-help">{total} total</span>
          </div>

          {orders.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="store-empty">
              <p>No orders yet.</p>
              <p>Orders placed while signed in will appear here.</p>
              <Link href="/shop" className="primary-button">
                Explore the collection
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="customer-order-list">
              {orders.map((order) => (
                <article className="customer-order-card" key={order.id}>
                  <div>
                    <Link
                      href={`/account/orders/${order.id}`}
                      className="customer-order-link"
                    >
                      {order.orderNumber}
                    </Link>
                    <p>
                      {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
                        dateStyle: "medium",
                        timeZone: "Asia/Dhaka",
                      }).format(order.createdAt)}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <span className="category-status">
                      {label(order.status)}
                    </span>
                    <p>Payment: {label(order.paymentStatus)}</p>
                  </div>

                  <strong>৳ {order.total.toFixed(2)}</strong>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <nav className="store-pagination" aria-label="Your order pages">
              {page > 1 && (
                <Link href={`/account?page=${page - 1}`}>Previous</Link>
              )}

              <span>
                Page {page} of {totalPages}
              </span>

              {page < totalPages && (
                <Link href={`/account?page=${page + 1}`}>Next</Link>
              )}
            </nav>
          )}
        </section>
      </section>
    </Storefront>
  );
}

export default function CustomerAccountPage(props: Props) {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={<div className="store-loading">Opening your account...</div>}
    >
      <AccountContent {...props} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

===== \src\app\actions\cart.ts =====
"use server";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

type CartInput = {
  variantId: string;
  quantity: number;
};

export type CartLine = {
  variantId: string;
  quantity: number;
  name: string;
  slug: string | null;
  image: string | null;
  imageAlt: string;
  option: string;
  stock: number;
  unitPrice: string | null;
  lineTotal: string | null;
  issue: string;
};

export type CartQuoteResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      lines: CartLine[];
      subtotal: string;
      hasIssues: boolean;
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      error: string;
    };

const visibleProduct: Prisma.ProductWhereInput = {
  status: "ACTIVE",
  images: { some: {} },
  category: {
    is: {
      isActive: true,
      OR: [
        { parentId: null },
        { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
      ],
    },
  },
};

function parseCartInput(input: unknown): CartInput[] | null {
  if (!Array.isArray(input) || input.length > 50) {
    return null;
  }

  const items: CartInput[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();

  for (const value of input) {
    if (
      !value ||
      typeof value !== "object" ||
      typeof value.variantId !== "string" ||
      value.variantId.length < 1 ||
      value.variantId.length > 100 ||
      !Number.isInteger(value.quantity) ||
      value.quantity < 1 ||
      value.quantity > 99 ||
      seen.has(value.variantId)
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    seen.add(value.variantId);

    items.push({
      variantId: value.variantId,
      quantity: value.quantity,
    });
  }

  return items;
}

export async function validateCartItem(
  variantId: string,
  quantity: number,
): Promise<
  | { ok: true; variantId: string; quantity: number }
  | { ok: false; error: string }
> {
  if (
    typeof variantId !== "string" ||
    variantId.length < 1 ||
    variantId.length > 100 ||
    !Number.isInteger(quantity) ||
    quantity < 1 ||
    quantity > 99
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Choose a quantity between 1 and 99.",
    };
  }

  try {
    const variant = await db.productVariant.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: variantId,
        isActive: true,
        product: { is: visibleProduct },
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        stock: true,
      },
    });

    if (!variant || variant.stock < 1) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "This item is currently unavailable.",
      };
    }

    if (quantity > variant.stock) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: `Only ${variant.stock} units are available.`,
      };
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      variantId: variant.id,
      quantity,
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Could not check availability. Please try again.",
    };
  }
}

export async function quoteCart(
  input: unknown,
): Promise<CartQuoteResult> {
  const items = parseCartInput(input);

  if (!items) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Your bag contains invalid data.",
    };
  }

  if (items.length === 0) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      lines: [],
      subtotal: "0.00",
      hasIssues: false,
    };
  }

  try {
    const variants = await db.productVariant.findMany({
      where: {
        id: {
          in: items.map((item) => item.variantId),
        },
        isActive: true,
        product: { is: visibleProduct },
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        size: true,
        color: true,
        price: true,
        stock: true,
        product: {
          select: {
            name: true,
            slug: true,
            images: {
              orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
              take: 1,
              select: {
                url: true,
                alt: true,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      },
    });

    const byId = new Map(
      variants.map((variant) => [variant.id, variant]),
    );

    let subtotal = new Prisma.Decimal(0);

    const lines: CartLine[] = items.map((item) => {
      const variant = byId.get(item.variantId);

      if (!variant) {
        return {
          ...item,
          name: "Unavailable item",
          slug: null,
          image: null,
          imageAlt: "",
          option: "",
          stock: 0,
          unitPrice: null,
          lineTotal: null,
          issue: "This item is no longer available. Please remove it.",
        };
      }

      const issue =
        variant.stock === 0
          ? "Currently out of stock."
          : item.quantity > variant.stock
            ? `Only ${variant.stock} units are available. Reduce the quantity.`
            : "";

      const lineTotal = issue
        ? null
        : variant.price.mul(item.quantity);

      if (lineTotal) {
        subtotal = subtotal.add(lineTotal);
      }

      return {
        ...item,
        name: variant.product.name,
        slug: variant.product.slug,
        image: variant.product.images[0]?.url ?? null,
        imageAlt:
          variant.product.images[0]?.alt ?? variant.product.name,
        option:
          [variant.size, variant.color]
            .filter(Boolean)
            .join(" / ") || "Standard",
        stock: variant.stock,
        unitPrice: variant.price.toFixed(2),
        lineTotal: lineTotal?.toFixed(2) ?? null,
        issue,
      };
    });

    return {
      ok: true,
      lines,
      subtotal: subtotal.toFixed(2),
      hasIssues: lines.some((line) => Boolean(line.issue)),
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Could not load your bag. Please try again.",
    };
  }
}


===== \src\app\actions\checkout.ts =====
"use server";

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { getCustomer } from "@/lib/customer-auth";
import {
  calculateCouponDiscount,
  CouponValidationError,
  normalizeCouponCode,
} from "@/lib/coupons";

class CheckoutError extends Error {}

type CheckoutItem = {
  variantId: string;
  quantity: number;
};

type CheckoutResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      orderId: string;
      items: CheckoutItem[];
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      error: string;
    };

function hash(value: string) {
  return createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
}

function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function parseItems(value: unknown): CheckoutItem[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length < 1 || value.length > 50) {
    throw new CheckoutError("Your bag is empty or invalid.");
  }

  const seen = new Set<string>();

  return value.map((item) => {
    if (
      !item ||
      typeof item.variantId !== "string" ||
      item.variantId.length < 1 ||
      item.variantId.length > 100 ||
      !Number.isInteger(item.quantity) ||
      item.quantity < 1 ||
      item.quantity > 99 ||
      seen.has(item.variantId)
    ) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Your bag contains invalid items.");
    }

    seen.add(item.variantId);

    return {
      variantId: item.variantId,
      quantity: item.quantity,
    };
  });
}

function normalizePhone(value: string) {
  const digits = value.replace(/[০-৯]/g, (digit) =>
    String("০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯".indexOf(digit)),
  );

  return digits.replace(/[\s()-]/g, "").replace(/^\+?88/, "");
}

async function grantReceiptAccess(orderId: string, secret: string) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  cookieStore.set("bimlay_order_receipt", `${orderId}:${secret}`, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    path: "/order-confirmation",
    maxAge: 48 * 60 * 60,
  });
}

export async function submitCheckout(input: unknown): Promise<CheckoutResult> {
  try {
    if (!input || typeof input !== "object") {
      throw new CheckoutError("Invalid checkout request.");
    }

    const data = input as Record<string, unknown>;

    const checkoutKey = stringValue(data.checkoutKey);
    const receiptSecret = stringValue(data.receiptSecret);

    if (
      !/^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-4[a-f0-9]{3}-[89ab][a-f0-9]{3}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/.test(
        checkoutKey,
      ) ||
      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(receiptSecret)
    ) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Invalid checkout session.");
    }

    const receiptTokenHash = hash(receiptSecret);

    // A retry must return the original order.
    const previous = await db.order.findUnique({
      where: { checkoutKey },
      include: { items: true },
    });

    if (previous) {
      if (previous.receiptTokenHash !== receiptTokenHash) {
        throw new CheckoutError("Invalid checkout session.");
      }

      await grantReceiptAccess(previous.id, receiptSecret);

      return {
        ok: true,
        orderId: previous.id,
        items: previous.items.flatMap((item) =>
          item.variantId
            ? [
                {
                  variantId: item.variantId,
                  quantity: item.quantity,
                },
              ]
            : [],
        ),
      };
    }

    const customerName = stringValue(data.customerName);
    const phone = normalizePhone(stringValue(data.phone));
    const email = stringValue(data.email).toLowerCase();
    const district = stringValue(data.district);
    const address = stringValue(data.address);
    const notes = stringValue(data.notes);
    const deliveryZone = stringValue(data.deliveryZone);
    const expectedTotal = stringValue(data.expectedTotal);
    const couponInput = stringValue(data.couponCode);
    const couponCode = couponInput ? normalizeCouponCode(couponInput) : null;
    const items = parseItems(data.items);

    if (customerName.length < 2 || customerName.length > 100) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Enter your full name.");
    }

    if (!/^01\d{9}$/.test(phone)) {
      throw new CheckoutError(
        "Enter a valid 11-digit Bangladesh mobile number.",
      );
    }

    if (
      email &&
      (email.length > 254 || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email))
    ) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Enter a valid email or leave it empty.");
    }

    if (district.length < 2 || district.length > 80) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Enter your district.");
    }

    if (address.length < 10 || address.length > 500) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Enter your complete delivery address.");
    }

    if (notes.length > 500) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Order notes must not exceed 500 characters.");
    }

    if (deliveryZone !== "DHAKA" && deliveryZone !== "OUTSIDE_DHAKA") {
      throw new CheckoutError("Choose a delivery area.");
    }

    if (!/^\d{1,14}\.\d{2}$/.test(expectedTotal)) {
      throw new CheckoutError("Refresh checkout and review your total.");
    }

    // Basic phone-based checkout attempt limit.
    const hour = Math.floor(Date.now() / 3600000);

    const limit = await db.checkoutLimit.upsert({
      where: {
        id: hash(`${phone}:${hour}`),
      },
      create: {
        id: hash(`${phone}:${hour}`),
        attempts: 1,
        expiresAt: new Date((hour + 1) * 3600000),
      },
      update: {
        attempts: { increment: 1 },
      },
    });

    if (limit.attempts > 8) {
      throw new CheckoutError(
        "Too many checkout attempts. Please try again later.",
      );
    }

    const customer = await getCustomer();

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
      try {
        const order = await db.$transaction(
          async (tx) => {
            const existing = await tx.order.findUnique({
              where: { checkoutKey },
              include: { items: true },
            });

            if (existing) {
              if (existing.receiptTokenHash !== receiptTokenHash) {
                throw new CheckoutError("Invalid checkout session.");
              }

              return existing;
            }

            const settings = await tx.storeSetting.findUnique({
              where: { id: "main" },
            });

            if (!settings?.codEnabled) {
              throw new CheckoutError("Orders are temporarily unavailable.");
            }

            const variants = await tx.productVariant.findMany({
              where: {
                id: {
                  in: items.map((item) => item.variantId),
                },
                isActive: true,
                product: {
                  is: {
                    status: "ACTIVE",
                    images: { some: {} },
                    category: {
                      is: {
                        isActive: true,
                        OR: [
                          { parentId: null },
                          {
                            parent: {
                              is: { isActive: true },
                            },
                          },
                        ],
                      },
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
              select: {
                id: true,
                sku: true,
                size: true,
                color: true,
                price: true,
                stock: true,
                productId: true,
                product: {
                  select: {
                    name: true,
                    images: {
                      orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
                      take: 1,
                      select: { url: true },
                    },
                  },
                },
              },
            });

            const byId = new Map(
              variants.map((variant) => [variant.id, variant]),
            );

            let subtotal = new Prisma.Decimal(0);

            const orderItems = items.map((item) => {
              const variant = byId.get(item.variantId);

              if (!variant || variant.stock < item.quantity) {
                throw new CheckoutError(
                  "An item is unavailable or its stock has changed. Review your bag.",
                );
              }

              const lineTotal = variant.price.mul(item.quantity);
              subtotal = subtotal.add(lineTotal);

              return {
                productId: variant.productId,
                variantId: variant.id,
                productName: variant.product.name,
                sku: variant.sku,
                size: variant.size,
                color: variant.color,
                imageUrl: variant.product.images[0]?.url ?? null,
                quantity: item.quantity,
                unitPrice: variant.price,
                lineTotal,
              };
            });

            const deliveryFee =
              deliveryZone === "DHAKA"
                ? settings.dhakaDeliveryCharge
                : settings.outsideDeliveryCharge;

            const coupon = couponCode
              ? await tx.coupon.findUnique({
                  where: { code: couponCode },
                })
              : null;

            if (couponCode && !coupon) {
              throw new CheckoutError("Coupon code is invalid.");
            }

            const discount = coupon
              ? calculateCouponDiscount(coupon, subtotal, new Date())
              : new Prisma.Decimal(0);

            const total = subtotal.sub(discount).add(deliveryFee);

            if (!total.eq(new Prisma.Decimal(expectedTotal))) {
              throw new CheckoutError(
                "Pricing or delivery charges changed. Refresh checkout and review the updated total before ordering.",
              );
            }

            if (coupon) {
              const claimed = await tx.coupon.updateMany({
                where: {
                  id: coupon.id,
                  isActive: true,
                  ...(coupon.usageLimit !== null
                    ? {
                        usedCount: {
                          lt: coupon.usageLimit,
                        },
                      }
                    : {}),
                },
                data: {
                  usedCount: { increment: 1 },
                },
              });

              if (claimed.count !== 1) {
                throw new CheckoutError(
                  "This coupon is no longer available. Review your total.",
                );
              }
            }

            for (const item of items) {
              const changed = await tx.productVariant.updateMany({
                where: {
                  id: item.variantId,
                  isActive: true,
                  stock: { gte: item.quantity },
                },
                data: {
                  stock: { decrement: item.quantity },
                },
              });

              if (changed.count !== 1) {
                throw new CheckoutError(
                  "Stock changed while placing your order. Review your bag.",
                );
              }
            }

            return tx.order.create({
              data: {
                orderNumber: `BL-${randomBytes(6).toString("hex").toUpperCase()}`,
                checkoutKey,
                receiptTokenHash,
                customerId: customer?.id ?? null,
                customerName,
                phone,
                email: email || null,
                district,
                address,
                notes: notes || null,
                deliveryZone,
                subtotal,
                deliveryFee,
                discount,
                couponCode,
                couponId: coupon?.id ?? null,
                total,
                items: {
                  create: orderItems,
                },
              },
              include: { items: true },
            });
          },
          {
            isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
            maxWait: 5000,
            timeout: 15000,
          },
        );

        await grantReceiptAccess(order.id, receiptSecret);

        revalidatePath("/admin");
        revalidatePath("/admin/products");
        revalidatePath("/shop");
        revalidatePath("/");

        return {
          ok: true,
          orderId: order.id,
          items: order.items.flatMap((item) =>
            item.variantId
              ? [
                  {
                    variantId: item.variantId,
                    quantity: item.quantity,
                  },
                ]
              : [],
          ),
        };
      } catch (error) {
        const retryable =
          error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
          (error.code === "P2034" || error.code === "P2002");

        if (retryable && attempt < 2) {
          continue;
        }

        throw error;
      }
    }

    throw new CheckoutError("Please try checkout again.");
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
       error instanceof CheckoutError ||
error instanceof CouponValidationError
  ? error.message
  : "Could not complete checkout. Please retry from this page."
    };
  }
}



===== \src\app\actions\coupons.ts =====
"use server";

import { quoteCart } from "@/app/actions/cart";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import {
  calculateCouponDiscount,
  CouponValidationError,
  normalizeCouponCode,
} from "@/lib/coupons";

export type CouponQuoteResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      code: string;
      subtotal: string;
      discount: string;
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      error: string;
    };

export async function quoteCheckoutCoupon(
  items: unknown,
  inputCode: unknown,
): Promise<CouponQuoteResult> {
  try {
    const code = normalizeCouponCode(inputCode);
    const quote = await quoteCart(items);

    if (!quote.ok) {
      return { ok: false, error: quote.error };
    }

    if (quote.lines.length === 0 || quote.hasIssues) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "Review your bag before applying a coupon.",
      };
    }

    const coupon = await db.coupon.findUnique({
      where: { code },
    });

    if (!coupon) {
      return {
        ok: false,
        error: "Coupon code is invalid.",
      };
    }

    const subtotal = new Prisma.Decimal(quote.subtotal);

    const discount = calculateCouponDiscount(
      coupon,
      subtotal,
      new Date(),
    );

    return {
      ok: true,
      code,
      subtotal: quote.subtotal,
      discount: discount.toFixed(2),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error:
        error instanceof CouponValidationError
          ? error.message
          : "Could not check this coupon. Please try again.",
    };
  }
}

"use server";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";

export async function addProductReview(formData: FormData) {
  const productId = formData.get("productId") as string;
  const userName = formData.get("userName") as string;
  const rating = Number(formData.get("rating"));
  const comment = formData.get("comment") as string;
  const imageUrl = formData.get("imageUrl") as string;

  if (!productId || !userName || !rating) {
    throw new Error("Porojoniyo field gulo puron korun.");
  }

  await db.review.create({
    data: {
      productId,
      userName,
      rating,
      comment,
      imageUrl: imageUrl || null,
    },
  });

  revalidatePath(`/products/${productId}`);
}


===== \src\app\actions\wishlist.ts =====
"use server";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { StoreProduct } from "@/lib/catalog";

export type WishlistResult =
  | {
      ok: true;
      products: StoreProduct[];
      unavailableIds: string[];
    }
  | {
      ok: false;
      error: string;
    };

export async function getWishlistProducts(
  input: unknown,
): Promise<WishlistResult> {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(input) ||
    input.length > 50 ||
    input.some(
      (id) =>
        typeof id !== "string" ||
        id.length < 1 ||
        id.length > 100,
    )
  ) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Your wishlist contains invalid data.",
    };
  }

  const ids = [...new Set(input as string[])];

  if (ids.length === 0) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      products: [],
      unavailableIds: [],
    };
  }

  try {
    const records = await db.product.findMany({
      where: {
        id: { in: ids },
        status: "ACTIVE",
        images: { some: {} },
        variants: {
          some: { isActive: true },
        },
        category: {
          is: {
            isActive: true,
            OR: [
              { parentId: null },
              { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
            ],
          },
        },
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        name: true,
        slug: true,
        shortDescription: true,
        images: {
          orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
          take: 1,
          select: {
            url: true,
            alt: true,
          },
        },
        variants: {
          where: { isActive: true },
          orderBy: { price: "asc" },
          select: {
            price: true,
            compareAtPrice: true,
            stock: true,
          },
        },
      },
    });

    const byId = new Map(
      records.map((product) => [product.id, product]),
    );

    const products: StoreProduct[] = [];
    const unavailableIds: string[] = [];

    for (const id of ids) {
      const product = byId.get(id);

      if (!product) {
        unavailableIds.push(id);
        continue;
      }

      const variant =
        product.variants.find((item) => item.stock > 0) ??
        product.variants[0];

      products.push({
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        slug: product.slug,
        shortDescription: product.shortDescription ?? "",
        image: product.images[0].url,
        imageAlt: product.images[0].alt,
        price: variant.price.toFixed(2),
        regularPrice:
          variant.compareAtPrice?.toFixed(2) ?? null,
        inStock: product.variants.some(
          (item) => item.stock > 0,
        ),
        hasMultipleVariants: product.variants.length > 1,
      });
    }

    return {
      ok: true,
      products,
      unavailableIds,
    };
  } catch {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: "Could not load your wishlist. Please try again.",
    };
  }
}


===== \src\app\admin\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { verifyPassword } from "@/lib/password";
import {
  createAdminSession,
  deleteAdminSession,
} from "@/lib/admin-auth";

export type LoginState = {
  error: string;
};

const WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 8;

// Nonexistent accounts still perform a password hash check.
const DUMMY_HASH = [
  "scrypt",
  "131072",
  "8",
  "1",
  "0".repeat(32),
  "0".repeat(128),
].join("$");

export async function loginAdmin(
  _previousState: LoginState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<LoginState> {
  const emailValue = formData.get("email");
  const passwordValue = formData.get("password");

  if (
    typeof emailValue !== "string" ||
    typeof passwordValue !== "string"
  ) {
    return { error: "Enter your email and password." };
  }

  const email = emailValue.trim().toLowerCase();
  const password = passwordValue;

  if (
    email.length > 254 ||
    !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ||
    password.length < 1 ||
    password.length > 128
  ) {
    return { error: "Email or password is incorrect." };
  }

  try {
    const windowNumber = Math.floor(Date.now() / WINDOW_MS);

    const limitId = createHash("sha256")
      .update(`${email}:${windowNumber}`)
      .digest("hex");

    const limit = await db.adminLoginLimit.upsert({
      where: { id: limitId },
      create: {
        id: limitId,
        attempts: 1,
        expiresAt: new Date((windowNumber + 1) * WINDOW_MS),
      },
      update: {
        attempts: { increment: 1 },
      },
    });

    if (limit.attempts > MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
      return {
        error: "Too many login attempts. Try again after 15 minutes.",
      };
    }

    const admin = await db.adminUser.findUnique({
      where: { email },
      select: {
        id: true,
        passwordHash: true,
        isActive: true,
      },
    });

    const validPassword = await verifyPassword(
      password,
      admin?.passwordHash ?? DUMMY_HASH,
    );

    if (!admin || !admin.isActive || !validPassword) {
      return { error: "Email or password is incorrect." };
    }

    await db.adminLoginLimit.deleteMany({
      where: {
        expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
      },
    });

    await createAdminSession(admin.id);
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Login is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",
    };
  }

  // redirect must stay outside the try/catch.
  redirect("/admin");
}

export async function logoutAdmin(): Promise<void> {
  await deleteAdminSession();
  redirect("/admin/login");
}

  display: grid;
  gap: 20px;
