===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\categories\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN,
  mediaImageExists,
} from "@/lib/media-storage";

export type CategoryFormState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

function readText(formData: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

export async function saveCategory(
  _previousState: CategoryFormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CategoryFormState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the store owner can manage categories.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const id = readText(formData, "id");
  const name = readText(formData, "name");
  const slug = readText(formData, "slug").toLowerCase();
  const description = readText(formData, "description");
  const imageUrl = readText(formData, "imageUrl");
  const parentId = readText(formData, "parentId") || null;
  const orderText = readText(formData, "sortOrder");
  const sortOrder = Number(orderText);
  const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 80) {
    return {
      error: "Category name must contain 2 to 80 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    slug.length < 2 ||
    slug.length > 100 ||
    !/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/.test(slug)
  ) {
    return {
      error:
        "Use an English slug with lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens. Example: mens-shirts",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (description.length > 1000) {
    return {
      error: "Description must not exceed 1000 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    orderText === "" ||
    !Number.isInteger(sortOrder) ||
    sortOrder < 0 ||
    sortOrder > 9999
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Display order must be a whole number from 0 to 9999.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (id.length > 100 || (parentId && parentId.length > 100)) {
    return {
      error: "Invalid category.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    if (imageUrl) {
  const filename = imageUrl.startsWith("/media/")
    ? imageUrl.slice("/media/".length)
    : "";

  if (
    !MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename) ||
    !(await mediaImageExists(imageUrl))
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Choose an uploaded category image.",
      success: "",
    };
  }
}
    const validationError = await db.$transaction(
      async (tx): Promise<string | null> => {
        if (id) {
          const existing = await tx.category.findUnique({
            where: { id },
            select: {
              id: true,
              _count: {
                select: { children: true },
              },
            },
          });

          if (!existing) {
            return "Category no longer exists. Refresh this page.";
          }

          if (parentId === id) {
            return "A category cannot be its own parent.";
          }

          if (parentId && existing._count.children > 0) {
            return "A category with subcategories must remain a main category.";
          }
        }

        if (parentId) {
          const parent = await tx.category.findUnique({
            where: { id: parentId },
            select: {
              id: true,
              parentId: true,
            },
          });

          if (!parent || parent.parentId !== null) {
            return "Choose an existing main category as the parent.";
          }
        }

        const data = {
          name,
          slug,
          description: description || null,
          imageUrl: imageUrl || null,
          parentId,
          sortOrder,
          isActive,
        };

        if (id) {
          await tx.category.update({
            where: { id },
            data,
          });
        } else {
          await tx.category.create({ data });
        }

        return null;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      },
    );

    if (validationError) {
      return { error: validationError, success: "" };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError) {
      if (error.code === "P2002") {
        return {
          error: "This slug is already used. Choose another slug.",
          success: "",
        };
      }

      if (error.code === "P2034") {
        return {
          error: "Another update happened at the same time. Please save again.",
          success: "",
        };
      }
    }

    return {
      error: "Could not save the category. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/categories");
  revalidatePath("/admin");
  revalidatePath("/");
revalidatePath("/shop");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: id ? "Category updated." : "Category created.",
  };
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\categories\category-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useActionState, useState } from "react";
import ImageUpload from "@/components/admin/image-upload";
import { saveCategory } from "./actions";

type CategoryData = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  description: string | null;
  imageUrl: string | null;
  parentId: string | null;
  sortOrder: number;
  isActive: boolean;
};

type ParentOption = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

type Props = {
  category?: CategoryData;
  parents: ParentOption[];
};

export default function CategoryForm({ category, parents }: Props) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(saveCategory, {
    error: "",
    success: "",
  });

  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState(category?.imageUrl ?? "");

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="category-form">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={category?.id ?? ""} />

      <fieldset disabled={pending}>
        <input type="hidden" name="imageUrl" value={imageUrl} />
        <div className="category-form-grid">
          <label>
            <span>Category name</span>
            <input
              name="name"
              defaultValue={category?.name ?? ""}
              placeholder="Example: Men"
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={80}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>URL slug</span>
            <input
              name="slug"
              defaultValue={category?.slug ?? ""}
              placeholder="Example: men"
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={100}
              pattern="[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*"
              autoCapitalize="none"
              spellCheck={false}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Parent category</span>
            <select name="parentId" defaultValue={category?.parentId ?? ""}>
              <option value="">None — main category</option>

              {parents
                .filter((parent) => parent.id !== category?.id)
                .map((parent) => (
                  <option key={parent.id} value={parent.id}>
                    {parent.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
            </select>
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Display order</span>
            <input
              name="sortOrder"
              type="number"
              min={0}
              max={9999}
              step={1}
              defaultValue={category?.sortOrder ?? 0}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="category-full-width">
            <span>Description</span>
            <textarea
              name="description"
              defaultValue={category?.description ?? ""}
              placeholder="A short introduction to this collection"
              maxLength={1000}
              rows={3}
            />
          </label>
          <div className="category-full-width category-image-field">
            <div>
              <strong>Category card image</strong>
              <p className="category-help">
                Upload an image for this category. Main categories appear as
                cards on the homepage.
              </p>
            </div>

            <ImageUpload onUploaded={(image) => setImageUrl(image.url)} />

            {imageUrl && (
              <div className="category-image-selection">
                <Image
                  src={imageUrl}
                  alt={`${category?.name ?? "Category"} card preview`}
                  width={480}
                  height={360}
                  unoptimized
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="category-remove-image"
                  onClick={() => setImageUrl("")}
                >
                  Remove selected image
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <label className="category-checkbox">
          <input
            name="isActive"
            type="checkbox"
            defaultChecked={category?.isActive ?? true}
          />
          <span>Active category</span>
        </label>

        <p className="category-help">
          Lower display order appears first. Slugs must be unique.
        </p>

        <button type="submit" className="primary-button" disabled={pending}>
          {pending
            ? "Saving..."
            : category
              ? "Save changes"
              : "Create category"}
        </button>
      </fieldset>

      <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
        {state.error && <p className="admin-form-error">{state.error}</p>}

        {state.success && <p className="category-success">{state.success}</p>}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}



===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\categories\page.tsx =====
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import CategoryForm from "./category-form";

export default async function CategoriesPage() {
  await connection();

  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return (
      <section className="admin-next-step">
        <h1>Owner access required</h1>
        <p>Only the store owner can manage categories.</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const categories = await db.category.findMany({
    orderBy: [
      { sortOrder: "asc" },
      { name: "asc" },
    ],
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      slug: true,
      description: true,
      imageUrl: true,
      parentId: true,
      sortOrder: true,
      isActive: true,
      parent: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
      _count: {
        select: {
          products: true,
          children: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });

  const parents = categories
    .filter((category) => category.parentId === null)
    .map((category) => ({
      id: category.id,
      name: category.name,
    }));

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Store organisation</p>
        <h1>Categories</h1>
        <p>
          Organise your collections and choose their display order.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="category-panel">
        <div className="category-panel-heading">
          <h2>Create a category</h2>
          <p>
            Leave the parent empty for a main category.
          </p>
        </div>

        <CategoryForm parents={parents} />
      </section>

      <section className="category-list">
        <div className="category-list-heading">
          <h2>Your categories</h2>
          <span>{categories.length} total</span>
        </div>

        {categories.length === 0 && (
          <div className="category-empty">
            No categories yet. Create your first collection above.
          </div>
        )}

        {categories.map((category) => (
          <article
            className="category-panel"
            key={category.id}
          >
            <div className="category-summary">
              <div>
                <p className="category-parent">
                  {category.parent
                    ? `${category.parent.name} / Subcategory`
                    : "Main category"}
                </p>

                <h3>{category.name}</h3>
                <p className="category-slug">
                  {category.slug}
                </p>
              </div>

              <span
                className={`category-status ${
                  category.isActive ? "is-active" : ""
                }`}
              >
                {category.isActive ? "Active" : "Hidden"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="category-meta">
              <span>
                {category._count.products} products
              </span>
              <span>
                {category._count.children} subcategories
              </span>
              <span>Order: {category.sortOrder}</span>
            </div>

            <details className="category-edit">
              <summary>Edit category</summary>

              <CategoryForm
                category={{
                  id: category.id,
                  name: category.name,
                  slug: category.slug,
                  description: category.description,
                  imageUrl: category.imageUrl,
                  parentId: category.parentId,
                  sortOrder: category.sortOrder,
                  isActive: category.isActive,
                }}
                parents={parents}
              />
            </details>
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\coupons\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { normalizeCouponCode } from "@/lib/coupons";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export type CouponFormState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

function field(formData: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(name);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function amount(value: string): Prisma.Decimal | null {
  if (!/^\d{1,7}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    return null;
  }

  const result = new Prisma.Decimal(value);
  return result.lte(1000000) ? result : null;
}

// Date inputs are interpreted as Bangladesh calendar dates.
function bdDay(value: string, nextDay = false): Date | null {
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)) {
    return null;
  }

  const check = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00Z`);

  if (
    Number.isNaN(check.getTime()) ||
    check.toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== value
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00+06:00`);

  if (nextDay) {
    date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + 1);
  }

  return date;
}

export async function createCoupon(
  _previous: CouponFormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<CouponFormState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can manage coupons.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  let code: string;

  try {
    code = normalizeCouponCode(field(formData, "code"));
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Use 3–32 English letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores for the code.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const kind = field(formData, "kind");
  const value = amount(field(formData, "value"));
  const minimumSubtotal = amount(
    field(formData, "minimumSubtotal"),
  );
  const maximumText = field(formData, "maximumDiscount");
  const maximumDiscount = maximumText
    ? amount(maximumText)
    : null;
  const usageText = field(formData, "usageLimit");
  const usageLimit = usageText ? Number(usageText) : null;
  const startText = field(formData, "startsAt");
  const expiryText = field(formData, "expiresAt");
  const startsAt = startText ? bdDay(startText) : null;
  const expiresAt = expiryText
    ? bdDay(expiryText, true)
    : null;
  const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

  if (kind !== "PERCENT" && kind !== "FIXED") {
    return { error: "Choose a discount type.", success: "" };
  }

  if (
    !value ||
    value.lte(0) ||
    (kind === "PERCENT" && value.gt(100))
  ) {
    return {
      error:
        kind === "PERCENT"
          ? "Enter a percentage greater than 0 and up to 100."
          : "Enter a fixed discount greater than 0.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (!minimumSubtotal || minimumSubtotal.lt(0)) {
    return {
      error: "Enter a valid minimum subtotal. Use 0 for no minimum.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    maximumText &&
    (!maximumDiscount || maximumDiscount.lte(0))
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Enter a valid maximum discount or leave it empty.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    usageText &&
    (!/^\d{1,7}$/.test(usageText) ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(usageLimit) ||
      usageLimit! < 1 ||
      usageLimit! > 1000000)
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Usage limit must be a whole number from 1 to 1000000.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if ((startText && !startsAt) || (expiryText && !expiresAt)) {
    return {
      error: "Enter valid start and expiry dates.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    startsAt &&
    expiresAt &&
    startsAt.getTime() >= expiresAt.getTime()
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Expiry date must be on or after the start date.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    await db.coupon.create({
      data: {
        code,
        kind,
        value,
        minimumSubtotal,
        maximumDiscount,
        usageLimit,
        startsAt,
        expiresAt,
        isActive,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2002"
    ) {
      return {
        error: "This coupon code already exists.",
        success: "",
      };
    }

    return {
      error: "Could not create the coupon. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/coupons");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: `Coupon ${code} created.`,
  };
}

export async function toggleCoupon(
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<void> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") return;

  const id = field(formData, "id");
  if (!id || id.length > 100) return;

  const coupon = await db.coupon.findUnique({
    where: { id },
    select: { isActive: true },
  });

  if (!coupon) return;

  await db.coupon.updateMany({
    where: { id, isActive: coupon.isActive },
    data: { isActive: !coupon.isActive },
  });

  revalidatePath("/admin/coupons");
}

===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\coupons\page.tsx =====
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import CouponForm from "./coupon-form";
import { toggleCoupon } from "./actions";

function bdDate(value: Date | null) {
  if (!value) return "No date limit";

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
    timeZone: "Asia/Dhaka",
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(value);
}

export default async function CouponsPage() {
  await connection();

  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return (
      <section className="admin-next-step">
        <h1>Owner access required</h1>
        <p>Only the owner can manage coupons.</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const coupons = await db.coupon.findMany({
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
  });

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Promotions</p>
        <h1>Coupons</h1>
        <p>Create discount codes and control availability.</p>
      </section>

      <section className="category-panel">
        <div className="category-panel-heading">
          <h2>Create a coupon</h2>
          <p>Codes are case-insensitive at checkout.</p>
        </div>

        <CouponForm />
      </section>

      <section className="category-list">
        <div className="category-list-heading">
          <h2>Your coupons</h2>
          <span>{coupons.length} total</span>
        </div>

        {coupons.length === 0 && (
          <div className="category-empty">
            No coupons yet. Create one above.
          </div>
        )}

        {coupons.map((coupon) => (
          <article
            key={coupon.id}
            className="category-panel coupon-admin-card"
          >
            <div className="category-summary">
              <div>
                <p className="category-parent">
                  {coupon.kind === "PERCENT"
                    ? "Percentage discount"
                    : "Fixed discount"}
                </p>
                <h3>{coupon.code}</h3>
                <p className="category-slug">
                  {coupon.kind === "PERCENT"
                    ? `${coupon.value.toFixed(2)}% off`
                    : `৳${coupon.value.toFixed(2)} off`}
                </p>
              </div>

              <span
                className={`category-status ${
                  coupon.isActive ? "is-active" : ""
                }`}
              >
                {coupon.isActive ? "Active" : "Paused"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="coupon-admin-meta">
              <span>
                Minimum: ৳
                {coupon.minimumSubtotal.toFixed(2)}
              </span>
              <span>
                Maximum discount:{" "}
                {coupon.maximumDiscount
                  ? `৳${coupon.maximumDiscount.toFixed(2)}`
                  : "No cap"}
              </span>
              <span>
                Used: {coupon.usedCount}
                {coupon.usageLimit !== null
                  ? ` / ${coupon.usageLimit}`
                  : " / unlimited"}
              </span>
              <span>
                Starts: {bdDate(coupon.startsAt)}
              </span>
              <span>
                Last valid:{" "}
                {coupon.expiresAt
                  ? bdDate(
                      new Date(
                        coupon.expiresAt.getTime() - 1,
                      ),
                    )
                  : "No date limit"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <form action={toggleCoupon}>
              <input
                type="hidden"
                name="id"
                value={coupon.id}
              />
              <button
                type="submit"
                className="coupon-admin-toggle"
              >
                {coupon.isActive
                  ? "Pause coupon"
                  : "Activate coupon"}
              </button>
            </form>
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\hero\actions.ts =====
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN,
  mediaImageExists,
} from "@/lib/media-storage";

export type HeroFormState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

class HeroInputError extends Error {}

function text(formData: FormData, key: string) {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function validButtonHref(value: string): boolean {
  if (
    !value.startsWith("/") ||
    value.startsWith("//") ||
    /[\\\u0000-\u0020]/.test(value) ||
    value.length > 300
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(value, "https://bimlay.com");

    if (url.origin !== "https://bimlay.com") {
      return false;
    }

    return (
      url.pathname === "/" ||
      url.pathname === "/shop" ||
      /^\/products\/[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/.test(
        url.pathname,
      )
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

async function verifyImage(url: string) {
  if (!url.startsWith("/media/")) {
    throw new HeroInputError("Use an uploaded banner image.");
  }

  const filename = url.slice("/media/".length);

  if (!MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename)) {
    throw new HeroInputError("Invalid banner image.");
  }

  try {
  const exists = await mediaImageExists(url);

  if (!exists) {
    throw new Error("Image not found.");
  }
} catch {
  throw new HeroInputError(
    "A banner image is missing. Upload it again.",
  );
}
}

export async function saveHeroSlide(
  _previousState: HeroFormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<HeroFormState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can manage banners.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const id = text(formData, "id");
  const eyebrow = text(formData, "eyebrow");
  const title = text(formData, "title");
  const subtitle = text(formData, "subtitle");
  const desktopImage = text(formData, "desktopImage");
  const mobileImage = text(formData, "mobileImage");
  const altText = text(formData, "altText");
  const buttonText = text(formData, "buttonText");
  const buttonHref = text(formData, "buttonHref");
  const orderText = text(formData, "sortOrder");
  const sortOrder = Number(orderText);
  const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

  let savedId = "";

  try {
    if (id.length > 100) {
      throw new HeroInputError("Invalid slide.");
    }

    if (
      title.length < 2 ||
      title.length > 90 ||
      eyebrow.length > 50 ||
      subtitle.length > 240
    ) {
      throw new HeroInputError(
        "Title: 2–90 characters. Eyebrow: maximum 50. Description: maximum 240.",
      );
    }

    if (altText.length < 2 || altText.length > 180) {
      throw new HeroInputError(
        "Describe the banner image in 2 to 180 characters.",
      );
    }

    if (buttonText.length < 2 || buttonText.length > 40) {
      throw new HeroInputError(
        "Button text must contain 2 to 40 characters.",
      );
    }

    if (!validButtonHref(buttonHref)) {
      throw new HeroInputError(
        "Use /shop, /shop?category=men, / or a valid /products/product-slug link.",
      );
    }

    if (
      orderText === "" ||
      !Number.isInteger(sortOrder) ||
      sortOrder < 0 ||
      sortOrder > 9999
    ) {
      throw new HeroInputError(
        "Display order must be a whole number from 0 to 9999.",
      );
    }

    await verifyImage(desktopImage);

    if (mobileImage) {
      await verifyImage(mobileImage);
    }

    savedId = await db.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        if (id) {
          const existing = await tx.heroSlide.findUnique({
            where: { id },
            select: { id: true },
          });

          if (!existing) {
            throw new HeroInputError("Slide not found.");
          }
        }

        if (isActive) {
          const activeCount = await tx.heroSlide.count({
            where: {
              isActive: true,
              ...(id ? { id: { not: id } } : {}),
            },
          });

          if (activeCount >= 8) {
            throw new HeroInputError(
              "Keep up to 8 active slides. Hide another slide first.",
            );
          }
        }

        const data = {
          eyebrow: eyebrow || null,
          title,
          subtitle: subtitle || null,
          desktopImage,
          mobileImage: mobileImage || null,
          altText,
          buttonText,
          buttonHref,
          sortOrder,
          isActive,
        };

        const slide = id
          ? await tx.heroSlide.update({
              where: { id },
              data,
              select: { id: true },
            })
          : await tx.heroSlide.create({
              data,
              select: { id: true },
            });

        return slide.id;
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof HeroInputError) {
      return { error: error.message, success: "" };
    }

    return {
      error: "Could not save the slide. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/hero");

  if (!id) {
    redirect(`/admin/hero?edit=${savedId}`);
  }

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Slide updated successfully.",
  };
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\hero\page.tsx =====
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { getHeroSlides } from "@/lib/hero-slides";
import SlideForm from "./slide-form";

export default async function AdminHeroPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<
    Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>
  >;
}) {
  await connection();

  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return (
      <section className="admin-next-step">
        <h1>Owner access required</h1>
        <p>Only the owner can manage homepage slides.</p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const params = await searchParams;
  const editId = typeof params.edit === "string"
    ? params.edit
    : "";

  const slides = await getHeroSlides(false);

  const selected = editId
    ? slides.find((slide) => slide.id === editId)
    : undefined;

  if (editId && !selected) {
    notFound();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Homepage campaigns</p>
        <h1>Hero slider</h1>
        <p>Create, arrange and update homepage banners.</p>
      </section>

      {selected && (
        <Link
          href="/admin/hero"
          className="product-small-button product-new-link"
        >
          Add another slide
        </Link>
      )}

      <section className="category-panel">
        <h2 className="product-section-title">
          {selected ? "Edit slide" : "Create slide"}
        </h2>

        <SlideForm
          key={selected?.id ?? "new"}
          slide={selected}
        />
      </section>

      <section className="category-list">
        <div className="category-list-heading">
          <h2>Your slides</h2>
          <span>{slides.length} total</span>
        </div>

        {slides.length === 0 && (
          <div className="category-empty">
            No slides yet. Create your first banner above.
          </div>
        )}

        {slides.map((slide) => (
          <article className="hero-admin-row" key={slide.id}>
            <Image
              src={slide.desktopImage}
              alt={slide.altText}
              width={180}
              height={90}
              unoptimized
            />

            <div>
              <h3>{slide.title}</h3>
              <p className="category-help">
                Order: {slide.sortOrder}
                {" · "}
                {slide.isActive ? "Active" : "Hidden"}
              </p>
            </div>

            <Link
              href={`/admin/hero?edit=${slide.id}`}
              className="product-small-button"
            >
              Edit slide
            </Link>
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>
    </>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\hero\slide-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useActionState, useState } from "react";
import ImageUpload from "@/components/admin/image-upload";
import type { HeroSlideData } from "@/lib/hero-slides";
import { saveHeroSlide } from "./actions";

export default function SlideForm({
  slide,
}: {
  slide?: HeroSlideData;
}) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    saveHeroSlide,
    { error: "", success: "" },
  );

  const [desktopImage, setDesktopImage] = useState(
    slide?.desktopImage ?? "",
  );

  const [mobileImage, setMobileImage] = useState(
    slide?.mobileImage ?? "",
  );

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="category-form">
      <input type="hidden" name="id" value={slide?.id ?? ""} />
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="desktopImage"
        value={desktopImage}
      />
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="mobileImage"
        value={mobileImage}
      />

      <fieldset disabled={pending}>
        <div className="category-form-grid">
          <label>
            <span>Small heading — optional</span>
            <input
              name="eyebrow"
              defaultValue={slide?.eyebrow ?? ""}
              placeholder="The new collection"
              maxLength={50}
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Main title</span>
            <input
              name="title"
              defaultValue={slide?.title ?? ""}
              placeholder="Objects of everyday desire."
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={90}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="category-full-width">
            <span>Short description — optional</span>
            <textarea
              name="subtitle"
              defaultValue={slide?.subtitle ?? ""}
              rows={2}
              maxLength={240}
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Button text</span>
            <input
              name="buttonText"
              defaultValue={slide?.buttonText ?? "Shop Collection"}
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={40}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Button link</span>
            <input
              name="buttonHref"
              defaultValue={slide?.buttonHref ?? "/shop"}
              placeholder="/shop?category=women"
              maxLength={300}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Image description — alt text</span>
            <input
              name="altText"
              defaultValue={slide?.altText ?? ""}
              placeholder="Describe what appears in this banner"
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={180}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Display order</span>
            <input
              name="sortOrder"
              type="number"
              min={0}
              max={9999}
              step={1}
              defaultValue={slide?.sortOrder ?? 10}
              required
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="hero-image-editors">
          <section>
            <h3>Desktop image</h3>
            <p className="category-help">
              Recommended size: 1600 × 650 pixels.
            </p>

            {desktopImage && (
              <Image
                src={desktopImage}
                alt="Selected desktop banner"
                width={640}
                height={260}
                className="hero-admin-preview"
                unoptimized
              />
            )}

            <ImageUpload
              onUploaded={(image) => setDesktopImage(image.url)}
            />
          </section>

          <section>
            <h3>Mobile image — optional</h3>
            <p className="category-help">
              Recommended size: 800 × 900 pixels.
              Without this image, desktop artwork is used.
            </p>

            {mobileImage && (
              <>
                <Image
                  src={mobileImage}
                  alt="Selected mobile banner"
                  width={300}
                  height={338}
                  className="hero-admin-preview hero-admin-mobile"
                  unoptimized
                />

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="product-small-button"
                  onClick={() => setMobileImage("")}
                >
                  Use desktop image on mobile
                </button>
              </>
            )}

            <ImageUpload
              onUploaded={(image) => setMobileImage(image.url)}
            />
          </section>
        </div>

        <label className="category-checkbox">
          <input
            name="isActive"
            type="checkbox"
            defaultChecked={slide?.isActive ?? true}
          />
          <span>Show this slide on the homepage</span>
        </label>

        <p className="category-help">
          Lower display order appears first. Keep headings short.
        </p>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="primary-button"
          disabled={!desktopImage}
        >
          {pending
            ? "Saving..."
            : slide
              ? "Save slide"
              : "Create slide"}
        </button>
      </fieldset>

      <div aria-live="polite">
        {state.error && (
          <p className="admin-form-error">{state.error}</p>
        )}

        {state.success && (
          <p className="category-success">{state.success}</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\homepage\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { mediaImageExists } from "@/lib/media-storage";

export type HomeSectionState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

function text(formData: FormData, key: string) {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function validLink(value: string) {
  if (
    !value.startsWith("/") ||
    value.startsWith("//") ||
    /[\\\u0000-\u0020]/.test(value) ||
    value.length > 300
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  try {
    const url = new URL(value, "https://bimlay.com");

    return (
      url.origin === "https://bimlay.com" &&
      (
        url.pathname === "/" ||
        url.pathname === "/shop" ||
        /^\/products\/[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/.test(
          url.pathname,
        )
      )
    );
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

async function imageExists(url: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    return await mediaImageExists(url);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function saveHomeSection(
  _previousState: HomeSectionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<HomeSectionState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can change homepage content.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const sectionKey = text(formData, "sectionKey");

  if (
    sectionKey !== "FOR_HIM" &&
    sectionKey !== "FOR_HER" &&
    sectionKey !== "STORY"
  ) {
    return { error: "Invalid section.", success: "" };
  }

  const eyebrow = text(formData, "eyebrow");
  const title = text(formData, "title");
  const description = text(formData, "description");
  const imageUrl = text(formData, "imageUrl");
  const mobileImageUrl = text(formData, "mobileImageUrl");
  const altText = text(formData, "altText");
  const buttonText = text(formData, "buttonText");
  const buttonHref = text(formData, "buttonHref");
  const isActive = formData.get("isActive") === "on";

  if (
    title.length < 2 ||
    title.length > 120 ||
    eyebrow.length > 50 ||
    description.length > 500 ||
    altText.length > 180
  ) {
    return {
      error:
        "Check text lengths: title 2–120, heading up to 50, description up to 500 and image description up to 180 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    buttonText.length < 2 ||
    buttonText.length > 40 ||
    !validLink(buttonHref)
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Enter button text and a valid internal shop link.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (isActive && (!imageUrl || altText.length < 2)) {
    return {
      error: "Upload an image and describe it before activating this section.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (imageUrl && !(await imageExists(imageUrl))) {
    return {
      error: "The selected image is missing. Upload it again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (mobileImageUrl && !(await imageExists(mobileImageUrl))) {
    return {
      error: "The mobile image is missing. Upload it again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const data = {
    eyebrow: eyebrow || null,
    title,
    description: description || null,
    imageUrl: imageUrl || null,
    mobileImageUrl: mobileImageUrl || null,
    altText,
    buttonText,
    buttonHref,
    isActive,
  };

  try {
    await db.homeSection.upsert({
      where: { key: sectionKey },
      create: {
        key: sectionKey,
        ...data,
      },
      update: data,
    });
  } catch {
    return {
      error: "Could not save this section. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/admin/homepage");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Homepage section saved.",
  };
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { getHomeSections } from "@/lib/home-sections";
import HomeSectionForm from "./section-form";

const sections = [
  { key: "FOR_HIM", name: "For Him" },
  { key: "FOR_HER", name: "For Her" },
  { key: "STORY", name: "Story" },
] as const;

export default async function AdminHomepage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<
    Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>
  >;
}) {
  await connection();

  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return (
      <section className="admin-next-step">
        <h1>Owner access required</h1>
      </section>
    );
  }

  const params = await searchParams;
  const requested = typeof params.section === "string"
    ? params.section
    : "";

  const selected =
    sections.find((section) => section.key === requested) ??
    sections[0];

  const records = await getHomeSections(false);

  const current = records.find(
    (record) => record.key === selected.key,
  );

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Homepage content</p>
        <h1>Homepage sections</h1>
        <p>Update your campaign images, copy and links.</p>
      </section>

      <nav className="homepage-editor-tabs" aria-label="Edit section">
        {sections.map((section) => (
          <Link
            key={section.key}
            href={`/admin/homepage?section=${section.key}`}
            className={
              section.key === selected.key ? "selected" : ""
            }
          >
            {section.name}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>

      <section className="category-panel">
        <h2 className="product-section-title">
          Edit {selected.name}
        </h2>

        <HomeSectionForm
          key={selected.key}
          sectionKey={selected.key}
          section={current}
        />
      </section>
    </>
  );
}

import Image from "next/image";

type SectionKey = "FOR_HIM" | "FOR_HER" | "STORY";

const defaults: Record<

export default function HomeSectionForm({
  sectionKey,
  section,
}: {

              name="eyebrow"

              maxLength={50}
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Main title</span>
            <input
              name="title"
              defaultValue={section?.title ?? initial.title}
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={120}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="category-full-width">
            <span>Description</span>
            <textarea
              name="description"
              defaultValue={
                section?.description ?? initial.description
              }
              rows={3}
              maxLength={500}
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Button text</span>
            <input
              name="buttonText"
              defaultValue={
                section?.buttonText ?? initial.buttonText
              }
              minLength={2}
              maxLength={40}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label>
            <span>Button link</span>
            <input
              name="buttonHref"
              defaultValue={
                section?.buttonHref ?? initial.buttonHref
              }
              maxLength={300}
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="category-full-width">
            <span>Image description — alt text</span>
            <input
              name="altText"
              defaultValue={section?.altText ?? ""}
              maxLength={180}

            />
          </label>
        </div>

                alt="Selected section image"
                width={600}

              onUploaded={(image) => setImageUrl(image.url)}

                </button>
              </>
            )}

            <ImageUpload
              onUploaded={(image) =>
                setMobileImageUrl(image.url)
              }
            />
          </section>
        </div>

        <label className="category-checkbox">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            name="isActive"
            defaultChecked={section?.isActive ?? false}
          />
          <span>Show this section on the homepage</span>
        </label>

        <p className="category-help">
          You can save a hidden section before uploading its images.
        </p>

        <button type="submit" className="primary-button">
          {pending ? "Saving..." : "Save section"}
        </button>
      </fieldset>

      <div aria-live="polite">
        {state.error && (
          <p className="admin-form-error">{state.error}</p>
        )}

        {state.success && (
          <p className="category-success">{state.success}</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\orders\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  allowedPaymentStates,
  isOrderState,
  NEXT_ORDER_STATES,
} from "@/lib/order-rules";

export type OrderActionState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

class OrderUpdateError extends Error {}

function text(formData: FormData, key: string) {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function refreshOrderPages(orderId: string) {
  revalidatePath("/admin/orders");
  revalidatePath(`/admin/orders/${orderId}`);
  revalidatePath(`/order-confirmation/${orderId}`);
  revalidatePath("/admin/products");
  revalidatePath("/admin");
  revalidatePath("/shop");
  revalidatePath("/");
}

function errorState(error: unknown): OrderActionState {
  if (error instanceof OrderUpdateError) {
    return { error: error.message, success: "" };
  }

  if (
    error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
    error.code === "P2034"
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Another update happened at the same time. Refresh and try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  return {
    error: "Could not update this order. Please try again.",
    success: "",
  };
}

export async function changeOrderStatus(
  _previousState: OrderActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<OrderActionState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can change order status.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const orderId = text(formData, "orderId");
  const target = text(formData, "status");
  const reason = text(formData, "reason");
  const restockReturn = formData.get("restockReturn") === "on";

  if (!orderId || orderId.length > 100 || !isOrderState(target)) {
    return { error: "Invalid order or status.", success: "" };
  }

  if (reason.length > 500) {
    return {
      error: "Notes must not exceed 500 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (
    (target === "CANCELLED" || target === "RETURNED") &&
    reason.length < 5
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Enter a reason containing at least 5 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    await db.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const order = await tx.order.findUnique({
          where: { id: orderId },
          include: { items: true },
        });

        if (!order) {
          throw new OrderUpdateError("Order not found.");
        }

        if (!NEXT_ORDER_STATES[order.status].includes(target)) {
          throw new OrderUpdateError(
            "This status change is not allowed. Refresh to see the current order status.",
          );
        }

        const shouldRestock =
          target === "CANCELLED" ||
          (target === "RETURNED" && restockReturn);

        let restoredUnits = 0;

        if (shouldRestock && !order.stockReleasedAt) {
          for (const item of order.items) {
            if (!item.variantId) continue;

            const result = await tx.productVariant.updateMany({
              where: { id: item.variantId },
              data: {
                stock: { increment: item.quantity },
              },
            });

            if (result.count === 1) {
              restoredUnits += item.quantity;
            }
          }
        }

        await tx.order.update({
          where: { id: orderId },
          data: {
            status: target,
            stockReleasedAt:
              shouldRestock && !order.stockReleasedAt
                ? new Date()
                : order.stockReleasedAt,
          },
        });

        const stockNote = shouldRestock
          ? `Restocked ${restoredUnits} units into existing variants.`
          : target === "RETURNED"
            ? "Returned items were not restocked."
            : "";

        await tx.orderAudit.create({
          data: {
            orderId,
            adminId: admin.id,
            actorEmail: admin.email,
            event: "STATUS_CHANGED",
            fromValue: order.status,
            toValue: target,
            note: [reason, stockNote].filter(Boolean).join(" "),
          },
        });
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
        timeout: 15000,
      },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorState(error);
  }

  refreshOrderPages(orderId);

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Order status updated.",
  };
}

export async function changeOrderPayment(
  _previousState: OrderActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<OrderActionState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can record payments.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const orderId = text(formData, "orderId");
  const target = text(formData, "paymentStatus");
  const note = text(formData, "paymentNote");

  if (
    !orderId ||
    orderId.length > 100 ||
    (target !== "PAID" && target !== "REFUNDED")
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Invalid payment update.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (note.length < 5 || note.length > 500) {
    return {
      error: "Enter a payment reference or note of 5 to 500 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    await db.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const order = await tx.order.findUnique({
          where: { id: orderId },
        });

        if (!order) {
          throw new OrderUpdateError("Order not found.");
        }

        const allowed = allowedPaymentStates(
          order.status,
          order.paymentStatus,
        );

        if (!allowed.includes(target)) {
          throw new OrderUpdateError(
            "This payment change is not allowed for the current order.",
          );
        }

        await tx.order.update({
          where: { id: orderId },
          data: {
            paymentStatus: target,
          },
        });

        await tx.orderAudit.create({
          data: {
            orderId,
            adminId: admin.id,
            actorEmail: admin.email,
            event: "PAYMENT_CHANGED",
            fromValue: order.paymentStatus,
            toValue: target,
            note,
          },
        });
      },
      {
        isolationLevel:
          Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    return errorState(error);
  }

  refreshOrderPages(orderId);

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Payment record updated.",
  };
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\orders\order-controls.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useActionState, useState } from "react";
import {
  allowedPaymentStates,
  label,
  NEXT_ORDER_STATES,
  type OrderState,
} from "@/lib/order-rules";

import {
  changeOrderPayment,
  changeOrderStatus,
} from "./actions";

type Props = {
  orderId: string;
  status: OrderState;
  paymentStatus: string;
};

export default function OrderControls({
  orderId,
  status,
  paymentStatus,
}: Props) {
  const nextStatuses = NEXT_ORDER_STATES[status];

  const [selectedStatus, setSelectedStatus] = useState<string>(
    nextStatuses[0] ?? "",
  );

  const [statusState, statusAction, statusPending] =
    useActionState(changeOrderStatus, {
      error: "",
      success: "",
    });

  const [paymentState, paymentAction, paymentPending] =
    useActionState(changeOrderPayment, {
      error: "",
      success: "",
    });

  const payments = allowedPaymentStates(status, paymentStatus);

  return (
    <div className="order-controls-grid">
      <section className="category-panel">
        <h2>Order status</h2>
        <p className="category-help">
          Current status: {label(status)}
        </p>

        {nextStatuses.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="category-help">
            No further status changes are available.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <form action={statusAction} className="category-form">
            <input type="hidden" name="orderId" value={orderId} />

            <fieldset disabled={statusPending}>
              <div className="category-form-grid">
                <label className="category-full-width">
                  <span>Change status to</span>
                  <select
                    name="status"
                    value={selectedStatus}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setSelectedStatus(event.target.value)
                    }
                    required
                  >
                    {nextStatuses.map((value) => (
                      <option key={value} value={value}>
                        {label(value)}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>

                <label className="category-full-width">
                  <span>Reason / internal note</span>
                  <textarea
                    name="reason"
                    rows={3}
                    maxLength={500}
                    minLength={
                      selectedStatus === "CANCELLED" ||
                      selectedStatus === "RETURNED"
                        ? 5
                        : undefined
                    }
                    required={
                      selectedStatus === "CANCELLED" ||
                      selectedStatus === "RETURNED"
                    }
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              {selectedStatus === "RETURNED" && (
                <label className="category-checkbox">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    name="restockReturn"
                  />
                  <span>
                    Returned goods are received and fit for resale.
                    Add them back to stock.
                  </span>
                </label>
              )}

              {selectedStatus === "CANCELLED" && (
                <p className="category-help">
                  Cancelling restores stock to existing variants.
                </p>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="primary-button"
              >
                {statusPending ? "Updating..." : "Update status"}
              </button>
            </fieldset>
          </form>
        )}

        <div aria-live="polite">
          {statusState.error && (
            <p className="admin-form-error">
              {statusState.error}
            </p>
          )}
          {statusState.success && (
            <p className="category-success">
              {statusState.success}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="category-panel">
        <h2>Payment record</h2>
        <p className="category-help">
          Current payment: {label(paymentStatus)}
        </p>

        {payments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="category-help">
            Payment updates are unavailable at this order stage.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <form action={paymentAction} className="category-form">
            <input type="hidden" name="orderId" value={orderId} />

            <fieldset disabled={paymentPending}>
              <div className="category-form-grid">
                <label className="category-full-width">
                  <span>Record payment as</span>
                  <select name="paymentStatus" required>
                    {payments.map((value) => (
                      <option key={value} value={value}>
                        {label(value)}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>

                <label className="category-full-width">
                  <span>Payment reference / note</span>
                  <textarea
                    name="paymentNote"
                    rows={3}
                    minLength={5}
                    maxLength={500}
                    required
                    placeholder="Record when and how payment or refund was completed."
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <p className="category-help">
                Record Paid only after receiving the money.
                Record Refunded only after completing the refund.
              </p>

              <button type="submit" className="primary-button">
                {paymentPending ? "Saving..." : "Save payment record"}
              </button>
            </fieldset>
          </form>
        )}

        <div aria-live="polite">
          {paymentState.error && (
            <p className="admin-form-error">
              {paymentState.error}
            </p>
          )}
          {paymentState.success && (
            <p className="category-success">
              {paymentState.success}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\orders\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import type { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  isOrderState,
  label,
  ORDER_STATUSES,
} from "@/lib/order-rules";

type SearchValues = Record<
  string,
  string | string[] | undefined
>;

function single(value: string | string[] | undefined) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : "";
}

export default async function OrdersPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<SearchValues>;
}) {
  await connection();
  await requireAdmin();

  const params = await searchParams;
  const query = single(params.q).trim().slice(0, 100);
  const status = single(params.status);
  const rawPage = Number(single(params.page) || "1");

  const requestedPage =
    Number.isSafeInteger(rawPage) && rawPage > 0
      ? rawPage
      : 1;

  const where: Prisma.OrderWhereInput = {};

  if (isOrderState(status)) {
    where.status = status;
  }

  if (query) {
    where.OR = [
      {
        orderNumber: {
          contains: query,
          mode: "insensitive",
        },
      },
      {
        customerName: {
          contains: query,
          mode: "insensitive",
        },
      },
      {
        phone: {
          contains: query,
        },
      },
    ];
  }

  const total = await db.order.count({ where });
  const pageSize = 20;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const page = Math.min(requestedPage, totalPages);

  const orders = await db.order.findMany({
    where,
    take: pageSize,
    skip: (page - 1) * pageSize,
    orderBy: [
      { createdAt: "desc" },
      { id: "desc" },
    ],
    select: {
      id: true,
      orderNumber: true,
      customerName: true,
      phone: true,
      total: true,
      status: true,
      paymentStatus: true,
      createdAt: true,
    },
  });

  function pageHref(target: number) {
    const values = new URLSearchParams();

    if (query) values.set("q", query);
    if (isOrderState(status)) values.set("status", status);
    values.set("page", String(target));

    return `/admin/orders?${values.toString()}`;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Sales workspace</p>
        <h1>Orders</h1>
        <p>{total} matching orders.</p>
      </section>

      <form
        action="/admin/orders"
        method="get"
        className="order-filter"
      >
        <input
          name="q"
          defaultValue={query}
          placeholder="Order number, customer name or phone"
          maxLength={100}
          aria-label="Search orders"
        />

        <select
          name="status"
          defaultValue={isOrderState(status) ? status : ""}
          aria-label="Order status"
        >
          <option value="">All statuses</option>
          {ORDER_STATUSES.map((value) => (
            <option key={value} value={value}>
              {label(value)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>

        <button type="submit" className="primary-button">
          Search
        </button>

        <Link href="/admin/orders" className="text-link">
          Reset
        </Link>
      </form>

      {orders.length === 0 ? (
        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2>No orders found.</h2>
          <p className="category-help">
            Orders placed through checkout will appear here.
          </p>
        </section>
      ) : (
        <div className="order-list">
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <article className="order-list-card" key={order.id}>
              <div>
                <Link
                  href={`/admin/orders/${order.id}`}
                  className="order-number-link"
                >
                  {order.orderNumber}
                </Link>

                <p>{order.customerName}</p>
                <p className="category-help">{order.phone}</p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="category-status">
                  {label(order.status)}
                </span>

                <p className="category-help">
                  Payment: {label(order.paymentStatus)}
                </p>

                <strong>
                  ৳ {order.total.toFixed(2)}
                </strong>
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="category-help">
                  {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
                    dateStyle: "medium",
                    timeStyle: "short",
                    timeZone: "Asia/Dhaka",
                  }).format(order.createdAt)}
                </p>

                <Link
                  href={`/admin/orders/${order.id}`}
                  className="product-small-button"
                >
                  View order
                </Link>
              </div>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {totalPages > 1 && (
        <nav className="store-pagination" aria-label="Order pages">
          {page > 1 && (
            <Link href={pageHref(page - 1)}>Previous</Link>
          )}

          <span>Page {page} of {totalPages}</span>

          {page < totalPages && (
            <Link href={pageHref(page + 1)}>Next</Link>
          )}
        </nav>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
