===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\orders\[id]\invoice\page.tsx =====


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\actions.ts =====
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";
import {
  MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN,
  mediaImageExists,
} from "@/lib/media-storage";

export type ProductFormState = {
  error: string;
};

class ProductInputError extends Error {}

function text(formData: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function objectValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
    throw new ProductInputError("Invalid product data.");
  }

  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function readArray(
  formData: FormData,
  key: string,
  maximum: number,
): unknown[] {
  const raw = text(formData, key);

  if (!raw || raw.length > 30000) {
    throw new ProductInputError("Invalid product data.");
  }

  let parsed: unknown;

  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductInputError("Invalid product data.");
  }

  if (
    !Array.isArray(parsed) ||
    parsed.length === 0 ||
    parsed.length > maximum
  ) {
    throw new ProductInputError(
      key === "images"
        ? "Add between 1 and 8 product images."
        : "Add between 1 and 12 product variants.",
    );
  }

  return parsed;
}

function parsePrice(value: string): Prisma.Decimal {
  if (!/^\d{1,10}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    throw new ProductInputError(
      "Enter a valid price with up to 2 decimal places.",
    );
  }

  const price = new Prisma.Decimal(value);

  if (price.lte(0)) {
    throw new ProductInputError("Product price must be greater than zero.");
  }

  return price;
}

export async function createProduct(
  _previousState: ProductFormState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ProductFormState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return { error: "Only the owner can create products." };
  }

  try {
    const name = text(formData, "name");
    const slug = text(formData, "slug").toLowerCase();
    const categoryId = text(formData, "categoryId");
    const shortDescription = text(formData, "shortDescription");
    const description = text(formData, "description");
    const status = text(formData, "status");
    const isFeatured = formData.get("isFeatured") === "on";

    if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 180) {
      throw new ProductInputError(
        "Product name must contain 2 to 180 characters.",
      );
    }

    if (
      slug.length < 2 ||
      slug.length > 180 ||
      !/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/.test(slug)
    ) {
      throw new ProductInputError(
        "Enter a valid English slug. Example: premium-cotton-shirt",
      );
    }

    if (!categoryId || categoryId.length > 100) {
      throw new ProductInputError("Choose a category.");
    }

    if (shortDescription.length > 300 || description.length > 10000) {
      throw new ProductInputError(
        "Short description: maximum 300 characters. Description: maximum 10000.",
      );
    }

    if (status !== "DRAFT" && status !== "ACTIVE") {
      throw new ProductInputError("Choose Draft or Active.");
    }

    const variants = readArray(formData, "variants", 12).map(
      (item, index) => {
        const value = objectValue(item);

        const sku = stringValue(value.sku).toUpperCase();
        const size = stringValue(value.size);
        const color = stringValue(value.color);
        const price = parsePrice(stringValue(value.price));
        const compareText = stringValue(value.compareAtPrice);
        const stockText = stringValue(value.stock);

        if (!/^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(sku)) {
          throw new ProductInputError(
            `Variant ${index + 1}: enter an SKU using English letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores.`,
          );
        }

        if (size.length > 40 || color.length > 60) {
          throw new ProductInputError(
            `Variant ${index + 1}: size or colour is too long.`,
          );
        }

        if (
          !/^\d{1,7}$/.test(stockText) ||
          Number(stockText) > 1000000
        ) {
          throw new ProductInputError(
            `Variant ${index + 1}: stock must be a whole number from 0 to 1000000.`,
          );
        }

        const compareAtPrice = compareText
          ? parsePrice(compareText)
          : null;

        if (compareAtPrice && compareAtPrice.lte(price)) {
          throw new ProductInputError(
            `Variant ${index + 1}: regular price must be higher than selling price, or left empty.`,
          );
        }

        return {
          sku,
          size: size || null,
          color: color || null,
          price,
          compareAtPrice,
          stock: Number(stockText),
        };
      },
    );

    if (new Set(variants.map((variant) => variant.sku)).size !== variants.length) {
      throw new ProductInputError("Each variant must have a different SKU.");
    }

    const combinations = variants.map((variant) =>
      JSON.stringify([
        variant.size?.toLowerCase() ?? "",
        variant.color?.toLowerCase() ?? "",
      ]),
    );

    if (new Set(combinations).size !== combinations.length) {
      throw new ProductInputError(
        "Each size and colour combination must be different.",
      );
    }

    const images = readArray(formData, "images", 8).map(
      (item, index) => {
        const value = objectValue(item);
        const url = stringValue(value.url);

        if (!url.startsWith("/media/")) {
          throw new ProductInputError("Use an uploaded product image.");
        }

        const filename = url.slice("/media/".length);

        if (!MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename)) {
          throw new ProductInputError("Invalid product image URL.");
        }

        return {
          url,
          alt: `${name} — image ${index + 1}`,
          sortOrder: index,
        };
      },
    );

    if (new Set(images.map((image) => image.url)).size !== images.length) {
      throw new ProductInputError("Do not add the same image twice.");
    }
for (const image of images) {
  let exists: boolean;

  try {
    exists = await mediaImageExists(image.url);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductInputError(
      "Could not verify an image. Please try again.",
    );
  }

  if (!exists) {
    throw new ProductInputError(
      "A selected image is missing. Please upload it again.",
    );
  }
}

    await db.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const category = await tx.category.findUnique({
          where: { id: categoryId },
          select: {
            isActive: true,
            parent: {
              select: { isActive: true },
            },
          },
        });

        if (
          !category ||
          !category.isActive ||
          (category.parent && !category.parent.isActive)
        ) {
          throw new ProductInputError(
            "Choose an active category with an active parent.",
          );
        }

        await tx.product.create({
          data: {
            name,
            slug,
            categoryId,
            shortDescription: shortDescription || null,
            description,
            status,
            isFeatured,
            variants: {
              create: variants,
            },
            images: {
              create: images,
            },
          },
        });
      },
      {
        isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ProductInputError) {
      return { error: error.message };
    }

    if (error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError) {
      if (error.code === "P2002") {
        return {
          error: "This product slug or an SKU is already used. Choose a unique value.",
        };
      }

      if (error.code === "P2034") {
        return {
          error: "Another update happened at the same time. Please save again.",
        };
      }
    }

    return {
      error: "Could not create the product. Please try again.",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/products");
  revalidatePath("/admin");

  redirect("/admin/products");
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\edit-actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

import { mediaImageExists } from "@/lib/media-storage";

export type ProductEditState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

class ProductEditError extends Error {}

function text(formData: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function priceValue(value: string): Prisma.Decimal {
  if (!/^\d{1,10}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    throw new ProductEditError(
      "Enter a valid price with up to 2 decimal places.",
    );
  }

  const price = new Prisma.Decimal(value);

  if (price.lte(0)) {
    throw new ProductEditError("Price must be greater than zero.");
  }

  return price;
}

type NewVariantInput = {
  sku: string;
  size: string | null;
  color: string | null;
  price: Prisma.Decimal;
  compareAtPrice: Prisma.Decimal | null;
  stock: number;
  isActive: boolean;
};

function parseNewVariants(formData: FormData): NewVariantInput[] {
  const raw = formData.get("newVariants");

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.length > 30000) {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid new variant data.");
  }

  let decoded: unknown;

  try {
    decoded = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid new variant data.");
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(decoded) || decoded.length > 12) {
    throw new ProductEditError("A product can have up to 12 variants.");
  }

  return decoded.map((item, index) => {
    if (
      !item ||
      typeof item !== "object" ||
      Array.isArray(item) ||
      typeof item.sku !== "string" ||
      typeof item.size !== "string" ||
      typeof item.color !== "string" ||
      typeof item.price !== "string" ||
      typeof item.compareAtPrice !== "string" ||
      typeof item.stock !== "string" ||
      typeof item.isActive !== "boolean"
    ) {
      throw new ProductEditError(`New variant ${index + 1}: invalid data.`);
    }

    const sku = item.sku.trim().toUpperCase();
    const size = item.size.trim();
    const color = item.color.trim();
    const price = priceValue(item.price.trim());
    const regularText = item.compareAtPrice.trim();
    const stockText = item.stock.trim();

    if (!/^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_-]{0,63}$/.test(sku)) {
      throw new ProductEditError(
        `New variant ${index + 1}: enter a valid SKU.`,
      );
    }

    if (size.length > 40 || color.length > 60) {
      throw new ProductEditError(
        `New variant ${index + 1}: size or colour is too long.`,
      );
    }

    if (!/^\d{1,7}$/.test(stockText) || Number(stockText) > 1000000) {
      throw new ProductEditError(
        `New variant ${index + 1}: invalid stock quantity.`,
      );
    }

    const compareAtPrice = regularText ? priceValue(regularText) : null;

    if (compareAtPrice && compareAtPrice.lte(price)) {
      throw new ProductEditError(
        `New variant ${index + 1}: regular price must exceed selling price.`,
      );
    }

    return {
      sku,
      size: size || null,
      color: color || null,
      price,
      compareAtPrice,
      stock: Number(stockText),
      isActive: item.isActive,
    };
  });
}

type SubmittedImage = {
  id?: string;
  url?: string;
  alt: string;
};

function parseSubmittedImages(
  formData: FormData,
  status: string,
): SubmittedImage[] {
  if (formData.get("imagesUploading") === "1") {
    throw new ProductEditError(
      "Wait for the image upload to finish, then save again.",
    );
  }

  const raw = formData.get("images");

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.length > 16000) {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid image list.");
  }

  let decoded: unknown;

  try {
    decoded = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid image list.");
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(decoded) || decoded.length > 8) {
    throw new ProductEditError("A product can have up to 8 images.");
  }

  if (status === "ACTIVE" && decoded.length === 0) {
    throw new ProductEditError("An active product needs at least one image.");
  }

  const seenIds = new Set<string>();
  const seenUrls = new Set<string>();

  return decoded.map((item): SubmittedImage => {
    if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || typeof item.alt !== "string") {
      throw new ProductEditError("Invalid image information.");
    }

    const alt = item.alt.trim();

    if (alt.length < 2 || alt.length > 180) {
      throw new ProductEditError(
        "Each image description must contain 2 to 180 characters.",
      );
    }

    if ("id" in item) {
      if (
        typeof item.id !== "string" ||
        !item.id ||
        item.id.length > 100 ||
        seenIds.has(item.id) ||
        ("url" in item && typeof item.url !== "string")
      ) {
        throw new ProductEditError("Invalid existing image.");
      }

      seenIds.add(item.id);
      return { id: item.id, alt };
    }

    if (
      !("url" in item) ||
      typeof item.url !== "string" ||
      seenUrls.has(item.url)
    ) {
      throw new ProductEditError("Invalid uploaded image.");
    }

    const match =
      /^\/media\/([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}\.webp)$/i.exec(
        item.url,
      );

    if (!match) {
      throw new ProductEditError("Invalid uploaded image URL.");
    }

    seenUrls.add(item.url);

    return {
      url: item.url,
      alt,
    };
  });
}

function parseOriginalImageIds(formData: FormData): string[] {
  const raw = formData.get("imagesOriginal");

  if (typeof raw !== "string" || raw.length > 5000) {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid original image list.");
  }

  let decoded: unknown;

  try {
    decoded = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid original image list.");
  }

  if (
    !Array.isArray(decoded) ||
    decoded.length > 8 ||
    decoded.some((id) => typeof id !== "string" || id.length > 100)
  ) {
    throw new ProductEditError("Invalid original image list.");
  }

  return decoded as string[];
}

export async function updateProduct(
  _previousState: ProductEditState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ProductEditState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return {
      error: "Only the owner can edit products.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  const productId = text(formData, "productId");
  const name = text(formData, "name");
  const categoryId = text(formData, "categoryId");
  const shortDescription = text(formData, "shortDescription");
  const description = text(formData, "description");
  const status = text(formData, "status");
  const isFeatured = formData.get("isFeatured") === "on";

  if (
    !productId ||
    productId.length > 100 ||
    !categoryId ||
    categoryId.length > 100
  ) {
    return {
      error: "Invalid product or category.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 180) {
    return {
      error: "Product name must contain 2 to 180 characters.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (shortDescription.length > 300 || description.length > 10000) {
    return {
      error: "Product description is too long.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  if (status !== "DRAFT" && status !== "ACTIVE" && status !== "ARCHIVED") {
    return {
      error: "Choose a valid publication status.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  try {
    const newVariants = parseNewVariants(formData);
    const submittedImages = parseSubmittedImages(formData, status);

    const originalImageIds = parseOriginalImageIds(formData);

   for (const image of submittedImages) {
  if (!image.url) continue;

  let exists: boolean;

  try {
    exists = await mediaImageExists(image.url);
  } catch {
    throw new ProductEditError(
      "Could not verify an uploaded image. Please try again.",
    );
  }

  if (!exists) {
    throw new ProductEditError(
      "An uploaded image is missing. Upload it again.",
    );
  }
}
    await db.$transaction(
      async (tx) => {
        const product = await tx.product.findUnique({
          where: { id: productId },
          select: {
            id: true,
            variants: {
              select: {
                id: true,
                sku: true,
                size: true,
                color: true,
              },
            },
            images: {
              orderBy: [{ sortOrder: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
              select: {
                id: true,
              },
            },
          },
        });
        if (!product) {
          throw new ProductEditError("Product was not found.");
        }

        if (product.variants.length + newVariants.length > 12) {
          throw new ProductEditError("A product can have up to 12 variants.");
        }

        const allSkus = [
          ...product.variants.map((variant) => variant.sku),
          ...newVariants.map((variant) => variant.sku),
        ];

        if (new Set(allSkus).size !== allSkus.length) {
          throw new ProductEditError("Each variant must have a different SKU.");
        }

        const combinations = [
          ...product.variants.map((variant) =>
            JSON.stringify([
              variant.size?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "",
              variant.color?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? "",
            ]),
          ),
          ...newVariants.map((variant) =>
            JSON.stringify([
              variant.size?.toLowerCase() ?? "",
              variant.color?.toLowerCase() ?? "",
            ]),
          ),
        ];

        if (new Set(combinations).size !== combinations.length) {
          throw new ProductEditError(
            "Each size and colour combination must be different.",
          );
        }

        const databaseImageIds = product.images.map((image) => image.id);

        if (
          JSON.stringify(databaseImageIds) !== JSON.stringify(originalImageIds)
        ) {
          throw new ProductEditError(
            "Product images changed in another window. Refresh and try again.",
          );
        }

        const ownedImageIds = new Set(databaseImageIds);

        for (const image of submittedImages) {
          if (image.id && !ownedImageIds.has(image.id)) {
            throw new ProductEditError(
              "An image does not belong to this product.",
            );
          }
        }

        const category = await tx.category.findUnique({
          where: { id: categoryId },
          select: {
            isActive: true,
            parent: {
              select: { isActive: true },
            },
          },
        });

        if (!category) {
          throw new ProductEditError("Category was not found.");
        }

        if (
          status === "ACTIVE" &&
          (!category.isActive || (category.parent && !category.parent.isActive))
        ) {
          throw new ProductEditError(
            "An active product must belong to an active category with an active parent.",
          );
        }

        const updates = product.variants.map((variant) => {
          const price = priceValue(text(formData, `price-${variant.id}`));

          const regularText = text(formData, `regular-${variant.id}`);

          const compareAtPrice = regularText ? priceValue(regularText) : null;

          const stockText = text(formData, `stock-${variant.id}`);

          if (!/^\d{1,7}$/.test(stockText) || Number(stockText) > 1000000) {
            throw new ProductEditError(
              `${variant.sku}: stock must be a whole number from 0 to 1000000.`,
            );
          }

          if (compareAtPrice && compareAtPrice.lte(price)) {
            throw new ProductEditError(
              `${variant.sku}: regular price must exceed selling price, or be empty.`,
            );
          }

          return {
            id: variant.id,
            price,
            compareAtPrice,
            stock: Number(stockText),
            isActive: formData.get(`active-${variant.id}`) === "on",
          };
        });

        if (
          status === "ACTIVE" &&
          !updates.some((variant) => variant.isActive) &&
          !newVariants.some((variant) => variant.isActive)
        ) {
          throw new ProductEditError(
            "An active product needs at least one active variant.",
          );
        }

        await tx.product.update({
          where: { id: productId },
          data: {
            name,
            categoryId,
            shortDescription: shortDescription || null,
            description,
            status,
            isFeatured,
          },
        });

        for (const variant of updates) {
          await tx.productVariant.update({
            where: { id: variant.id },
            data: {
              price: variant.price,
              compareAtPrice: variant.compareAtPrice,
              stock: variant.stock,
              isActive: variant.isActive,
            },
          });
        }

        for (const variant of newVariants) {
          await tx.productVariant.create({
            data: {
              productId,
              sku: variant.sku,
              size: variant.size,
              color: variant.color,
              price: variant.price,
              compareAtPrice: variant.compareAtPrice,
              stock: variant.stock,
              isActive: variant.isActive,
            },
          });
        }

        const retainedIds = submittedImages
          .filter((image) => image.id)
          .map((image) => image.id as string);

        await tx.productImage.deleteMany({
          where: {
            productId,
            id: {
              notIn: retainedIds,
            },
          },
        });

        for (const [index, image] of submittedImages.entries()) {
          if (image.id) {
            await tx.productImage.update({
              where: { id: image.id },
              data: {
                alt: image.alt,
                sortOrder: index,
              },
            });
          } else if (image.url) {
            await tx.productImage.create({
              data: {
                productId,
                url: image.url,
                alt: image.alt,
                sortOrder: index,
              },
            });
          }
        }
      },
      {
        isolationLevel: Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable,
      },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ProductEditError) {
      return { error: error.message, success: "" };
    }

    if (
  error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
  error.code === "P2002"
) {
  return {
    error: "This SKU is already used by another product.",
    success: "",
  };
}

    if (
      error instanceof Prisma.PrismaClientKnownRequestError &&
      error.code === "P2034"
    ) {
      return {
        error: "Another update happened at the same time. Please save again.",
        success: "",
      };
    }

    return {
      error: "Could not update the product. Please try again.",
      success: "",
    };
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/products");
  revalidatePath(`/admin/products/${productId}/edit`);
  revalidatePath("/admin");
  revalidatePath("/");
  revalidatePath("/shop");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Product updated successfully.",
  };
}



===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\edit-product-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useActionState, useEffect } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import ProductImageEditor from "./product-image-editor";
import { updateProduct } from "./edit-actions";
import NewVariantEditor from "./new-variant-editor";

type ProductEditData = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  categoryId: string;
  shortDescription: string;
  description: string;
  status: "DRAFT" | "ACTIVE" | "ARCHIVED";
  isFeatured: boolean;
  images: {
    id: string;
    url: string;
    alt: string;
  }[];
  variants: {
    id: string;
    sku: string;
    size: string;
    color: string;
    price: string;
    compareAtPrice: string;
    stock: number;
    isActive: boolean;
  }[];
};

type CategoryOption = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

export default function EditProductForm({
  product,
  categories,
}: {
  product: ProductEditData;
  categories: CategoryOption[];
}) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(updateProduct, {
    error: "",
    success: "",
  });

  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.success) {
      router.refresh();
    }
  }, [state.success, router]);

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="product-editor">
      <input type="hidden" name="productId" value={product.id} />

      <fieldset disabled={pending}>
        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2 className="product-section-title">Product information</h2>

          <div className="category-form-grid">
            <label>
              <span>Product name</span>
              <input
                name="name"
                defaultValue={product.name}
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={180}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>URL slug — unchanged</span>
              <input value={product.slug} readOnly aria-readonly="true" />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Category</span>
              <select
                name="categoryId"
                defaultValue={product.categoryId}
                required
              >
                {categories.map((category) => (
                  <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                    {category.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Publication status</span>
              <select name="status" defaultValue={product.status}>
                <option value="DRAFT">Draft</option>
                <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
                <option value="ARCHIVED">Archived</option>
              </select>
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Short description</span>
              <textarea
                name="shortDescription"
                defaultValue={product.shortDescription}
                rows={2}
                maxLength={300}
              />
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Full description</span>
              <textarea
                name="description"
                defaultValue={product.description}
                rows={6}
                maxLength={10000}
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <label className="category-checkbox">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              name="isFeatured"
              defaultChecked={product.isFeatured}
            />
            <span>Feature this product on the homepage</span>
          </label>
        </section>

        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2 className="product-section-title">Pricing & stock</h2>

          <p className="category-help">
            Update each variant separately. An inactive variant will be excluded
            from the storefront when connected.
          </p>

          {product.variants.map((variant) => (
            <div className="product-variant" key={variant.id}>
              <div className="product-variant-heading">
                <div>
                  <h3>{variant.sku}</h3>
                  <p className="category-help">
                    {variant.size || "Standard size"}
                    {" · "}
                    {variant.color || "Standard colour"}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="category-form-grid">
                <label>
                  <span>Selling price — BDT</span>
                  <input
                    name={`price-${variant.id}`}
                    type="number"
                    min="0.01"
                    max="9999999999.99"
                    step="0.01"
                    defaultValue={variant.price}
                    required
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Regular price — optional</span>
                  <input
                    name={`regular-${variant.id}`}
                    type="number"
                    min="0.01"
                    max="9999999999.99"
                    step="0.01"
                    defaultValue={variant.compareAtPrice}
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Stock quantity</span>
                  <input
                    name={`stock-${variant.id}`}
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    max={1000000}
                    step={1}
                    defaultValue={variant.stock}
                    required
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <label className="category-checkbox">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  name={`active-${variant.id}`}
                  defaultChecked={variant.isActive}
                />
                <span>Active variant</span>
              </label>
            </div>
          ))}
        </section>

        <NewVariantEditor
          key={product.variants.map((variant) => variant.id).join("|")}
          existingCount={product.variants.length}
        />

        <ProductImageEditor
          key={product.images.map((image) => image.id).join("|")}
          images={product.images}
          productName={product.name}
        />

        <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
          {state.error && (
            <p className="admin-form-error product-save-error">{state.error}</p>
          )}

          {state.success && (
            <p className="category-success product-save-message">
              {state.success}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <button type="submit" className="primary-button product-save-button">
          {pending ? "Saving changes..." : "Save changes"}
        </button>
      </fieldset>
    </form>
  );
}



===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\new-variant-editor.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type NewVariant = {
  key: number;
  sku: string;
  size: string;
  color: string;
  price: string;
  compareAtPrice: string;
  stock: string;
  isActive: boolean;
};

export default function NewVariantEditor({
  existingCount,
}: {
  existingCount: number;
}) {
  const [variants, setVariants] = useState<NewVariant[]>([]);
  const nextKey = useRef(0);

  function addVariant() {
    if (existingCount + variants.length >= 12) return;

    const key = nextKey.current++;

    setVariants((current) => [
      ...current,
      {
        key,
        sku: "",
        size: "",
        color: "",
        price: "",
        compareAtPrice: "",
        stock: "0",
        isActive: true,
      },
    ]);
  }

  function changeVariant(
    key: number,
    changes: Partial<Omit<NewVariant, "key">>,
  ) {
    setVariants((current) =>
      current.map((variant) =>
        variant.key === key
          ? { ...variant, ...changes }
          : variant,
      ),
    );
  }

  return (
    <section className="category-panel">
      <h2 className="product-section-title">
        Add size or colour variants
      </h2>

      <p className="category-help">
        Existing variants stay above. Add up to 12 variants in
        total. Every SKU and size/colour combination must be unique.
      </p>

      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="newVariants"
        value={JSON.stringify(
          variants.map((variant) => ({
            sku: variant.sku,
            size: variant.size,
            color: variant.color,
            price: variant.price,
            compareAtPrice: variant.compareAtPrice,
            stock: variant.stock,
            isActive: variant.isActive,
          })),
        )}
      />

      {variants.map((variant, index) => (
        <div className="product-variant" key={variant.key}>
          <div className="product-variant-heading">
            <h3>New variant {index + 1}</h3>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="product-small-button"
              onClick={() =>
                setVariants((current) =>
                  current.filter(
                    (item) => item.key !== variant.key,
                  ),
                )
              }
            >
              Remove
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="category-form-grid">
            <label>
              <span>Unique SKU</span>
              <input
                value={variant.sku}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    sku: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                maxLength={64}
                placeholder="BIMLAY-SHIRT-BLK-M"
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Size - optional</span>
              <input
                value={variant.size}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    size: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                maxLength={40}
                placeholder="M"
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Colour - optional</span>
              <input
                value={variant.color}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    color: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                maxLength={60}
                placeholder="Black"
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Selling price - BDT</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0.01"
                max="9999999999.99"
                step="0.01"
                value={variant.price}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    price: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Regular price - optional</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0.01"
                max="9999999999.99"
                step="0.01"
                value={variant.compareAtPrice}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    compareAtPrice: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Stock quantity</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0"
                max="1000000"
                step="1"
                value={variant.stock}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  changeVariant(variant.key, {
                    stock: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                required
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <label className="category-checkbox">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={variant.isActive}
              onChange={(event) =>
                changeVariant(variant.key, {
                  isActive: event.target.checked,
                })
              }
            />
            <span>Active variant</span>
          </label>
        </div>
      ))}

      <button
        type="button"
        className="product-small-button"
        onClick={addVariant}
        disabled={existingCount + variants.length >= 12}
      >
        + Add variant
      </button>

      {existingCount + variants.length >= 12 && (
        <p className="category-help">
          Maximum of 12 variants reached.
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\page.tsx =====
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export default async function ProductsPage() {
  await connection();

  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  const [products, total] = await Promise.all([
    db.product.findMany({
      take: 50,
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
      select: {
        id: true,
        name: true,
        slug: true,
        status: true,
        isFeatured: true,
        category: {
          select: {
            name: true,
            isActive: true,
            parent: {
              select: { isActive: true },
            },
          },
        },
        images: {
          orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
          take: 1,
          select: {
            url: true,
            alt: true,
          },
        },
        variants: {
          where: { isActive: true },
          orderBy: { price: "asc" },
          select: {
            price: true,
            stock: true,
          },
        },
      },
    }),
    db.product.count(),
  ]);

  return (
    <>
      <section className="admin-welcome">
        <p className="eyebrow">Your catalogue</p>
        <h1>Products</h1>
        <p>
          {total} products in your database. Showing the latest{" "}
          {products.length}.
        </p>
      </section>

      {admin.role === "OWNER" && (
        <Link
          href="/admin/products/new"
          className="primary-button product-new-link"
        >
          Create product
        </Link>
      )}

      {products.length === 0 ? (
        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2>No products yet.</h2>
          <p className="category-help">
            Create your first product to start building your catalogue.
          </p>
        </section>
      ) : (
        <section className="product-admin-list">
          {products.map((product) => {
            const mainImage = product.images[0];
            const firstVariant = product.variants[0];
            const stock = product.variants.reduce(
              (totalStock, variant) => totalStock + variant.stock,
              0,
            );

            const categoryVisible =
              product.category.isActive &&
              (product.category.parent?.isActive ?? true);

            return (
              <article className="product-admin-row" key={product.id}>
                <div className="product-admin-thumbnail">
                  {mainImage && (
                    <Image
                      src={mainImage.url}
                      alt={mainImage.alt}
                      width={90}
                      height={110}
                      unoptimized
                    />
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="product-admin-information">
                  <p className="category-parent">{product.category.name}</p>
                  <h2>{product.name}</h2>
                  <p className="category-slug">{product.slug}</p>

                  {admin.role === "OWNER" && (
                    <Link
                      href={`/admin/products/${product.id}/edit`}
                      className="product-small-button product-edit-link"
                    >
                      Edit product
                    </Link>
                  )}

                  <div className="category-meta">
                    <span>{product.variants.length} variants</span>
                    <span>{stock} units in stock</span>
                    {product.isFeatured && <span>Featured</span>}
                    {!categoryVisible && <span>Category hidden</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="product-admin-price">
                  <span
                    className={`category-status ${
                      product.status === "ACTIVE" ? "is-active" : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    {product.status}
                  </span>

                  <strong>
                    {firstVariant
                      ? `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
                          minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                          maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                        }).format(Number(firstVariant.price))}`
                      : "No active variant"}
                  </strong>

                  {product.variants.length > 1 && (
                    <span className="category-help">Starting price</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </section>
      )}
    </>
  );
}



===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\product-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useActionState, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowUp, Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import ImageUpload from "@/components/admin/image-upload";
import { createProduct } from "./actions";

type CategoryOption = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

type Variant = {
  key: number;
  sku: string;
  size: string;
  color: string;
  price: string;
  compareAtPrice: string;
  stock: string;
};

type ProductImage = {
  url: string;
};

function emptyVariant(key: number): Variant {
  return {
    key,
    sku: "",
    size: "",
    color: "",
    price: "",
    compareAtPrice: "",
    stock: "0",
  };
}

export default function ProductForm({
  categories,
}: {
  categories: CategoryOption[];
}) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(
    createProduct,
    { error: "" },
  );

  const nextKey = useRef(1);

  const [variants, setVariants] = useState<Variant[]>([
    emptyVariant(0),
  ]);

  const [images, setImages] = useState<ProductImage[]>([]);

  function updateVariant(
    key: number,
    field: keyof Omit<Variant, "key">,
    value: string,
  ) {
    setVariants((current) =>
      current.map((variant) =>
        variant.key === key
          ? { ...variant, [field]: value }
          : variant,
      ),
    );
  }

  function addVariant() {
    const key = nextKey.current;
    nextKey.current += 1;

    setVariants((current) => [
      ...current,
      emptyVariant(key),
    ]);
  }

  function moveImageUp(index: number) {
    if (index === 0) return;

    setImages((current) => {
      const updated = [...current];
      [updated[index - 1], updated[index]] = [
        updated[index],
        updated[index - 1],
      ];
      return updated;
    });
  }

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="product-editor">
      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="variants"
        value={JSON.stringify(variants)}
      />

      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="images"
        value={JSON.stringify(images)}
      />

      <fieldset disabled={pending}>
        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2 className="product-section-title">
            Product information
          </h2>

          <div className="category-form-grid">
            <label>
              <span>Product name</span>
              <input
                name="name"
                placeholder="Premium Cotton Shirt"
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={180}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>URL slug</span>
              <input
                name="slug"
                placeholder="premium-cotton-shirt"
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={180}
                pattern="[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*"
                autoCapitalize="none"
                spellCheck={false}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Category</span>
              <select name="categoryId" defaultValue="" required>
                <option value="" disabled>
                  Choose a category
                </option>

                {categories.map((category) => (
                  <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                    {category.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Publication status</span>
              <select name="status" defaultValue="DRAFT">
                <option value="DRAFT">Draft</option>
                <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
              </select>
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Short description</span>
              <textarea
                name="shortDescription"
                rows={2}
                maxLength={300}
                placeholder="A short introduction to the product"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Full description</span>
              <textarea
                name="description"
                rows={6}
                maxLength={10000}
                placeholder="Material, fit, care instructions and product details"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          <label className="category-checkbox">
            <input type="checkbox" name="isFeatured" />
            <span>Feature this product on the homepage</span>
          </label>
        </section>

        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2 className="product-section-title">Product images</h2>

          <p className="category-help">
            Add up to 8 images. The first image is the main image.
            Use the up arrow to change their order.
          </p>

          {images.length > 0 && (
            <div className="product-image-selection">
              {images.map((image, index) => (
                <div className="product-selected-image" key={image.url}>
                  <Image
                    src={image.url}
                    alt={`Selected product image ${index + 1}`}
                    width={180}
                    height={180}
                    unoptimized
                  />

                  <span>
                    {index === 0 ? "Main image" : `Image ${index + 1}`}
                  </span>

                  <div className="product-image-controls">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={index === 0}
                      onClick={() => moveImageUp(index)}
                      aria-label={`Move image ${index + 1} earlier`}
                    >
                      <ArrowUp size={15} />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        setImages((current) =>
                          current.filter((item) => item.url !== image.url),
                        )
                      }
                      aria-label={`Remove image ${index + 1}`}
                    >
                      <X size={15} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {images.length < 8 && (
            <ImageUpload
              onUploaded={(uploaded) =>
                setImages((current) => {
                  if (
                    current.length >= 8 ||
                    current.some((item) => item.url === uploaded.url)
                  ) {
                    return current;
                  }

                  return [...current, { url: uploaded.url }];
                })
              }
            />
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="category-panel">
          <h2 className="product-section-title">
            Variants, pricing & stock
          </h2>

          <p className="category-help">
            Create one variant for each size and colour combination.
            For a simple product, leave size and colour empty.
          </p>

          {variants.map((variant, index) => (
            <div className="product-variant" key={variant.key}>
              <div className="product-variant-heading">
                <h3>Variant {index + 1}</h3>

                {variants.length > 1 && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="product-small-button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setVariants((current) =>
                        current.filter((item) => item.key !== variant.key),
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    Remove
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="category-form-grid">
                <label>
                  <span>SKU — unique stock code</span>
                  <input
                    value={variant.sku}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(variant.key, "sku", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="BIM-SHIRT-WHT-M"
                    maxLength={64}
                    required
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Size — optional</span>
                  <input
                    value={variant.size}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(variant.key, "size", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="M"
                    maxLength={40}
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Colour — optional</span>
                  <input
                    value={variant.color}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(variant.key, "color", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="White"
                    maxLength={60}
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Stock quantity</span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min={0}
                    max={1000000}
                    step={1}
                    value={variant.stock}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(variant.key, "stock", event.target.value)
                    }
                    required
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Selling price — BDT</span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0.01"
                    max="9999999999.99"
                    step="0.01"
                    value={variant.price}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(variant.key, "price", event.target.value)
                    }
                    placeholder="1800"
                    required
                  />
                </label>

                <label>
                  <span>Regular price — optional</span>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="0.01"
                    max="9999999999.99"
                    step="0.01"
                    value={variant.compareAtPrice}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      updateVariant(
                        variant.key,
                        "compareAtPrice",
                        event.target.value,
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="2200"
                  />
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {variants.length < 12 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="product-small-button"
              onClick={addVariant}
            >
              <Plus size={15} />
              Add variant
            </button>
          )}
        </section>

        <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
          {state.error && (
            <p className="admin-form-error product-save-error">
              {state.error}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="primary-button product-save-button"
          disabled={images.length === 0}
        >
          {pending ? "Creating product..." : "Create product"}
        </button>

        {images.length === 0 && (
          <p className="category-help">
            Upload at least one image to create this product.
          </p>
        )}
      </fieldset>
    </form>
  );
}


===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\products\product-image-editor.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ExistingImage = {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  alt: string;
};

type EditableImage = {
  id?: string;
  url: string;
  alt: string;
};

export default function ProductImageEditor({
  images: initialImages,
  productName,
}: {
  images: ExistingImage[];
  productName: string;
}) {
  const [images, setImages] = useState<EditableImage[]>(
    initialImages.map((image) => ({
      id: image.id,
      url: image.url,
      alt: image.alt || productName,
    })),
  );

  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  async function uploadImage(
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
  ) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    if (images.length >= 8) {
      setError("A product can have up to 8 images.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    if (
      !["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"].includes(file.type) ||
      file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024
    ) {
      setError("Choose a JPG, PNG or WebP image up to 8 MB.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setUploading(true);

    try {
      const body = new FormData();
      body.append("image", file);

      const response = await fetch("/api/admin/uploads", {
        method: "POST",
        body,
      });

      const result: unknown = await response.json();

      if (!result || typeof result !== "object") {
        throw new Error("Unexpected upload response.");
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message =
          "error" in result && typeof result.error === "string"
            ? result.error
            : "Image upload failed.";

        throw new Error(message);
      }

      if (
        !("url" in result) ||
        typeof result.url !== "string" ||
        !/^\/media\/[0-9a-f-]+\.webp$/i.test(result.url)
      ) {
        throw new Error("The upload did not return a valid image.");
      }

      setImages((current) => [
        ...current,
        {
          url: result.url as string,
          alt: productName,
        },
      ]);
    } catch (cause) {
      setError(
        cause instanceof Error
          ? cause.message
          : "Image upload failed.",
      );
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);

      if (fileRef.current) {
        fileRef.current.value = "";
      }
    }
  }

  function moveImage(index: number, direction: -1 | 1) {
    setImages((current) => {
      const destination = index + direction;

      if (destination < 0 || destination >= current.length) {
        return current;
      }

      const next = [...current];

      [next[index], next[destination]] = [
        next[destination],
        next[index],
      ];

      return next;
    });
  }

  return (
    <section className="category-panel">
      <h2 className="product-section-title">Product images</h2>

      <p className="category-help">
        The first image is the main product image. Add up to 8
        images, then save the product to keep your changes.
      </p>

      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="images"
        value={JSON.stringify(images)}
      />

      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="imagesOriginal"
        value={JSON.stringify(
          initialImages.map((image) => image.id),
        )}
      />

      <input
        type="hidden"
        name="imagesUploading"
        value={uploading ? "1" : "0"}
      />

      <div className="product-edit-images">
        {images.map((image, index) => (
          <div
            className="product-edit-image"
            key={image.id ?? image.url}
          >
            <div className="product-edit-image-preview">
              <Image
                src={image.url}
                alt={image.alt}
                width={220}
                height={220}
                unoptimized
              />
            </div>

            <p className="category-help">
              {index === 0
                ? "Main image"
                : `Image ${index + 1}`}
            </p>

            <label>
              <span>Image description (alt text)</span>
              <input
                value={image.alt}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  const alt = event.target.value;

                  setImages((current) =>
                    current.map((item, itemIndex) =>
                      itemIndex === index
                        ? { ...item, alt }
                        : item,
                    ),
                  );
                }}
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={180}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <div className="product-edit-image-buttons">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => moveImage(index, -1)}
                disabled={index === 0 || uploading}
              >
                Move up
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => moveImage(index, 1)}
                disabled={
                  index === images.length - 1 || uploading
                }
              >
                Move down
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setImages((current) =>
                    current.filter(
                      (_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index,
                    ),
                  )
                }
                disabled={uploading}
              >
                Remove
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <label className="product-edit-upload">
        <span>Add product image</span>
        <input
          ref={fileRef}
          type="file"
          accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
          onChange={uploadImage}
          disabled={uploading || images.length >= 8}
        />
      </label>

      <p className="category-help">
        JPG, PNG or WebP; up to 8 MB. An active product needs at
        least one image.
      </p>

      {uploading && (
        <p role="status">Uploading image...</p>
      )}

      {error && (
        <p className="admin-form-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

        <h1>Owner access required</h1>
        <p>Only the owner can create products.</p>
      </section>
    );

    where: {
      isActive: true,
      OR: [
        { parentId: null },
        { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
      ],

    orderBy: [
      { sortOrder: "asc" },
      { name: "asc" },
    ],

      id: true,
      name: true,

        select: { name: true },

        <h1>Create product</h1>

          <p>Create an active category before adding a product.</p>
          <Link
            href="/admin/categories"
            className="primary-button"
          >
            Manage categories
          </Link>
        </section>
      ) : (
        <ProductForm
          categories={categories.map((category) => ({
            id: category.id,
            name: category.parent
              ? `${category.parent.name} / ${category.name}`
              : category.name,
          }))}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== \src\app\admin\(protected)\settings\actions.ts =====
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { requireAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

export type SettingsState = {
  error: string;
  success: string;
};

function text(formData: FormData, key: string): string {
  const value = formData.get(key);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function fail(error: string): SettingsState {
  return { error, success: "" };
}

function parseCharge(value: string): Prisma.Decimal | null {
  if (!/^\d{1,7}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    return null;
  }

  const amount = new Prisma.Decimal(value);

  if (amount.lt(0) || amount.gt(1000000)) {
    return null;
  }

  return amount;
}

function normalizeWhatsApp(value: string): string {
  const digits = value
    .replace(/[০-৯]/g, (digit) =>
      String("০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯".indexOf(digit)),
    )
    .replace(/[\s()+-]/g, "");

  return digits.startsWith("01") ? `88${digits}` : digits;
}

function normalizeFacebook(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return "";

  if (value.length > 500) return null;

  try {
    const url = new URL(value);

    const allowedHosts = new Set([
      "facebook.com",
      "www.facebook.com",
      "m.facebook.com",
      "fb.com",
      "www.fb.com",
    ]);

    if (
      url.protocol !== "https:" ||
      !allowedHosts.has(url.hostname.toLowerCase()) ||
      url.username ||
      url.password ||
      url.port
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    url.hash = "";

    return url.toString();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function saveStoreSettings(
  _previousState: SettingsState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<SettingsState> {
  const admin = await requireAdmin();

  if (admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return fail("Only the owner can change store settings.");
  }

  const storeName = text(formData, "storeName");

  const supportPhone = text(formData, "supportPhone")
    .replace(/[\s()-]/g, "");

  const whatsappNumber = normalizeWhatsApp(
    text(formData, "whatsappNumber"),
  );

  const facebookPageUrl = normalizeFacebook(
    text(formData, "facebookPageUrl"),
  );

  const supportEmail = text(formData, "supportEmail");

  const dhakaDeliveryCharge = parseCharge(
    text(formData, "dhakaDeliveryCharge"),
  );

  const outsideDeliveryCharge = parseCharge(
    text(formData, "outsideDeliveryCharge"),
  );

  const codEnabled = formData.get("codEnabled") === "on";

  if (storeName.length < 2 || storeName.length > 100) {
    return fail("Store name must contain 2 to 100 characters.");
  }

  if (supportPhone && !/^\+?\d{7,15}$/.test(supportPhone)) {
    return fail("Enter a valid support phone number or leave it empty.");
  }

  if (
    whatsappNumber &&
    !/^8801[3-9]\d{8}$/.test(whatsappNumber)
  ) {
    return fail(
      "Enter a valid Bangladesh WhatsApp number starting with 01 or 8801.",
    );
  }

  if (facebookPageUrl === null) {
    return fail(
      "Enter a complete HTTPS Facebook page link, or leave it empty.",
    );
  }

  if (
    supportEmail &&
    (
      supportEmail.length > 254 ||
      !/^[^\s@<>]+@[^\s@<>]+\.[^\s@<>]+$/.test(supportEmail)
    )
  ) {
    return fail("Enter a valid support email or leave it empty.");
  }

  if (!dhakaDeliveryCharge || !outsideDeliveryCharge) {
    return fail(
      "Enter valid delivery charges from 0 to 1000000, with up to 2 decimal places.",
    );
  }

  const data = {
    storeName,
    supportPhone: supportPhone || null,
    whatsappNumber: whatsappNumber || null,
    facebookPageUrl: facebookPageUrl || null,
    supportEmail: supportEmail || null,
    dhakaDeliveryCharge,
    outsideDeliveryCharge,
    codEnabled,
  };

  try {
    await db.storeSetting.upsert({
      where: { id: "main" },
      create: {
        id: "main",
        ...data,
      },
      update: data,
    });
  } catch {
    return fail("Could not save settings. Please try again.");
  }

  revalidatePath("/admin/settings");
  revalidatePath("/checkout");
  revalidatePath("/", "layout");

  return {
    error: "",
    success: "Store settings saved successfully.",
  };
}

===== \src\app\api\admin\uploads\route.ts =====
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import sharp, { type OutputInfo } from "sharp";
import { getAdmin } from "@/lib/admin-auth";
import { saveMediaImage } from "@/lib/media-storage";

const MAX_FILE_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_REQUEST_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_PIXELS = 40_000_000;

const ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES = new Set([
  "image/jpeg",
  "image/png",
  "image/webp",
]);

class UploadTooLargeError extends Error {}

function isAllowedOrigin(request: Request): boolean {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");
  if (!origin) return true; // Direct/same-origin server-side request

  // Render Environment Variable চেক
  if (process.env.APP_ORIGIN && origin === process.env.APP_ORIGIN) {
    return true;
  }

  try {
    const originUrl = new URL(origin);
    const hostHeader =
      request.headers.get("x-forwarded-host") ??
      request.headers.get("host") ??
      new URL(request.url).host;

    // একই হোস্টের রিকোয়েস্ট হলে এলাউ করবে
    if (originUrl.host === hostHeader) {
      return true;
    }

    // bimlay.com এবং এর সাবডোমেইন এলাউ করতে
    if (
      originUrl.hostname === "bimlay.com" ||
      originUrl.hostname.endsWith(".bimlay.com") ||
      originUrl.hostname === "localhost" ||
      originUrl.hostname === "127.0.0.1" ||
      originUrl.hostname.endsWith(".onrender.com")
    ) {
      return true;
    }
  } catch {
    return false;
  }

  return false;
}

async function readLimitedFormData(request: Request): Promise<FormData> {
  const contentType = request.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";

  if (!contentType.toLowerCase().startsWith("multipart/form-data;")) {
    throw new Error("Invalid upload request.");
  }

  const declaredSize = Number(request.headers.get("content-length") ?? 0);

  if (declaredSize > MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {
    throw new UploadTooLargeError();
  }

  if (!request.body) {
    throw new Error("Missing upload body.");
  }

  const reader = request.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  try {
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();

      if (done) {
        break;
      }

      total += value.byteLength;

      if (total > MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {
        await reader.cancel();
        throw new UploadTooLargeError();
      }

      chunks.push(value);
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }

  const body = new Uint8Array(total);
  let offset = 0;

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    body.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.byteLength;
  }

  return new Response(body.buffer, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": contentType,
    },
  }).formData();
}

export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!isAllowedOrigin(request)) {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Upload origin is not allowed." },
      { status: 403 },
    );
  }

  const admin = await getAdmin();

  if (!admin || admin.role !== "OWNER") {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Owner login is required." },
      { status: 403 },
    );
  }

  let formData: FormData;

  try {
    formData = await readLimitedFormData(request);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof UploadTooLargeError) {
      return Response.json(
        { error: "Upload is too large. Choose an image under 8 MB." },
        { status: 413 },
      );
    }

    return Response.json(
      { error: "Invalid upload request." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const images = formData.getAll("image");
  const file = images[0];

  if (images.length !== 1 || !(file instanceof File)) {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Choose one image." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  if (file.size === 0 || file.size > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Image must be smaller than 8 MB and not empty." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  if (!ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES.has(file.type)) {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Only JPG, PNG and WebP images are supported." },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  let processed: {
    data: Buffer;
    info: OutputInfo;
  };

  try {
    const input = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());

    const metadata = await sharp(input, {
      limitInputPixels: MAX_PIXELS,
    }).metadata();

    if (
      !metadata.format ||
      !["jpeg", "png", "webp"].includes(metadata.format) ||
      !metadata.width ||
      !metadata.height ||
      (metadata.pages ?? 1) > 1
    ) {
      return Response.json(
        { error: "Choose a valid, non-animated JPG, PNG or WebP image." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    processed = await sharp(input, {
      limitInputPixels: MAX_PIXELS,
    })
      .rotate()
      .resize({
        width: 1600,
        height: 1600,
        fit: "inside",
        withoutEnlargement: true,
      })
      .webp({ quality: 85 })
      .toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });
  } catch {
    return Response.json(
      {
        error:
          "This image could not be processed. Choose a valid image under 40 megapixels.",
      },
      { status: 400 },
    );
  }

  const filename = `${randomUUID()}.webp`;

  try {
    await saveMediaImage(filename, processed.data);
  } catch {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Could not save the image. Please try again." },
      { status: 500 },
    );
  }

  return Response.json(
    {
      url: `/media/${filename}`,
      width: processed.info.width,
      height: processed.info.height,
      bytes: processed.info.size,
    },
    {
      status: 201,
      headers: {
        "Cache-Control": "no-store",
      },
    },
  );
}


===== \src\app\api\search\suggestions\route.ts =====
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  await connection();

  const query = new URL(request.url).searchParams
    .get("q")
    ?.trim()
    .slice(0, 100);

  if (!query || query.length < 2) {
    return Response.json(
      { items: [] },
      { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
    );
  }

  const products = await db.product.findMany({
    where: {
      status: "ACTIVE",
      category: {
        is: {
          isActive: true,
          OR: [
            { parentId: null },
            { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
          ],
        },
      },
      images: { some: {} },
      variants: { some: { isActive: true } },
      OR: [
        {
          name: {
            contains: query,
            mode: "insensitive",
          },
        },
        {
          variants: {
            some: {
              isActive: true,
              sku: {
                contains: query,
                mode: "insensitive",
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }],
    take: 6,
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      slug: true,
      images: {
        orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
        take: 1,
        select: { url: true, alt: true },
      },
      variants: {
        where: { isActive: true },
        orderBy: { price: "asc" },
        select: { price: true, stock: true },
      },
    },
  });

  return Response.json(
    {
      items: products.map((product) => {
        const variant =
          product.variants.find((item) => item.stock > 0) ??
          product.variants[0];

        return {
          id: product.id,
          name: product.name,
          slug: product.slug,
          image: product.images[0].url,
          imageAlt: product.images[0].alt,
          price: variant.price.toFixed(2),
          inStock: product.variants.some(
            (item) => item.stock > 0,
          ),
        };
      }),
    },
    { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
  );
}


===== \src\app\api\store\contact\route.ts =====
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

export async function GET() {
  await connection();

  try {
    const settings = await db.storeSetting.findUnique({
      where: { id: "main" },
      select: {
        whatsappNumber: true,
        facebookPageUrl: true,
        supportEmail: true,
      },
    });

    return Response.json(
      {
        whatsappNumber: settings?.whatsappNumber ?? "",
        facebookPageUrl: settings?.facebookPageUrl ?? "",
        supportEmail: settings?.supportEmail ?? "",
      },
      {
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control": "no-store",
          "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
        },
      },
    );
  } catch {
    return Response.json(
      { error: "Contact information is temporarily unavailable." },
      {
        status: 503,
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control": "no-store",
        },
      },
    );
  }
}

===== \src\app\shop\page.tsx =====
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import Storefront from "@/components/storefront";
import {
  getShopFilterOptions,
  getStoreCategories,
  getStoreProducts,
  type ShopSort,
} from "@/lib/catalog";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Shop",
  description: "Explore BIMLAY fashion, accessories and lifestyle essentials.",
};

type SearchValues = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;

function single(value: string | string[] | undefined) {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : "";
}

function priceValue(value: string): number | undefined {
  if (!/^\d{1,7}(?:\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(value)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const price = Number(value);
  return price <= 1000000 ? price : undefined;
}

async function ShopContent({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<SearchValues>;
}) {
  await connection();

  const params = await searchParams;

  const query = single(params.q).trim().slice(0, 100);
  const categorySlug = single(params.category).trim();
  const rawPage = single(params.page);
  const pageNumber = Number(rawPage || "1");

  const page =
    Number.isSafeInteger(pageNumber) && pageNumber > 0 ? pageNumber : 1;

  const minPrice = priceValue(single(params.minPrice));
  const maxPrice = priceValue(single(params.maxPrice));
  const size = single(params.size).trim().slice(0, 40);
  const color = single(params.color).trim().slice(0, 60);
  const inStockOnly = single(params.inStock) === "1";

  const requestedSort = single(params.sort);
  const sort: ShopSort =
    requestedSort === "price_asc" || requestedSort === "price_desc"
      ? requestedSort
      : "newest";

  const [categories, filterOptions] = await Promise.all([
    getStoreCategories(),
    getShopFilterOptions(),
  ]);

  const category = categorySlug
    ? categories.find((item) => item.slug === categorySlug)
    : undefined;

  if (categorySlug && !category) {
    notFound();
  }

  const catalog = await getStoreProducts({
    query,
    categoryId: category?.id,
    page,
    minPrice,
    maxPrice:
      minPrice !== undefined && maxPrice !== undefined && maxPrice < minPrice
        ? undefined
        : maxPrice,
    size,
    color,
    inStockOnly,
    sort,
  });

  return (
    <Storefront
      mode="shop"
      filterSizes={filterOptions.sizes}
      filterColors={filterOptions.colors}
      minPrice={minPrice?.toString() ?? ""}
      maxPrice={maxPrice?.toString() ?? ""}
      selectedSize={size}
      selectedColor={color}
      inStockOnly={inStockOnly}
      sort={sort}
      categories={categories}
      products={catalog.products}
      query={query}
      selectedCategory={categorySlug}
      total={catalog.total}
      page={catalog.currentPage}
      totalPages={catalog.totalPages}
    />
  );
}

export default function ShopPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<SearchValues>;
}) {
  return (
    <Suspense
      fallback={<div className="store-loading">Loading the collection...</div>}
    >
      <ShopContent searchParams={searchParams} />
    </Suspense>
  );
}
