===== \src\components\cart-page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { ShoppingBag, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";

import {
  quoteCart,
  validateCartItem,
  type CartQuoteResult,
  type CartLine,
} from "@/app/actions/cart";

import {
  readCartItems,
  removeCartItem,
  setCartQuantity,
  useCartSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/cart-store";

function money(value: string) {
  return `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value))}`;
}

function QuantityEditor({
  line,
  disabled,
  onUpdate,
}: {
  line: CartLine;
  disabled: boolean;
  onUpdate: (variantId: string, quantity: number) => void;
}) {
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(String(line.quantity));

  return (
    <form
      className="cart-quantity-form"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        onUpdate(line.variantId, Number(quantity));
      }}
    >
      <label>
        <span className="cart-small-label">Quantity</span>
        <input
          type="number"
          min={1}
          max={99}
          step={1}
          value={quantity}
          disabled={disabled}
          onChange={(event) => setQuantity(event.target.value)}
          aria-label={`Quantity for ${line.name}`}
          required
        />
      </label>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={disabled}
        className="product-small-button"
      >
        Update
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

export default function CartPageContent() {
  const snapshot = useCartSnapshot();

  const [reply, setReply] = useState<{
    snapshot: string;
    result: CartQuoteResult;
  } | null>(null);

  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadCart() {
      try {
        const result = await quoteCart(readCartItems(snapshot));

        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({ snapshot, result });
        }
      } catch {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({
            snapshot,
            result: {
              ok: false,
              error: "Could not load your bag. Please try again.",
            },
          });
        }
      }
    }

    void loadCart();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [snapshot, refreshKey]);

  const loading = reply?.snapshot !== snapshot;

  const quote = reply?.result.ok ? reply.result : null;

  const loadError = reply && !reply.result.ok ? reply.result.error : "";

  function updateQuantity(variantId: string, quantity: number) {
    setError("");

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const result = await validateCartItem(variantId, quantity);

        if (!result.ok) {
          setError(result.error);
          setRefreshKey((current) => current + 1);
          return;
        }

        setCartQuantity(result.variantId, result.quantity);
      } catch {
        setError("Could not update your bag. Please try again.");
      }
    });
  }

  function removeItem(variantId: string) {
    setError("");

    try {
      removeCartItem(variantId);
    } catch {
      setError("Could not remove the item. Please try again.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="cart-page shell">
      <div className="cart-heading">
        <p className="eyebrow">Your selection</p>
        <h1>Shopping bag</h1>
        <p>Review your pieces before completing your order.</p>
      </div>

      <div aria-live="polite">
        {error && <p className="admin-form-error cart-message">{error}</p>}

        {!loading && loadError && (
          <div className="cart-load-error">
            <p className="admin-form-error">{loadError}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="product-small-button"
              onClick={() => setRefreshKey((current) => current + 1)}
            >
              Try again
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {loading && (
        <p className="cart-updating" role="status">
          Checking current prices and availability...
        </p>
      )}

      {!loading && quote?.lines.length === 0 && (
        <div className="cart-empty">
          <ShoppingBag size={38} strokeWidth={1.2} />
          <h2>Your bag is waiting.</h2>
          <p>Discover something for your everyday.</p>

          <Link href="/shop" className="primary-button">
            Explore the collection
          </Link>
        </div>
      )}

      {quote && quote.lines.length > 0 && (
        <div
          className={`cart-layout ${loading ? "is-updating" : ""}`}
          aria-busy={loading || pending}
        >
          <div className="cart-items">
            {quote.lines.map((line) => (
              <article className="cart-item" key={line.variantId}>
                <div className="cart-item-image">
                  {line.image ? (
                    <Image
                      src={line.image}
                      alt={line.imageAlt}
                      width={130}
                      height={160}
                      unoptimized
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <ShoppingBag size={30} strokeWidth={1.2} />
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="cart-item-information">
                  <h2>
                    {line.slug ? (
                      <Link href={`/products/${line.slug}`}>{line.name}</Link>
                    ) : (
                      line.name
                    )}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="cart-option">{line.option}</p>

                  {line.unitPrice && (
                    <p className="cart-unit-price">
                      {money(line.unitPrice)} each
                    </p>
                  )}

                  {line.issue && (
                    <p className="cart-item-issue">{line.issue}</p>
                  )}

                  {line.slug && line.stock > 0 && (
                    <QuantityEditor
                      key={`${line.variantId}-${line.quantity}`}
                      line={line}
                      disabled={loading || pending}
                      onUpdate={updateQuantity}
                    />
                  )}

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="cart-remove"
                    disabled={loading || pending}
                    onClick={() => removeItem(line.variantId)}
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={14} />
                    Remove
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="cart-line-total">
                  {line.lineTotal ? money(line.lineTotal) : "Unavailable"}
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>

          <aside className="cart-summary">
            <p className="eyebrow">Order summary</p>
            <h2>Your everyday edit.</h2>

            <div className="cart-summary-row">
              <span>
                {quote.hasIssues ? "Available items subtotal" : "Subtotal"}
              </span>
              <strong>{money(quote.subtotal)}</strong>
            </div>

            <div className="cart-summary-row">
              <span>Delivery</span>
              <span>Calculated at checkout</span>
            </div>

            {quote.hasIssues && (
              <p className="cart-summary-note cart-item-issue">
                Update or remove unavailable items before checkout. Their prices
                are excluded from this subtotal.
              </p>
            )}

            <p className="cart-summary-note">
              Adding an item to your bag does not reserve stock.
            </p>

            {!quote.hasIssues && !loading && !pending && (
              <Link href="/checkout" className="primary-button checkout-submit">
                Proceed to checkout
              </Link>
            )}

            <Link href="/shop" className="text-link">
              Continue shopping
            </Link>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}



===== \src\components\checkout-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { quoteCheckoutCoupon } from "@/app/actions/coupons";
import { useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";

import { quoteCart, type CartQuoteResult } from "@/app/actions/cart";
import { submitCheckout } from "@/app/actions/checkout";

import {
  completeCartOrder,
  readCartItems,
  useCartSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/cart-store";

type Props = {
  dhakaCharge: string;
  outsideCharge: string;
};

const CHECKOUT_SESSION_KEY = "bimlay-checkout-pending-v1";

function checkoutIdentity(): {
  checkoutKey: string;
  receiptSecret: string;
} {
  const existing = window.sessionStorage.getItem(CHECKOUT_SESSION_KEY);

  if (existing) {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(existing);

    if (
      typeof parsed.checkoutKey === "string" &&
      typeof parsed.receiptSecret === "string"
    ) {
      return parsed;
    }

    throw new Error(
      "Invalid checkout session. Please reopen this browser tab.",
    );
  }

  const bytes = window.crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));

  const identity = {
    checkoutKey: window.crypto.randomUUID(),
    receiptSecret: Array.from(bytes)
      .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(""),
  };

  window.sessionStorage.setItem(CHECKOUT_SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(identity));

  return identity;
}

function cents(value: string) {
  const [whole, fraction = ""] = value.split(".");
  return Number(whole) * 100 + Number(fraction.padEnd(2, "0").slice(0, 2));
}

function decimalFromCents(value: number) {
  return `${Math.floor(value / 100)}.${String(value % 100).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function money(value: string) {
  return `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value))}`;
}

export default function CheckoutForm({ dhakaCharge, outsideCharge }: Props) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const snapshot = useCartSnapshot();

  const [reply, setReply] = useState<{
    snapshot: string;
    result: CartQuoteResult;
  } | null>(null);

  const [zone, setZone] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const [couponCode, setCouponCode] = useState("");
  const [couponMessage, setCouponMessage] = useState("");
  const [appliedCoupon, setAppliedCoupon] = useState<{
    snapshot: string;
    code: string;
    subtotal: string;
    discount: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [couponPending, startCouponTransition] = useTransition();

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    quoteCart(readCartItems(snapshot))
      .then((result) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({ snapshot, result });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({
            snapshot,
            result: {
              ok: false,
              error: "Could not load checkout. Refresh this page.",
            },
          });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [snapshot]);

  if (!reply || reply.snapshot !== snapshot) {
    return <div className="store-loading">Checking your order...</div>;
  }

  if (!reply.result.ok) {
    return (
      <div className="shell checkout-page">
        <p className="admin-form-error">{reply.result.error}</p>
        <Link href="/cart" className="text-link">
          Return to bag
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const quote = reply.result;
  const activeCoupon =
    appliedCoupon?.snapshot === snapshot &&
    appliedCoupon.subtotal === quote.subtotal
      ? appliedCoupon
      : null;

  if (quote.lines.length === 0 || quote.hasIssues) {
    return (
      <div className="shell checkout-page">
        <h1>Review your shopping bag.</h1>
        <p className="category-help">
          {quote.lines.length === 0
            ? "Add an item before proceeding to checkout."
            : "Update or remove unavailable items before checkout."}
        </p>
        <Link href="/cart" className="primary-button">
          Return to bag
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const shipping =
    zone === "DHAKA"
      ? dhakaCharge
      : zone === "OUTSIDE_DHAKA"
        ? outsideCharge
        : null;

  const total = decimalFromCents(
    cents(quote.subtotal) -
      (activeCoupon ? cents(activeCoupon.discount) : 0) +
      (shipping ? cents(shipping) : 0),
  );

  function applyCoupon() {
    const requestedCode = couponCode.trim();

    if (!requestedCode) {
      setCouponMessage("Enter a coupon code.");
      return;
    }

    setCouponMessage("");
    setAppliedCoupon(null);

    startCouponTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const result = await quoteCheckoutCoupon(
          readCartItems(snapshot),
          requestedCode,
        );

        if (!result.ok) {
          setCouponMessage(result.error);
          return;
        }

        setAppliedCoupon({
          snapshot,
          code: result.code,
          subtotal: result.subtotal,
          discount: result.discount,
        });
        setCouponCode(result.code);
        setCouponMessage(`${result.code} applied successfully.`);
      } catch {
        setCouponMessage("Could not check this coupon. Please try again.");
      }
    });
  }

  function placeOrder(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    setError("");

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const identity = checkoutIdentity();

        const result = await submitCheckout({
          ...identity,
          customerName: formData.get("customerName"),
          phone: formData.get("phone"),
          email: formData.get("email"),
          district: formData.get("district"),
          address: formData.get("address"),
          notes: formData.get("notes"),
          deliveryZone: zone,
          expectedTotal: total,
          couponCode: activeCoupon?.code ?? "",
          items: readCartItems(snapshot),
        });

        if (!result.ok) {
          setError(result.error);
          return;
        }

        // A committed order must still open its receipt,
        // even if browser storage cleanup fails.
        try {
          completeCartOrder(result.orderId, result.items);

          window.sessionStorage.removeItem(CHECKOUT_SESSION_KEY);
        } catch {
          // Keep the checkout identity for a safe retry.
        }

        router.push(`/order-confirmation/${result.orderId}`);
      } catch {
        setError("Checkout could not finish. Please retry from this page.");
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <section className="shell checkout-page">
      <div className="cart-heading">
        <p className="eyebrow">Complete your selection</p>
        <h1>Checkout</h1>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={placeOrder} className="checkout-layout">
        <fieldset disabled={pending} className="checkout-fields">
          <h2>Delivery details</h2>

          <div className="category-form-grid">
            <label>
              <span>Full name</span>
              <input
                name="customerName"
                autoComplete="name"
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={100}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Mobile number</span>
              <input
                name="phone"
                type="tel"
                autoComplete="tel"
                placeholder="01XXXXXXXXX"
                maxLength={25}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>Email — optional</span>
              <input
                name="email"
                type="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                maxLength={254}
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              <span>District</span>
              <input
                name="district"
                autoComplete="address-level2"
                minLength={2}
                maxLength={80}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Complete delivery address</span>
              <textarea
                name="address"
                autoComplete="street-address"
                placeholder="House, road, area, thana and other delivery details"
                rows={4}
                minLength={10}
                maxLength={500}
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Delivery area</span>
              <select
                value={zone}
                onChange={(event) => setZone(event.target.value)}
                required
              >
                <option value="" disabled>
                  Choose delivery area
                </option>
                <option value="DHAKA">
                  Inside Dhaka — {money(dhakaCharge)}
                </option>
                <option value="OUTSIDE_DHAKA">
                  Outside Dhaka — {money(outsideCharge)}
                </option>
              </select>
            </label>

            <label className="category-full-width">
              <span>Order notes — optional</span>
              <textarea
                name="notes"
                rows={3}
                maxLength={500}
                placeholder="Any helpful delivery instructions"
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        </fieldset>

        <aside className="cart-summary checkout-summary">
          <p className="eyebrow">Your order</p>
          <h2>Order summary</h2>

          {quote.lines.map((line) => (
            <div className="checkout-order-line" key={line.variantId}>
              <div>
                <strong>{line.name}</strong>
                <p>
                  {line.option} · Qty {line.quantity}
                </p>
              </div>
              <span>{money(line.lineTotal!)}</span>
            </div>
          ))}

          <div className="cart-summary-row">
            <span>Subtotal</span>
            <strong>{money(quote.subtotal)}</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="checkout-coupon">
            <label htmlFor="checkout-coupon-code">Coupon code</label>

            <div className="checkout-coupon-controls">
              <input
                id="checkout-coupon-code"
                type="text"
                value={couponCode}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setCouponCode(event.target.value);
                  setAppliedCoupon(null);
                  setCouponMessage("");
                }}
                onKeyDown={(event) => {
                  if (event.key === "Enter") {
                    event.preventDefault();
                    applyCoupon();
                  }
                }}
                maxLength={32}
                autoComplete="off"
                placeholder="Enter code"
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={applyCoupon}
                disabled={pending || couponPending || !couponCode.trim()}
              >
                {couponPending ? "Checking..." : "Apply"}
              </button>
            </div>

            {activeCoupon && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="checkout-coupon-remove"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAppliedCoupon(null);
                  setCouponCode("");
                  setCouponMessage("");
                }}
                disabled={pending || couponPending}
              >
                Remove coupon
              </button>
            )}

            <p className="checkout-coupon-message" role="status">
              {couponMessage}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="cart-summary-row">
            <span>Delivery</span>
            <span>{shipping ? money(shipping) : "Choose area"}</span>
          </div>

          {activeCoupon && (
            <div className="cart-summary-row checkout-discount">
              <span>Coupon ({activeCoupon.code})</span>
              <strong>−{money(activeCoupon.discount)}</strong>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="cart-summary-row checkout-total">
            <strong>Total</strong>
            <strong>{money(total)}</strong>
          </div>

          <div className="checkout-payment">
            <strong>Cash on Delivery</strong>
            <p>Pay when your order arrives.</p>
          </div>

          <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
            {error && <p className="admin-form-error cart-message">{error}</p>}
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="primary-button checkout-submit"
            disabled={pending || couponPending || !zone}
          >
            {pending ? "Placing order..." : "Place order"}
          </button>
        </aside>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}

===== \src\components\flash-timer.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "node_modules/@types/react"; // অথবা শুধু 'react'

export default function FlashTimer({ endTime }: { endTime: string }) {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState({
    hours: 0,
    minutes: 0,
    seconds: 0,
    isExpired: false,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const targetTime = new Date(endTime).getTime();

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date().getTime();
      const difference = targetTime - now;

      if (difference <= 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        setTimeLeft({ hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0, isExpired: true });
      } else {
        const hours = Math.floor((difference / (1000 * 60 * 60)));
        const minutes = Math.floor((difference % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
        const seconds = Math.floor((difference % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);

        setTimeLeft({ hours, minutes, seconds, isExpired: false });
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [endTime]);

  if (timeLeft.isExpired) {
    return <span style={{ color: "red", fontWeight: "bold" }}>Flash Sale Ended</span>;
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: "0.5rem", alignItems: "center", background: "#fee2e2", padding: "0.5rem 1rem", borderRadius: "6px", color: "#991b1b", width: "fit-content" }}>
      <span style={{ fontSize: "0.85rem", fontWeight: "600" }}>Limited Time Deal:</span>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace", fontWeight: "bold" }}>
        {String(timeLeft.hours).padStart(2, "0")}h : {String(timeLeft.minutes).padStart(2, "0")}m : {String(timeLeft.seconds).padStart(2, "0")}s
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== \src\components\floating-actions.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import {
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type PointerEvent,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { ShoppingBag } from "lucide-react";
import { useCartCount } from "@/lib/cart-store";

type Position = {
  x: number;
  y: number;
};

type DragState = {
  pointerId: number;
  startX: number;
  startY: number;
  left: number;
  top: number;
};

function keepInsideScreen(
  position: Position,
  width: number,
  height: number,
): Position {
  const padding = 12;
  const bottomSpace = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches
    ? 90
    : 16;

  return {
    x: Math.max(
      padding,
      Math.min(position.x, window.innerWidth - width - padding),
    ),
    y: Math.max(
      padding,
      Math.min(position.y, window.innerHeight - height - bottomSpace),
    ),
  };
}

function DraggableAction({
  href,
  label,
  variant,
  external = false,
  children,
}: {
  href: string;
  label: string;
  variant: "cart" | "whatsapp";
  external?: boolean;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const elementRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
  const dragRef = useRef<DragState | null>(null);
  const blockClickRef = useRef(false);

  const [position, setPosition] = useState<Position | null>(null);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleResize() {
      const element = elementRef.current;
      if (!element) return;

      const { width, height } = element.getBoundingClientRect();

      setPosition((current) =>
        current ? keepInsideScreen(current, width, height) : current,
      );
    }

    window.addEventListener("resize", handleResize);

    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", handleResize);
  }, []);

  function startDrag(event: PointerEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
    if (!event.isPrimary || event.button !== 0) return;

    const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();

    blockClickRef.current = false;

    dragRef.current = {
      pointerId: event.pointerId,
      startX: event.clientX,
      startY: event.clientY,
      left: rect.left,
      top: rect.top,
    };

    event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  }

  function moveDrag(event: PointerEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
    const drag = dragRef.current;

    if (!drag || drag.pointerId !== event.pointerId) return;

    const dx = event.clientX - drag.startX;
    const dy = event.clientY - drag.startY;

    // Small finger movements still count as a normal tap.
    if (!blockClickRef.current && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 8) return;

    blockClickRef.current = true;
    setDragging(true);

    const { width, height } = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();

    setPosition(
      keepInsideScreen(
        {
          x: drag.left + dx,
          y: drag.top + dy,
        },
        width,
        height,
      ),
    );
  }

  function finishDrag(event: PointerEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) {
    if (dragRef.current?.pointerId !== event.pointerId) return;

    dragRef.current = null;
    setDragging(false);

    if (event.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) {
      event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Link
      ref={elementRef}
      href={href}
      prefetch={false}
      target={external ? "_blank" : undefined}
      rel={external ? "noopener noreferrer" : undefined}
      aria-label={label}
      title={`${label} — ধরে সরাতে পারবে`}
      draggable={false}
      className={[
        "bimlay-floating-action",
        `bimlay-floating-${variant}`,
        dragging ? "is-dragging" : "",
      ].join(" ")}
      style={
        position
          ? {
              left: position.x,
              top: position.y,
              right: "auto",
              bottom: "auto",
            }
          : undefined
      }
      onPointerDown={startDrag}
      onPointerMove={moveDrag}
      onPointerUp={finishDrag}
      onPointerCancel={finishDrag}
      onLostPointerCapture={() => {
        dragRef.current = null;
        setDragging(false);
      }}
      onDragStart={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
      onClick={(event) => {
        if (blockClickRef.current) {
          event.preventDefault();
          event.stopPropagation();
        }
      }}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if (event.key === "Enter") {
          blockClickRef.current = false;
          return;
        }

        // Home restores the original position.
        if (event.key === "Home") {
          event.preventDefault();
          setPosition(null);
          return;
        }

        const directions: Record<string, Position> = {
          ArrowLeft: { x: -16, y: 0 },
          ArrowRight: { x: 16, y: 0 },
          ArrowUp: { x: 0, y: -16 },
          ArrowDown: { x: 0, y: 16 },
        };

        const direction = directions[event.key];
        if (!direction) return;

        event.preventDefault();

        const rect = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();

        setPosition(
          keepInsideScreen(
            {
              x: rect.left + direction.x,
              y: rect.top + direction.y,
            },
            rect.width,
            rect.height,
          ),
        );
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
}

function WhatsAppIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 32 32"
      width="30"
      height="30"
      fill="none"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <path
        d="M27.5 15.5a11.5 11.5 0 0 1-17.1 10L4 27l1.6-6.1A11.5 11.5 0 1 1 27.5 15.5Z"
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth="2"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      />
      <path
        d="m11.5 9.5 2 4-1.5 1.5c1.1 2.2 2.8 3.9 5 5l1.5-1.5 4 2c-.5 2-1.8 3-3.5 2.5-5.8-1.5-9.5-5.2-11-11-.5-1.7.5-3 2.5-3Z"
        fill="currentColor"
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default function FloatingActions({
  whatsappNumber,
}: {
  whatsappNumber: string;
}) {
  const cartCount = useCartCount();
  const digits = whatsappNumber.replace(/\D/g, "");

  const whatsappEnabled = /^8801[3-9]\d{8}$/.test(digits);

  const whatsappHref = whatsappEnabled
    ? `https://wa.me/${digits}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
        "Hello BIMLAY! আপনাদের পণ্য সম্পর্কে জানতে চাই।",
      )}`
    : "";

  return (
    <>
      {whatsappEnabled && (
        <DraggableAction
          href={whatsappHref}
          label="WhatsApp-এ BIMLAY-এর সঙ্গে কথা বলুন"
          variant="whatsapp"
          external
        >
          <WhatsAppIcon />
        </DraggableAction>
      )}

      <DraggableAction
        href="/cart"
        label={`Shopping bag, ${cartCount} items`}
        variant="cart"
      >
        <ShoppingBag size={25} strokeWidth={1.6} />

        {cartCount > 0 && (
          <span className="bimlay-floating-count" aria-hidden="true">
            {cartCount > 99 ? "99+" : cartCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </DraggableAction>
    </>
  );
}


===== \src\components\hero-slider.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Pause, Play } from "lucide-react";
import type { HeroSlideData } from "@/lib/hero-slides";

export default function HeroSlider({
  slides,
}: {
  slides: HeroSlideData[];
}) {

  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [autoPlay, setAutoPlay] = useState(true);
  const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);

  const touchStart = useRef<{
    x: number;
    y: number;
  } | null>(null);

  const activeIndex = slides.length
    ? index % slides.length
    : 0;

  const slide = slides[activeIndex];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!autoPlay || hovered || slides.length < 2) {
      return;
    }

    const motionPreference = window.matchMedia(
      "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)",
    );

    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (
        document.visibilityState !== "visible" ||
        motionPreference.matches
      ) {
        return;
      }

      setIndex((current) => (current + 1) % slides.length);
    }, 6000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [autoPlay, hovered, slides.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (slides.length < 2) return;

    const next = slides[(activeIndex + 1) % slides.length];
    const mobile = window.matchMedia(
      "(max-width: 640px)",
    ).matches;

    const preload = new window.Image();

    preload.src =
      mobile && next.mobileImage
        ? next.mobileImage
        : next.desktopImage;
  }, [activeIndex, slides]);

  function goTo(target: number) {
    if (slides.length === 0) return;

    setAutoPlay(false);
    setIndex(
      (target + slides.length) % slides.length,
    );
  }

if (!slide) return null;
  return (
    <section
      className="hero hero-slider"
      aria-label="Featured collections"
      aria-roledescription="carousel"
      onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(false)}
      onFocusCapture={(event) => {
        const target = event.target as HTMLElement;

        if (!target.closest("[data-carousel-rotation]")) {
          setAutoPlay(false);
        }
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="hero-slide-content"
        aria-live={autoPlay ? "off" : "polite"}
        aria-atomic="true"
      >
        {slide.eyebrow && (
          <p className="eyebrow">{slide.eyebrow}</p>
        )}

        <h1 className="hero-slider-title">{slide.title}</h1>

        <div
          className="hero-art hero-slider-art"
          role="group"
          aria-roledescription="slide"
          aria-label={`${activeIndex + 1} of ${slides.length}`}
          onTouchStart={(event) => {
            const touch = event.touches[0];

            touchStart.current = {
              x: touch.clientX,
              y: touch.clientY,
            };
          }}
          onTouchEnd={(event) => {
            const start = touchStart.current;
            const end = event.changedTouches[0];
            touchStart.current = null;

            if (!start || !end) return;

            const dx = end.clientX - start.x;
            const dy = end.clientY - start.y;

            if (
              Math.abs(dx) > 50 &&
              Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) * 1.4
            ) {
              goTo(activeIndex + (dx < 0 ? 1 : -1));
            }
          }}
        >
          <picture key={slide.id}>
            {slide.mobileImage && (
              <source
                media="(max-width: 640px)"
                srcSet={slide.mobileImage}
              />
            )}

            <Image
              src={slide.desktopImage}
              alt={slide.altText}
              width={1600}
              height={650}
              className="hero-slider-image"
              unoptimized
              loading="eager"
              fetchPriority={activeIndex === 0 ? "high" : "auto"}
            />
          </picture>

          {slides.length > 1 && (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="hero-arrow hero-arrow-previous"
                aria-label="Previous banner"
                onClick={() => goTo(activeIndex - 1)}
              >
                <ArrowLeft size={20} />
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="hero-arrow hero-arrow-next"
                aria-label="Next banner"
                onClick={() => goTo(activeIndex + 1)}
              >
                <ArrowRight size={20} />
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        {slide.subtitle && (
          <p className="hero-slider-description shell">
            {slide.subtitle}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      {slides.length > 1 && (
        <div className="hero-slider-controls">
          <div className="hero-slider-dots" aria-label="Choose banner">
            {slides.map((item, position) => (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                className={
                  position === activeIndex ? "selected" : ""
                }
                aria-label={`Show banner ${position + 1}: ${item.title}`}
                aria-current={
                  position === activeIndex ? "true" : undefined
                }
                onClick={() => goTo(position)}
              >
                <span />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="hero-rotation-button"
            data-carousel-rotation
            aria-label={
              autoPlay
                ? "Pause automatic banners"
                : "Start automatic banners"
            }
            onClick={() => setAutoPlay((current) => !current)}
          >
            {autoPlay ? <Pause size={15} /> : <Play size={15} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\components\home-sections.tsx =====
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { HomeSectionData } from "@/lib/home-sections";

function SectionImage({
  section,
  sizes,
}: {
  section: HomeSectionData;
  sizes: string;
}) {
  if (!section.imageUrl) return null;

  return (
    <picture className="home-section-picture">
      {section.mobileImageUrl && (
        <source
          media="(max-width: 640px)"
          srcSet={section.mobileImageUrl}
        />
      )}

      <Image
        src={section.imageUrl}
        alt={section.altText || section.title}
        fill
        unoptimized
        sizes={sizes}
      />
    </picture>
  );
}

export function HomeCampaigns({
  sections,
}: {
  sections: HomeSectionData[];
}) {
  const campaigns = ["FOR_HIM", "FOR_HER"]
    .map((key) =>
      sections.find(
        (section) =>
          section.key === key &&
          section.isActive &&
          section.imageUrl,
      ),
    )
    .filter(
      (section): section is HomeSectionData =>
        section !== undefined,
    );

  if (campaigns.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section
      className={`campaigns shell ${
        campaigns.length === 1 ? "single-campaign" : ""
      }`}
      aria-label="Featured collections"
    >
      {campaigns.map((section) => (
        <article className="campaign" key={section.key}>
          <SectionImage
            section={section}
            sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 50vw"
          />

          <div className="campaign-copy">
            {section.eyebrow && (
              <p className="eyebrow">{section.eyebrow}</p>
            )}

            <h2>{section.title}</h2>

            {section.description && (
              <p>{section.description}</p>
            )}

            <Link
              href={section.buttonHref}
              className="text-link"
            >
              {section.buttonText}
              <ArrowRight size={15} />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </article>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

export function HomeStory({
  sections,
}: {
  sections: HomeSectionData[];
}) {
  const section = sections.find(
    (item) =>
      item.key === "STORY" &&
      item.isActive &&
      item.imageUrl,
  );

  if (!section) return null;

  return (
    <section id="story" className="story">
      <div className="story-image">
        <SectionImage
          section={section}
          sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 50vw"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="story-copy">
        {section.eyebrow && (
          <p className="eyebrow">{section.eyebrow}</p>
        )}

        <h2>{section.title}</h2>

        {section.description && (
          <p className="home-story-description">
            {section.description}
          </p>
        )}

        <Link href={section.buttonHref} className="text-link">
          {section.buttonText}
          <ArrowRight size={15} />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\components\live-search.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

type Suggestion = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
  image: string;
  imageAlt: string;
  price: string;
  inStock: boolean;
};

export default function LiveSearch({
  initialQuery = "",
}: {
  initialQuery?: string;
}) {
  const [term, setTerm] = useState(initialQuery);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Suggestion[]>([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<
    "idle" | "loading" | "ready" | "error"
  >("idle");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);
  const [loadedQuery, setLoadedQuery] = useState("");
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const searchQuery = term.trim();
  const showResults = open && searchQuery.length >= 2;
  const currentResults =
    loadedQuery === searchQuery ? items : [];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (searchQuery.length < 2) return;

    const controller = new AbortController();

    const timer = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `/api/search/suggestions?q=${encodeURIComponent(searchQuery)}`,
          {
            signal: controller.signal,
            cache: "no-store",
          },
        );

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error("Search failed");
        }

        const data: { items: Suggestion[] } =
          await response.json();

        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setItems(data.items);
          setLoadedQuery(searchQuery);
          setStatus("ready");
        }
      } catch {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setStatus("error");
        }
      }
    }, 300);

    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [searchQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function closeOutside(event: PointerEvent) {
      if (
        event.target instanceof Node &&
        !containerRef.current?.contains(event.target)
      ) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", closeOutside);
    return () =>
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", closeOutside);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="live-search-field" ref={containerRef}>
      <input
        name="q"
        type="search"
        value={term}
        onChange={(event) => {
          setTerm(event.target.value);
          setOpen(true);
          setStatus("loading");
        }}
        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
        }}
        placeholder="Search by product name or SKU"
        maxLength={100}
        aria-label="Search by product name or SKU"
        aria-controls="live-search-results"

      />

      {showResults && (
        <div
          id="live-search-results"
          className="live-search-results"
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
        >
          {status === "loading" ||
          loadedQuery !== searchQuery ? (
            <p className="live-search-message">
              Searching...
            </p>
          ) : status === "error" ? (
            <p className="live-search-message">
              Suggestions are temporarily unavailable.
            </p>
          ) : currentResults.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="live-search-message">
              No matching products.
            </p>
          ) : (
            currentResults.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.id}
                href={`/products/${item.slug}`}
                className="live-search-item"
                prefetch={false}
                onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              >
                <Image
                  src={item.image}
                  alt={item.imageAlt}
                  width={52}
                  height={58}
                  unoptimized
                />

                <span className="live-search-item-copy">
                  <strong>{item.name}</strong>
                  <small>
                    ৳ {Number(item.price).toLocaleString("en-BD")}
                    {!item.inStock && " · Out of stock"}
                  </small>
                </span>
              </Link>
            ))
          )}

          <button type="submit" className="live-search-all">
            View all results for “{searchQuery}”
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== \src\components\product-detail.tsx =====
"use client";

import ProductGallery from "@/components/product-gallery";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { Heart, ShoppingBag } from "lucide-react";
import { validateCartItem } from "@/app/actions/cart";
import { getCartQuantity, setCartQuantity } from "@/lib/cart-store";
import ProductShare from "@/components/product-share";
import { addProductReview } from "@/app/actions/reviews";
import FlashTimer from "@/components/flash-timer";
import {
  readWishlistIds,
  toggleWishlist,
  useWishlistSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/wishlist-store";

export type ProductDetailData = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  shortDescription: string;
  category: {
    name: string;
    slug: string;
  };
  images: {
    id: string;
    url: string;
    alt: string;
  }[];
  variants: {
    id: string;
    sku: string;
    size: string;
    color: string;
    price: string;
    regularPrice: string | null;
    flashPrice?: string | null;   
    flashSaleEnd?: string | null;
    stock: number;
  }[];
  reviews?: {
    id: string;
    userName: string;
    rating: number;
    comment: string;
    imageUrl: string | null;
    createdAt: string;
  }[];
};

function money(value: string) {
  return `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value))}`;
}

export default function ProductDetail({
  product,
}: {
  product: ProductDetailData;
}) {
  const firstAvailable =
    product.variants.find((variant) => variant.stock > 0) ??
    product.variants[0];

  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState(firstAvailable.id);

  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState("1");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [pending, startTransition] = useTransition();
  const wishlistSnapshot = useWishlistSnapshot();

  const isSaved = readWishlistIds(wishlistSnapshot).includes(product.id);

  const variant =
    product.variants.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) ?? firstAvailable;

  function addToBag() {
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    const requested = Number(quantity);

    if (!Number.isInteger(requested) || requested < 1 || requested > 99) {
      setError("Enter a quantity between 1 and 99.");
      return;
    }

    startTransition(async () => {
      try {
        const totalQuantity = getCartQuantity(variant.id) + requested;

        const result = await validateCartItem(variant.id, totalQuantity);

        if (!result.ok) {
          setError(result.error);
          return;
        }

        setCartQuantity(result.variantId, result.quantity);

        setSuccess(
          `${requested} item${requested > 1 ? "s" : ""} added to your bag.`,
        );
      } catch (error) {
        setError(
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Could not save your bag. Please try again.",
        );
      }
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="product-detail shell">
      <nav className="product-breadcrumb" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
        <Link href="/">Home</Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <Link href="/shop">Shop</Link>
        <span>/</span>
        <Link
          href={`/shop?category=${encodeURIComponent(product.category.slug)}`}
        >
          {product.category.name}
        </Link>
      </nav>

      <div className="product-detail-grid">
        <ProductGallery images={product.images} />

        <section className="detail-information">
          <p className="eyebrow">{product.category.name}</p>
          <h1>{product.name}</h1>
<div className="detail-price">
            {variant.flashPrice && variant.flashSaleEnd && new Date(variant.flashSaleEnd).getTime() > Date.now() ? (
              <div style={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", gap: "0.5rem" }}>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "1rem" }}>
                  <strong style={{ color: "#dc2626", fontSize: "1.5rem" }}>{money(variant.flashPrice)}</strong>
                  <del style={{ color: "#888", fontSize: "1.1rem" }}>{money(variant.price)}</del>
                  <span style={{ background: "#fee2e2", color: "#991b1b", padding: "0.2rem 0.5rem", borderRadius: "4px", fontSize: "0.75rem", fontWeight: "bold" }}>
                    FLASH SALE
                  </span>
                </div>
                <FlashTimer endTime={variant.flashSaleEnd} />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <strong>{money(variant.price)}</strong>
                {variant.regularPrice && <del>{money(variant.regularPrice)}</del>}
              </>
            )}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="detail-wishlist-button"
            aria-pressed={isSaved}
            onClick={() => {
              try {
                toggleWishlist(product.id);
                setError("");
              } catch (error) {
                setError(
                  error instanceof Error
                    ? error.message
                    : "Could not update your wishlist.",
                );
              }
            }}
          >
            <Heart
              size={17}
              fill={isSaved ? "#816334" : "none"}
              color={isSaved ? "#816334" : "currentColor"}
            />
            {isSaved ? "Saved to wishlist" : "Save to wishlist"}
          </button>

          {product.shortDescription && (
            <p className="detail-introduction">{product.shortDescription}</p>
          )}

          <label className="detail-field">
            <span>Choose an option</span>

            <select
              value={variant.id}
              disabled={pending}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSelectedId(event.target.value);
                setQuantity("1");
                setError("");
                setSuccess("");
              }}
            >
              {product.variants.map((item) => (
                <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
                  {[item.size, item.color].filter(Boolean).join(" / ") ||
                    "Standard"}
                  {item.stock === 0 ? " — Out of stock" : ""}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>

          <p className="detail-sku">SKU: {variant.sku}</p>

          <p className={`detail-stock ${variant.stock > 0 ? "available" : ""}`}>
            {variant.stock > 0
              ? `${variant.stock} units available`
              : "Currently out of stock"}
          </p>

          <div className="detail-purchase">
            <label className="detail-field detail-quantity">
              <span>Quantity</span>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                max={Math.max(1, Math.min(variant.stock, 99))}
                step={1}
                value={quantity}
                disabled={pending || variant.stock === 0}
                onChange={(event) => setQuantity(event.target.value)}
              />
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="primary-button"
              disabled={pending || variant.stock === 0}
              onClick={addToBag}
            >
              <ShoppingBag size={17} />
              {pending
                ? "Checking availability..."
                : variant.stock === 0
                  ? "Out of stock"
                  : "Add to bag"}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
            {error && (
              <p className="admin-form-error detail-message">{error}</p>
            )}

            {success && (
              <div className="category-success detail-message">
                <p>{success}</p>

                <Link href="/cart" className="text-link">
                  View bag
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <p className="detail-note">
            Final availability and pricing are confirmed at checkout.
          </p>

          <ProductShare />

          <section className="detail-description">
            <h2>Product details</h2>
            <p>
              {product.description ||
                "Further product details will be added soon."}
            </p>
          </section>

          {/* Product Reviews & Ratings Section */}
          <section
            className="product-reviews-section"
            style={{
              marginTop: "3rem",
              borderTop: "1px solid #eaeaea",
              paddingTop: "2rem",
            }}
          >
            <h2>Customer Reviews &amp; Ratings</h2>

            <div className="reviews-list" style={{ margin: "1.5rem 0" }}>
              {product.reviews && product.reviews.length > 0 ? (
                product.reviews.map((review) => (
                  <div
                    key={review.id}
                    style={{
                      padding: "1rem 0",
                      borderBottom: "1px solid #f0f0f0",
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        display: "flex",
                        justifyContent: "space-between",
                        alignItems: "center",
                      }}
                    >
                      <strong>{review.userName}</strong>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: "0.85rem", color: "#666" }}>
                        {new Date(review.createdAt).toISOString().split("T")[0]}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ color: "#f59e0b", margin: "0.25rem 0" }}>
                      {"★".repeat(review.rating)}
                      {"☆".repeat(5 - review.rating)}
                    </div>
                    {review.comment && (
                      <p style={{ margin: "0.5rem 0" }}>{review.comment}</p>
                    )}
                    {review.imageUrl && (
                      <div style={{ marginTop: "0.5rem" }}>
                        <img
                          src={review.imageUrl}
                          alt="Review attachment"
                          style={{
                            width: "80px",
                            height: "80px",
                            objectFit: "cover",
                            borderRadius: "4px",
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))
              ) : (
                <p style={{ color: "#666" }}>
                  No reviews yet. Be the first to review this product!
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            <div
              className="add-review-form"
              style={{
                background: "#fafafa",
                padding: "1.5rem",
                borderRadius: "8px",
                marginTop: "2rem",
              }}
            >
              <h3>Write a Review</h3>
              <form
                action={addProductReview}
                style={{
                  display: "flex",
                  flexDirection: "column",
                  gap: "1rem",
                  marginTop: "1rem",
                }}
              >
                <input type="hidden" name="productId" value={product.id} />

                <div>
                  <label
                    style={{
                      display: "block",
                      marginBottom: "0.5rem",
                      fontSize: "0.9rem",
                    }}
                  >
                    Your Name
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    name="userName"
                    required
                    placeholder="Enter your name"
                    style={{
                      width: "100%",
                      padding: "0.5rem",
                      border: "1px solid #ccc",
                      borderRadius: "4px",
                    }}
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    style={{
                      display: "block",
                      marginBottom: "0.5rem",
                      fontSize: "0.9rem",
                    }}
                  >
                    Rating (1 to 5)
                  </label>
                  <select
                    name="rating"
                    required
                    style={{
                      width: "100%",
                      padding: "0.5rem",
                      border: "1px solid #ccc",
                      borderRadius: "4px",
                    }}
                  >
                    <option value="5">5 Stars - Excellent</option>
                    <option value="4">4 Stars - Good</option>
                    <option value="3">3 Stars - Average</option>
                    <option value="2">2 Stars - Poor</option>
                    <option value="1">1 Star - Terrible</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    style={{
                      display: "block",
                      marginBottom: "0.5rem",
                      fontSize: "0.9rem",
                    }}
                  >
                    Your Review
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    name="comment"
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="Write your feedback..."
                    style={{
                      width: "100%",
                      padding: "0.5rem",
                      border: "1px solid #ccc",
                      borderRadius: "4px",
                    }}
                  ></textarea>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label
                    style={{
                      display: "block",
                      marginBottom: "0.5rem",
                      fontSize: "0.9rem",
                    }}
                  >
                    Image URL (Optional)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="url"
                    name="imageUrl"
                    placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
                    style={{
                      width: "100%",
                      padding: "0.5rem",
                      border: "1px solid #ccc",
                      borderRadius: "4px",
                    }}
                  />
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="primary-button"
                  style={{
                    alignSelf: "flex-start",
                    padding: "0.5rem 1.5rem",
                    cursor: "pointer",
                  }}
                >
                  Submit Review
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </section>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== \src\components\product-gallery.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";

type ProductImage = {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  alt: string;
};

export default function ProductGallery({
  images,
}: {
  images: ProductImage[];
}) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const dialog = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const image = images[index] ?? images[0];

  if (!image) return null;

  function thumbnails() {
    if (images.length < 2) return null;

    return (
      <div className="detail-thumbnails">
        {images.map((item, position) => (
          <button
            key={item.id}
            type="button"
            className={position === index ? "selected" : ""}
            onClick={() => setIndex(position)}
            aria-label={`View product image ${position + 1}`}
            aria-pressed={position === index}
          >
            <Image
              src={item.url}
              alt={item.alt}
              width={90}
              height={110}
              unoptimized
            />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="Product gallery">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="detail-main-image bimlay-gallery-open"
        aria-label="Open larger product image"
        aria-haspopup="dialog"
        onClick={() => dialog.current?.showModal()}
      >
        <Image
          src={image.url}
          alt={image.alt}
          fill
          unoptimized
          priority
          sizes="(max-width: 760px) 100vw, 50vw"
        />
        <span className="bimlay-gallery-hint">
          Tap to enlarge
        </span>
      </button>

      {thumbnails()}

      <dialog
        ref={dialog}
        className="bimlay-image-dialog"
        aria-label="Enlarged product images"
      >
        <div className="bimlay-image-toolbar">
          <span aria-live="polite">
            Image {index + 1} of {images.length}
          </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => dialog.current?.close()}
          >
            Close ×
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="bimlay-image-preview">
          <Image
            src={image.url}
            alt={image.alt}
            fill
            unoptimized
            sizes="95vw"
          />
        </div>

        {thumbnails()}
      </dialog>
    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\components\product-share.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useRef, useState } from "react";

function productUrl() {
  const canonical = document.querySelector<HTMLLinkElement>(
    'link[rel="canonical"]',
  )?.href;

  return canonical || new URL(
    window.location.pathname,
    window.location.origin,
  ).href;
}

export default function ProductShare() {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [manualUrl, setManualUrl] = useState("");
  const manualInput = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  function openShare(url: string) {
    window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
  }

  async function copyLink() {
    const url = productUrl();

    try {
      if (!navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
        throw new Error("Clipboard unavailable");
      }

      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setManualUrl("");
      setMessage("Product link copied.");
    } catch {
      setManualUrl(url);
      setMessage("Select and copy the link below.");

      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        manualInput.current?.focus();
        manualInput.current?.select();
      });
    }
  }

  async function nativeShare() {
    if (!navigator.share) {
      await copyLink();
      return;
    }

    try {
      await navigator.share({
        title: document.title,
        url: productUrl(),
      });
      setMessage("");
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof DOMException &&
        error.name === "AbortError"
      ) {
        return;
      }

      await copyLink();
    }
  }

  return (
    <section className="product-share" aria-label="Share this product">
      <span className="product-share-label">Share this product</span>

      <div className="product-share-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            const text = `Take a look at this product: ${productUrl()}`;
            openShare(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`);
          }}
        >
          WhatsApp
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => {
            openShare(
              `https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${
                encodeURIComponent(productUrl())
              }`,
            );
          }}
        >
          Facebook
        </button>

        <button type="button" onClick={copyLink}>
          Copy link
        </button>

        <button type="button" onClick={nativeShare}>
          Share
        </button>
      </div>

      <p className="product-share-message" role="status">
        {message}
      </p>

      {manualUrl && (
        <input
          ref={manualInput}
          type="text"
          readOnly
          value={manualUrl}
          aria-label="Product link to copy"
          onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()}
        />
      )}
    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\components\store-contact.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { Mail, MessageCircle } from "lucide-react";
import FloatingActions from "@/components/floating-actions";

type ContactData = {
  whatsappNumber: string;
  facebookPageUrl: string;
  supportEmail: string;
};

function subscribeToMount() {
  return () => {};
}

function clientSnapshot() {
  return true;
}

function serverSnapshot() {
  return false;
}

export default function StoreContact() {
  const mounted = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeToMount,
    clientSnapshot,
    serverSnapshot,
  );

  const [contact, setContact] = useState<ContactData | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    let controller: AbortController | undefined;

    async function loadContact() {
      controller?.abort();
      controller = new AbortController();

      try {
        const response = await fetch("/api/store/contact", {
          cache: "no-store",
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (!response.ok) return;

        const data: unknown = await response.json();

        if (
          !active ||
          !data ||
          typeof data !== "object" ||
          !("whatsappNumber" in data) ||
          !("facebookPageUrl" in data) ||
          !("supportEmail" in data) ||
          typeof data.whatsappNumber !== "string" ||
          typeof data.facebookPageUrl !== "string" ||
          typeof data.supportEmail !== "string"
        ) {
          return;
        }

        setContact({
          whatsappNumber: data.whatsappNumber,
          facebookPageUrl: data.facebookPageUrl,
          supportEmail: data.supportEmail,
        });
      } catch {
        // Keep the cart available even if contacts cannot load.
      }
    }

    void loadContact();

    function refreshContact() {
      void loadContact();
    }

    window.addEventListener("focus", refreshContact);

    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller?.abort();
      window.removeEventListener("focus", refreshContact);
    };
  }, []);

  const whatsappNumber = contact?.whatsappNumber ?? "";
  const whatsappAvailable = /^8801[3-9]\d{8}$/.test(whatsappNumber);

  const hasContacts = Boolean(
    whatsappAvailable || contact?.facebookPageUrl || contact?.supportEmail,
  );

  return (
    <>
      {hasContacts && (
        <div className="bimlay-footer-contact">
          <p className="bimlay-footer-contact-title">Connect with BIMLAY</p>

          <div className="bimlay-footer-contact-links">
            {whatsappAvailable && (
              <a
                href={`https://wa.me/${whatsappNumber}`}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
              >
                <MessageCircle size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>WhatsApp</span>
              </a>
            )}

            {contact?.facebookPageUrl && (
              <a
                href={contact.facebookPageUrl}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
              >
                <svg
                  width="18"
                  height="18"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  fill="currentColor"
                  aria-hidden="true"
                >
                  <path d="M15.5 8H13V6.5c0-.7.3-1 1-1h1.5V3H13c-2.1 0-3.5 1.4-3.5 3.5V8H7v3h2.5v10H13V11h2.2l.3-3Z" />
                </svg>
                <span>Facebook</span>
              </a>
            )}

            {contact?.supportEmail && (
              <a href={`mailto:${contact.supportEmail}`}>
                <Mail size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>{contact.supportEmail}</span>
              </a>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {mounted &&
        createPortal(
          <FloatingActions whatsappNumber={whatsappNumber} />,
          document.body,
        )}
    </>
  );
}



===== \src\components\storefront.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";

import { useCartCount } from "@/lib/cart-store";
import HeroSlider from "@/components/hero-slider";
import type { HeroSlideData } from "@/lib/hero-slides";
import StoreContact from "@/components/store-contact";
import { HomeCampaigns, HomeStory } from "@/components/home-sections";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import LiveSearch from "@/components/live-search";

import type { HomeSectionData } from "@/lib/home-sections";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Grid2X2,
  Heart,
  House,
  Menu,
  Search,
  ShoppingBag,
  UserRound,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  readWishlistIds,
  toggleWishlist,
  useWishlistSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/wishlist-store";

import type { StoreCategory, StoreProduct } from "@/lib/catalog";

type Props = {
  categories: StoreCategory[];
  products: StoreProduct[];
  categoryProducts?: {
    category: StoreCategory;
    products: StoreProduct[];
  }[];
  mode:
    | "home"
    | "shop"
    | "product"
    | "cart"
    | "checkout"
    | "wishlist"
    | "account";
  children?: ReactNode;
  query?: string;
  selectedCategory?: string;
  total?: number;
  page?: number;
  totalPages?: number;
  heroSlides?: HeroSlideData[];
  homeSections?: HomeSectionData[];
  filterSizes?: string[];
  filterColors?: string[];
  minPrice?: string;
  maxPrice?: string;
  selectedSize?: string;
  selectedColor?: string;
  inStockOnly?: boolean;
  sort?: "newest" | "price_asc" | "price_desc";
};

function money(value: string) {
  return `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value))}`;
}

function Brand() {
  return (
    <Link href="/" className="brand brand-original" aria-label="BIMLAY home">
      <Image
        src="/branding/bimlay-logo.png"
        alt="BIMLAY — Fashion & Lifestyle"
        width={256}
        height={256}
        className="original-logo"
        unoptimized
      />
    </Link>
  );
}

export default function Storefront({
  categories,
  products,
  categoryProducts = [],
  mode,
  query = "",
  selectedCategory = "",
  total = 0,
  page = 1,
  totalPages = 1,
  children,
  heroSlides = [],
  homeSections = [],
  filterSizes = [],
  filterColors = [],
  minPrice = "",
  maxPrice = "",
  selectedSize = "",
  selectedColor = "",
  inStockOnly = false,
  sort = "newest",
}: Props) {
  const cartCount = useCartCount();
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);
  const wishlistSnapshot = useWishlistSnapshot();
  const saved = readWishlistIds(wishlistSnapshot);
  const visibleProducts = products;
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");

  const mainCategories = categories.filter(
    (category) => category.parentId === null,
  );

  const categoryCarouselRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const categoryCount = mainCategories.length;

  useEffect(() => {
    const track = categoryCarouselRef.current;

    if (mode !== "home" || !track || categoryCount < 2) return;

    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      if (
        document.visibilityState !== "visible" ||
        window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches ||
        track.matches(":hover") ||
        track.contains(document.activeElement)
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const firstCard = track.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".home-category-card");
      if (!firstCard) return;

      const gap = parseFloat(window.getComputedStyle(track).gap) || 0;
      const step = firstCard.getBoundingClientRect().width + gap;
      const maxScroll = track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth;

      if (maxScroll < 4) return;

      track.scrollTo({
        left:
          track.scrollLeft >= maxScroll - 3
            ? 0
            : Math.min(track.scrollLeft + step, maxScroll),
        behavior: "smooth",
      });
    }, 4500);

    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, [mode, categoryCount]);

  function categoryHref(slug: string) {
    return `/shop?category=${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`;
  }

  function pageHref(targetPage: number) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    if (query) params.set("q", query);
    if (selectedCategory) {
      params.set("category", selectedCategory);
    }
    if (minPrice) params.set("minPrice", minPrice);
    if (maxPrice) params.set("maxPrice", maxPrice);
    if (selectedSize) params.set("size", selectedSize);
    if (selectedColor) params.set("color", selectedColor);
    if (inStockOnly) params.set("inStock", "1");
    if (sort !== "newest") params.set("sort", sort);

    params.set("page", String(targetPage));

    return `/shop?${params.toString()}`;
  }

  function shopCategoryHref(slug = "") {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

    if (query) params.set("q", query);
    if (slug) params.set("category", slug);
    if (minPrice) params.set("minPrice", minPrice);
    if (maxPrice) params.set("maxPrice", maxPrice);
    if (selectedSize) params.set("size", selectedSize);
    if (selectedColor) params.set("color", selectedColor);
    if (inStockOnly) params.set("inStock", "1");
    if (sort !== "newest") params.set("sort", sort);

    const search = params.toString();
    return search ? `/shop?${search}` : "/shop";
  }

  function toggleSaved(id: string) {
    try {
      toggleWishlist(id);
    } catch (error) {
      setNotice(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Could not update your wishlist.",
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="announcement">BIMLAY — A MORE CONSIDERED EVERYDAY</div>

      <header className="site-header">
        <div className="shell header-inner">
          <nav className="desktop-links" aria-label="Shop navigation">
            <Link href="/shop">Shop</Link>

            {mainCategories.slice(0, 4).map((category) => (
              <Link key={category.id} href={categoryHref(category.slug)}>
                {category.name}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-button mobile-menu-button"
            aria-label={menuOpen ? "Close menu" : "Open menu"}
            aria-expanded={menuOpen}
            aria-controls="store-mobile-menu"
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen((current) => !current)}
          >
            {menuOpen ? <X size={23} /> : <Menu size={23} />}
          </button>

          <Brand />

          <div className="header-actions">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="icon-button"
              aria-label="Search products"
              aria-expanded={searchOpen}
              aria-controls="store-search"
              onClick={() => setSearchOpen((current) => !current)}
            >
              <Search size={21} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            </button>

            <Link
              href="/wishlist"
              className="icon-button header-wishlist"
              aria-label={`Wishlist, ${saved.length} saved products`}
            >
              <Heart size={21} strokeWidth={1.5} />

              {saved.length > 0 && (
                <span className="cart-count">{saved.length}</span>
              )}
            </Link>

            <Link
              href="/account"
              className="icon-button header-account"
              aria-label="My account"
            >
              <UserRound size={21} strokeWidth={1.5} />
            </Link>

            <Link
              href="/cart"
              className="icon-button"
              aria-label={`Shopping bag, ${cartCount} items`}
            >
              <ShoppingBag size={21} strokeWidth={1.5} />

              {cartCount > 0 && <span className="cart-count">{cartCount}</span>}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {searchOpen && (
          <form
            id="store-search"
            action="/shop"
            method="get"
            className="store-search shell"
          >
            <LiveSearch initialQuery={query} />

            <button type="submit" className="primary-button">
              Search
            </button>
          </form>
        )}

        {menuOpen && (
          <nav
            id="store-mobile-menu"
            className="mobile-menu"
            aria-label="Mobile shop navigation"
          >
            <Link href="/shop" onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}>
              All products
            </Link>

            {categories.map((category) => (
              <Link
                key={category.id}
                href={categoryHref(category.slug)}
                onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
              >
                {category.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>
        )}
      </header>

      <main>
        {children}
        {mode === "home" && (
          <>
            <HeroSlider slides={heroSlides} />

            {mainCategories.length > 0 && (
              <section
                className="home-category-section shell"
                aria-labelledby="home-category-title"
              >
                <div className="section-heading">
                  <div>
                    <p className="eyebrow">Curated for every day</p>
                    <h2 id="home-category-title">Shop by category</h2>
                  </div>
                  <Link href="/shop" className="text-link">
                    Explore all <ArrowRight size={16} />
                  </Link>
                </div>

                <div
                  className="home-category-grid"
                  ref={categoryCarouselRef}
                  aria-label="Shop by category"
                >
                  {mainCategories.map((category) => (
                    <Link
                      key={category.id}
                      href={categoryHref(category.slug)}
                      className="home-category-card"
                    >
                      <div className="home-category-media">
                        {category.imageUrl ? (
                          <Image
                            src={category.imageUrl}
                            alt={category.name}
                            fill
                            sizes="(max-width: 700px) 50vw, (max-width: 1100px) 33vw, 25vw"
                            unoptimized
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className="home-category-placeholder">
                            BIMLAY
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="home-category-caption">
                        <span>{category.name}</span>
                        <ArrowRight size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
                      </div>
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </section>
            )}

            <HomeCampaigns sections={homeSections} />
          </>
        )}

        {(mode === "home" || mode === "shop") && (
          <section id="products" className="products-section shell">
            <div className="section-heading">
              <div>
                <h2>
                  {mode === "home"
                    ? "Featured Picks"
                    : (categories.find(
                        (category) => category.slug === selectedCategory,
                      )?.name ?? "Shop All Products")}
                </h2>
