                <p>
                  {mode === "home"
                    ? "A Thoughtful Selection For Your Everyday."
                    : `${total} products found`}
                </p>
              </div>

              {mode === "home" && (
                <Link href="/shop" className="text-link">
                  View all <ArrowRight size={14} />
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>

            {mode === "shop" && (
              <div className="shop-tools">
                <nav
                  className="shop-category-tabs"
                  aria-label="Product categories"
                >
                  <Link
                    href={shopCategoryHref()}
                    aria-current={!selectedCategory ? "page" : undefined}
                  >
                    All products
                  </Link>

                  {mainCategories.map((category) => (
                    <Link
                      key={category.id}
                      href={shopCategoryHref(category.slug)}
                      aria-current={
                        selectedCategory === category.slug ? "page" : undefined
                      }
                    >
                      {category.name}
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </nav>
                <details className="shop-filter-panel" open={Boolean(query)}>
                  <summary>Search &amp; filters</summary>

                  <form action="/shop" method="get" className="store-filter">
                    <input
                      name="q"
                      type="search"
                      defaultValue={query}
                      maxLength={100}
                      placeholder="Search products"
                      aria-label="Search products"
                    />

                    <select
                      name="category"
                      defaultValue={selectedCategory}
                      aria-label="Filter by category"
                    >
                      <option value="">All categories</option>
                      {categories.map((category) => (
                        <option key={category.id} value={category.slug}>
                          {category.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>

                    <div className="shop-advanced-filters">
                      <label>
                        <span>Minimum price (৳)</span>
                        <input
                          name="minPrice"
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="1000000"
                          step="0.01"
                          defaultValue={minPrice}
                          placeholder="From"
                        />
                      </label>

                      <label>
                        <span>Maximum price (৳)</span>
                        <input
                          name="maxPrice"
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          max="1000000"
                          step="0.01"
                          defaultValue={maxPrice}
                          placeholder="To"
                        />
                      </label>

                      <label>
                        <span>Size</span>
                        <select name="size" defaultValue={selectedSize}>
                          <option value="">All sizes</option>
                          {filterSizes.map((sizeOption) => (
                            <option key={sizeOption} value={sizeOption}>
                              {sizeOption}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </label>

                      <label>
                        <span>Colour</span>
                        <select name="color" defaultValue={selectedColor}>
                          <option value="">All colours</option>
                          {filterColors.map((colorOption) => (
                            <option key={colorOption} value={colorOption}>
                              {colorOption}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </label>

                      <label>
                        <span>Sort by</span>
                        <select name="sort" defaultValue={sort}>
                          <option value="newest">Newest first</option>
                          <option value="price_asc">Price: low to high</option>
                          <option value="price_desc">Price: high to low</option>
                        </select>
                      </label>

                      <label className="shop-stock-filter">
                        <input
                          name="inStock"
                          type="checkbox"
                          value="1"
                          defaultChecked={inStockOnly}
                        />
                        <span>In stock only</span>
                      </label>
                    </div>

                    <button type="submit" className="primary-button">
                      Apply
                    </button>
                    <Link href="/shop" className="text-link">
                      Reset
                    </Link>
                  </form>
                </details>
              </div>
            )}

            {visibleProducts.length === 0 && (
              <div className="store-empty">
                {mode === "home"
                  ? "Our featured collection is being prepared."
                  : "No products match your search. Try another category or search term."}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="product-grid">
              {visibleProducts.map((product) => {
                const isSaved = saved.includes(product.id);

                return (
                  <article key={product.id}>
                    <div className="product-image">
                      <Link
                        href={`/products/${product.slug}`}
                        aria-label={`View ${product.name}`}
                      >
                        <Image
                          src={product.image}
                          alt={product.imageAlt}
                          fill
                          unoptimized
                          sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, 25vw"
                        />
                      </Link>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="icon-button wishlist-button"
                        aria-label={
                          isSaved
                            ? `Unsave ${product.name}`
                            : `Save ${product.name}`
                        }
                        aria-pressed={isSaved}
                        onClick={() => toggleSaved(product.id)}
                      >
                        <Heart
                          size={17}
                          strokeWidth={1.5}
                          fill={isSaved ? "#816334" : "none"}
                          color={isSaved ? "#816334" : "currentColor"}
                        />
                      </button>

                      {!product.inStock && (
                        <span className="store-stock-label">Out of stock</span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="product-info">
                      <div>
                        <h3>
                          <Link href={`/products/${product.slug}`}>
                            {product.name}
                          </Link>
                        </h3>

                        <p>
                          {product.hasMultipleVariants && (
                            <span className="store-from">From </span>
                          )}

                          {money(product.price)}

                          {product.regularPrice && (
                            <del className="store-regular-price">
                              {money(product.regularPrice)}
                            </del>
                          )}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {mode === "shop" && totalPages > 1 && (
              <nav className="store-pagination" aria-label="Product pages">
                {page > 1 && <Link href={pageHref(page - 1)}>Previous</Link>}

                <span>
                  Page {page} of {totalPages}
                </span>

                {page < totalPages && (
                  <Link href={pageHref(page + 1)}>Next</Link>
                )}
              </nav>
            )}

            {/* Category-Wise Product Grids */}
            {mode === "home" &&
              categoryProducts &&
              categoryProducts.length > 0 &&
              categoryProducts.map(({ category, products: catProducts }) => {
                if (!catProducts || catProducts.length === 0) return null;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={category.id}
                    className="category-showcase-block"
                    style={{ marginTop: "3.5rem" }}
                  >
                    <div className="section-heading">
                      <div>
                        <h2>{category.name}</h2>
                        <p>
                          Explore our latest {category.name.toLowerCase()}{" "}
                          collection
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <Link
                        href={categoryHref(category.slug)}
                        className="text-link"
                      >
                        View all <ArrowRight size={14} />
                      </Link>
                    </div>

                    <div className="product-grid">
                      {catProducts.map((product) => {
                        const isSaved = saved.includes(product.id);

                        return (
                          <article key={product.id}>
                            <div className="product-image">
                              <Link
                                href={`/products/${product.slug}`}
                                aria-label={`View ${product.name}`}
                              >
                                <Image
                                  src={product.image}
                                  alt={product.imageAlt}
                                  fill
                                  unoptimized
                                  sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, 25vw"
                                />
                              </Link>

                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="icon-button wishlist-button"
                                aria-label={
                                  isSaved
                                    ? `Unsave ${product.name}`
                                    : `Save ${product.name}`
                                }
                                aria-pressed={isSaved}
                                onClick={() => toggleSaved(product.id)}
                              >
                                <Heart
                                  size={17}
                                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                                  fill={isSaved ? "#816334" : "none"}
                                  color={isSaved ? "#816334" : "currentColor"}
                                />
                              </button>

                              {!product.inStock && (
                                <span className="store-stock-label">
                                  Out of stock
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>

                            <div className="product-info">
                              <div>
                                <h3>
                                  <Link href={`/products/${product.slug}`}>
                                    {product.name}
                                  </Link>
                                </h3>

                                <p>
                                  {product.hasMultipleVariants && (
                                    <span className="store-from">From </span>
                                  )}

                                  {money(product.price)}

                                  {product.regularPrice && (
                                    <del className="store-regular-price">
                                      {money(product.regularPrice)}
                                    </del>
                                  )}
                                </p>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </article>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
          </section>
        )}

        {mode === "home" && <HomeStory sections={homeSections} />}
      </main>

      <footer className="site-footer">
        <div className="shell">
          <div className="footer-grid">
            <div className="footer-brand">
              <Brand />
              <p className="footer-description">
                Fashion and lifestyle essentials for a more considered everyday.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="footer-links">
              <h3>Explore</h3>

              {mainCategories.map((category) => (
                <Link key={category.id} href={categoryHref(category.slug)}>
                  {category.name}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="footer-links">
              <h3>BIMLAY</h3>
              <Link href="/">Home</Link>
              <Link href="/shop">All products</Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          <StoreContact />

          <div className="footer-bottom">
            © 2026 BIMLAY. All rights reserved.
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>

      <nav className="bottom-nav" aria-label="Mobile shopping navigation">
        <Link href="/" className={mode === "home" ? "active" : ""}>
          <House size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <span>Home</span>
        </Link>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={menuOpen}
          aria-controls="store-mobile-menu"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen((current) => !current)}
        >
          <Grid2X2 size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <span>Categories</span>
        </button>

        <Link
          href="/wishlist"
          className={mode === "wishlist" ? "active" : ""}
          aria-label={`Wishlist, ${saved.length} saved products`}
        >
          <Heart size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <span>Wishlist{saved.length > 0 ? ` (${saved.length})` : ""}</span>
        </Link>

        <Link href="/cart" className={mode === "cart" ? "active" : ""}>
          <ShoppingBag size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <span>Cart{cartCount > 0 ? ` (${cartCount})` : ""}</span>
        </Link>

        <Link href="/account" className={mode === "account" ? "active" : ""}>
          <UserRound size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} />
          <span>Account</span>
        </Link>
      </nav>

      {notice && (
        <div className="notice" role="status">
          <span>{notice}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="icon-button"
            aria-label="Dismiss message"
            onClick={() => setNotice("")}
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
}


===== \src\components\wishlist-page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Heart } from "lucide-react";

import {
  getWishlistProducts,
  type WishlistResult,
} from "@/app/actions/wishlist";

import {
  readWishlistIds,
  removeWishlistItems,
  useWishlistSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/wishlist-store";

function money(value: string) {
  return `৳ ${new Intl.NumberFormat("en-BD", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value))}`;
}

export default function WishlistPageContent() {
  const snapshot = useWishlistSnapshot();

  const [reply, setReply] = useState<{
    snapshot: string;
    result: WishlistResult;
  } | null>(null);

  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    getWishlistProducts(readWishlistIds(snapshot))
      .then((result) => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({ snapshot, result });
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setReply({
            snapshot,
            result: {
              ok: false,
              error: "Could not load your wishlist.",
            },
          });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [snapshot, retry]);

  function remove(ids: string[]) {
    setError("");

    try {
      removeWishlistItems(ids);
    } catch {
      setError("Could not update your wishlist. Please try again.");
    }
  }

  const loading = !reply || reply.snapshot !== snapshot;

  return (
    <section className="products-section shell wishlist-page">
      <div className="cart-heading">
        <p className="eyebrow">Your personal edit</p>
        <h1>Wishlist</h1>
        <p>Keep your favourites close.</p>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <p className="admin-form-error cart-message" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <p className="cart-updating" role="status">
          Loading your saved pieces...
        </p>
      ) : !reply.result.ok ? (
        <div className="store-empty">
          <p>{reply.result.error}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="product-small-button"
            onClick={() => setRetry((current) => current + 1)}
          >
            Try again
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          {reply.result.unavailableIds.length > 0 && (
            <div className="wishlist-unavailable">
              <p>
                {reply.result.unavailableIds.length} saved
                product(s) are no longer available.
              </p>

              <button
                type="button"
                className="product-small-button"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (reply.result.ok) {
                    remove(reply.result.unavailableIds);
                  }
                }}
              >
                Remove unavailable products
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {reply.result.products.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="cart-empty">
              <Heart size={38} strokeWidth={1.2} />
              <h2>Make room for your favourites.</h2>
              <p>Tap a heart to save a piece you love.</p>

              <Link href="/shop" className="primary-button">
                Explore the collection
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="product-grid">
              {reply.result.products.map((product) => (
                <article key={product.id}>
                  <div className="product-image">
                    <Link
                      href={`/products/${product.slug}`}
                      aria-label={`View ${product.name}`}
                    >
                      <Image
                        src={product.image}
                        alt={product.imageAlt}
                        fill
                        unoptimized
                        sizes="(max-width: 640px) 50vw, 25vw"
                      />
                    </Link>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="icon-button wishlist-button"
                      aria-label={`Remove ${product.name} from wishlist`}
                      onClick={() => remove([product.id])}
                    >
                      <Heart
                        size={17}
                        fill="#816334"
                        color="#816334"
                      />
                    </button>

                    {!product.inStock && (
                      <span className="store-stock-label">
                        Out of stock
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="product-info">
                    <div>
                      <h3>
                        <Link href={`/products/${product.slug}`}>
                          {product.name}
                        </Link>
                      </h3>

                      <p>
                        {product.hasMultipleVariants && (
                          <span className="store-from">From </span>
                        )}

                        {money(product.price)}

                        {product.regularPrice && (
                          <del className="store-regular-price">
                            {money(product.regularPrice)}
                          </del>
                        )}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}


===== \src\components\admin\image-upload.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Upload } from "lucide-react";

type UploadedImage = {
  url: string;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  bytes: number;
};

type Props = {
  onUploaded?: (image: UploadedImage) => void;
};

export default function ImageUpload({ onUploaded }: Props) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [image, setImage] = useState<UploadedImage | null>(null);

  async function uploadImage() {
    const file = inputRef.current?.files?.[0];

    setError("");

    if (!file) {
      setError("Choose an image first.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      !["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"].includes(file.type)
    ) {
      setError("Choose a JPG, PNG or WebP image.");
      return;
    }

    if (file.size === 0 || file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) {
      setError("Choose a non-empty image smaller than 8 MB.");
      return;
    }

    setPending(true);

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.set("image", file);

      const response = await fetch("/api/admin/uploads", {
        method: "POST",
        body: formData,
      });

      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          typeof result.error === "string"
            ? result.error
            : "Upload failed.",
        );
      }

      if (
        typeof result.url !== "string" ||
        typeof result.width !== "number" ||
        typeof result.height !== "number" ||
        typeof result.bytes !== "number"
      ) {
        throw new Error("Unexpected upload response.");
      }

      const uploaded: UploadedImage = {
        url: result.url,
        width: result.width,
        height: result.height,
        bytes: result.bytes,
      };

      setImage(uploaded);
      onUploaded?.(uploaded);

      if (inputRef.current) {
        inputRef.current.value = "";
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Upload failed. Please try again.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="image-upload">
      <label className="image-upload-label">
        <span>Choose an image</span>

        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="file"
          accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
          disabled={pending}
        />
      </label>

      <p className="category-help">
        JPG, PNG or WebP · Maximum 8 MB · Automatically optimised
      </p>

      <button
        type="button"
        className="primary-button"
        onClick={uploadImage}
        disabled={pending}
      >
        <Upload size={16} />
        {pending ? "Uploading..." : "Upload image"}
      </button>

      <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
        {error && <p className="admin-form-error">{error}</p>}

        {image && (
          <div className="image-upload-result">
            <p className="category-success">
              Image uploaded successfully.
            </p>

            <div className="image-upload-preview">
              <Image
                src={image.url}
                alt="Uploaded image preview"
                width={image.width}
                height={image.height}
                unoptimized
              />
            </div>

            <p className="category-help">
              {image.width} × {image.height} px ·{" "}
              {Math.ceil(image.bytes / 1024)} KB · WebP
            </p>

            <label className="image-upload-label">
              <span>Saved image URL</span>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={image.url}
                readOnly
                onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()}
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== \src\lib\admin-auth.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

const COOKIE_NAME = "bimlay_admin_session";
const SESSION_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60;

function tokenHash(token: string) {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
}

export async function createAdminSession(adminId: string) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  const previousToken = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (previousToken && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(previousToken)) {
    await db.adminSession.deleteMany({
      where: {
        tokenHash: tokenHash(previousToken),
      },
    });
  }

  const token = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
  const expiresAt = new Date(
    Date.now() + SESSION_SECONDS * 1000,
  );

  await db.adminSession.deleteMany({
    where: {
      adminId,
      expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
    },
  });

  await db.adminSession.create({
    data: {
      adminId,
      tokenHash: tokenHash(token),
      expiresAt,
    },
  });

  cookieStore.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: SESSION_SECONDS,
    expires: expiresAt,
  });
}

export async function getAdmin() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (!token || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(token)) {
    return null;
  }

  const session = await db.adminSession.findUnique({
    where: {
      tokenHash: tokenHash(token),
    },
    select: {
      expiresAt: true,
      admin: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          name: true,
          email: true,
          role: true,
          isActive: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (
    !session ||
    session.expiresAt.getTime() <= Date.now() ||
    !session.admin.isActive
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return session.admin;
}

export async function requireAdmin() {
  const admin = await getAdmin();

  if (!admin) {
    redirect("/admin/login");
  }

  return admin;
}

export async function deleteAdminSession() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (token && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(token)) {
    await db.adminSession.deleteMany({
      where: {
        tokenHash: tokenHash(token),
      },
    });
  }

  cookieStore.delete(COOKIE_NAME);
}


===== \src\lib\cart-store.ts =====
"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "bimlay-cart-v1";
const CHANGE_EVENT = "bimlay-cart-change";

type CartItem = {
  variantId: string;
  quantity: number;
};

type CartState = {
  items: CartItem[];
  completedOrders: string[];
};

function getSnapshot(): string {
  try {
    return window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) ?? "[]";
  } catch {
    return "[]";
  }
}

function getServerSnapshot(): string {
  return "[]";
}

function parseState(raw: string): CartState {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);

    // Supports carts saved before this update.
    const source = Array.isArray(parsed)
      ? parsed
      : parsed?.items;

    const completed = Array.isArray(parsed?.completedOrders)
      ? parsed.completedOrders
      : [];

    const items = new Map<string, number>();

    if (Array.isArray(source)) {
      for (const entry of source.slice(0, 50)) {
        if (
          entry &&
          typeof entry.variantId === "string" &&
          entry.variantId.length > 0 &&
          entry.variantId.length <= 100 &&
          Number.isInteger(entry.quantity) &&
          entry.quantity >= 1 &&
          entry.quantity <= 99
        ) {
          items.set(entry.variantId, entry.quantity);
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      items: [...items].map(([variantId, quantity]) => ({
        variantId,
        quantity,
      })),
      completedOrders: completed
        .filter(
          (id: unknown): id is string =>
            typeof id === "string" && id.length <= 100,
        )
        .slice(-20),
    };
  } catch {
    return { items: [], completedOrders: [] };
  }
}

function writeState(state: CartState) {
  window.localStorage.setItem(
    STORAGE_KEY,
    JSON.stringify(state),
  );

  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CHANGE_EVENT));
}

function subscribe(callback: () => void) {
  window.addEventListener("storage", callback);
  window.addEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, callback);

  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("storage", callback);
    window.removeEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

export function useCartSnapshot(): string {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    getSnapshot,
    getServerSnapshot,
  );
}

export function readCartItems(snapshot: string): CartItem[] {
  return parseState(snapshot).items;
}

export function useCartCount(): number {
  return readCartItems(useCartSnapshot()).reduce(
    (total, item) => total + item.quantity,
    0,
  );
}

export function getCartQuantity(variantId: string): number {
  return (
    readCartItems(getSnapshot()).find(
      (item) => item.variantId === variantId,
    )?.quantity ?? 0
  );
}

export function setCartQuantity(
  variantId: string,
  quantity: number,
) {
  if (
    !variantId ||
    variantId.length > 100 ||
    !Number.isInteger(quantity) ||
    quantity < 1 ||
    quantity > 99
  ) {
    throw new Error("Invalid cart quantity.");
  }

  const state = parseState(getSnapshot());

  const existing = state.items.find(
    (item) => item.variantId === variantId,
  );

  if (existing) {
    existing.quantity = quantity;
  } else {
    if (state.items.length >= 50) {
      throw new Error("Your bag can contain up to 50 different items.");
    }

    state.items.push({ variantId, quantity });
  }

  writeState(state);
}

export function removeCartItem(variantId: string) {
  const state = parseState(getSnapshot());

  state.items = state.items.filter(
    (item) => item.variantId !== variantId,
  );

  writeState(state);
}

export function completeCartOrder(
  orderId: string,
  purchasedItems: CartItem[],
) {
  const state = parseState(getSnapshot());

  if (state.completedOrders.includes(orderId)) {
    return;
  }

  const purchased = new Map(
    purchasedItems.map((item) => [
      item.variantId,
      item.quantity,
    ]),
  );

  state.items = state.items
    .map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      quantity: Math.max(
        0,
        item.quantity - (purchased.get(item.variantId) ?? 0),
      ),
    }))
    .filter((item) => item.quantity > 0);

  state.completedOrders = [
    ...state.completedOrders,
    orderId,
  ].slice(-20);

  writeState(state);
}


===== \src\lib\catalog.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { Prisma } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

export async function getStoreCategories() {
  const categories = await db.category.findMany({
    where: {
      isActive: true,
      OR: [
        { parentId: null },
        { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [
      { sortOrder: "asc" },
      { name: "asc" },
    ],
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      slug: true,
      imageUrl: true,
      parentId: true,
      parent: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
    },
  });

  return categories.map((category) => ({
    id: category.id,
    name: category.name,
    slug: category.slug,
    imageUrl: category.imageUrl,
    parentId: category.parentId,
    label: category.parent
      ? `${category.parent.name} / ${category.name}`
      : category.name,
  }));
}

export type StoreCategory = Awaited<
  ReturnType<typeof getStoreCategories>
>[number];

export type ShopSort = "newest" | "price_asc" | "price_desc";

type CatalogOptions = {
  featured?: boolean;
  categoryId?: string;
  query?: string;
  page?: number;
  minPrice?: number;
  maxPrice?: number;
  size?: string;
  color?: string;
  inStockOnly?: boolean;
  sort?: ShopSort;
};

export async function getStoreProducts({
  featured = false,
  categoryId,
  query = "",
  page = 1,
  minPrice,
  maxPrice,
  size,
  color,
  inStockOnly = false,
  sort = "newest",
}: CatalogOptions = {}) {
  const pageSize = featured ? 8 : 12;

  const variantWhere: Prisma.ProductVariantWhereInput = {
    isActive: true,
    ...(size
      ? { size: { equals: size, mode: "insensitive" as const } }
      : {}),
    ...(color
      ? { color: { equals: color, mode: "insensitive" as const } }
      : {}),
    ...(inStockOnly ? { stock: { gt: 0 } } : {}),
    ...(minPrice !== undefined || maxPrice !== undefined
      ? {
          price: {
            ...(minPrice !== undefined
              ? { gte: new Prisma.Decimal(minPrice) }
              : {}),
            ...(maxPrice !== undefined
              ? { lte: new Prisma.Decimal(maxPrice) }
              : {}),
          },
        }
      : {}),
  };

  const filters: Prisma.ProductWhereInput[] = [
    { status: "ACTIVE" },
    {
      category: {
        is: {
          isActive: true,
          OR: [
            { parentId: null },
            { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
          ],
        },
      },
    },
    { variants: { some: variantWhere } },
    { images: { some: {} } },
  ];

  if (featured) {
    filters.push({ isFeatured: true });
  }

  if (categoryId) {
    filters.push({
      OR: [
        { categoryId },
        { category: { is: { parentId: categoryId } } },
      ],
    });
  }

  if (query) {
    filters.push({
      OR: [
        { name: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" } },
        {
          variants: {
            some: {
              isActive: true,
              sku: { contains: query, mode: "insensitive" },
            },
          },
        },
      ],
    });
  }

  const where: Prisma.ProductWhereInput = { AND: filters };

  const total = await db.product.count({ where });
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
  const currentPage = Math.min(
    Math.max(1, Math.floor(page)),
    totalPages,
  );
  const offset = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize;
  const priceSort = sort === "price_asc" || sort === "price_desc";

  let sortedProductIds: string[] | null = null;

  if (priceSort) {
    const grouped = await db.productVariant.groupBy({
      by: ["productId"],
      where: {
        ...variantWhere,
        product: { is: where },
      },
      _min: { price: true },
      orderBy: [
        {
          _min: {
            price: sort === "price_asc" ? "asc" : "desc",
          },
        },
        { productId: "asc" },
      ],
      skip: offset,
      take: pageSize,
    });

    sortedProductIds = grouped.map((item) => item.productId);
  }

  const products = await db.product.findMany({
    where: sortedProductIds
      ? { id: { in: sortedProductIds } }
      : where,
    ...(sortedProductIds
      ? {}
      : {
          take: pageSize,
          skip: offset,
          orderBy: [
            { createdAt: "desc" as const },
            { id: "desc" as const },
          ],
        }),
    select: {
      id: true,
      name: true,
      slug: true,
      shortDescription: true,
      images: {
        orderBy: { sortOrder: "asc" },
        take: 1,
        select: {
          url: true,
          alt: true,
        },
      },
      variants: {
        where: variantWhere,
        orderBy: { price: "asc" },
        select: {
          price: true,
          compareAtPrice: true,
          stock: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (sortedProductIds) {
    const positions = new Map(
      sortedProductIds.map((id, index) => [id, index]),
    );

    products.sort(
      (a, b) =>
        (positions.get(a.id) ?? 0) -
        (positions.get(b.id) ?? 0),
    );
  }

  return {
    total,
    currentPage,
    totalPages,
    products: products.map((product) => {
      const variant = priceSort
        ? product.variants[0]
        : product.variants.find((item) => item.stock > 0) ??
          product.variants[0];

      return {
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        slug: product.slug,
        shortDescription: product.shortDescription ?? "",
        image: product.images[0].url,
        imageAlt: product.images[0].alt,
        price: variant.price.toFixed(2),
        regularPrice:
          variant.compareAtPrice?.toFixed(2) ?? null,
        inStock: product.variants.some(
          (item) => item.stock > 0,
        ),
        hasMultipleVariants: product.variants.length > 1,
      };
    }),
  };
}

export type StoreProduct = Awaited<
  ReturnType<typeof getStoreProducts>
>["products"][number];

export async function getShopFilterOptions() {
  const activeVariant: Prisma.ProductVariantWhereInput = {
    isActive: true,
    product: {
      is: {
        status: "ACTIVE",
        images: { some: {} },
        category: {
          is: {
            isActive: true,
            OR: [
              { parentId: null },
              { parent: { is: { isActive: true } } },
            ],
          },
        },
      },
    },
  };

  const [sizeRows, colorRows] = await Promise.all([
    db.productVariant.groupBy({
      by: ["size"],
      where: {
        ...activeVariant,
        size: { not: null },
      },
      orderBy: { size: "asc" },
    }),
    db.productVariant.groupBy({
      by: ["color"],
      where: {
        ...activeVariant,
        color: { not: null },
      },
      orderBy: { color: "asc" },
    }),
  ]);

  return {
    sizes: sizeRows
      .map((row) => row.size)
      .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value)),
    colors: colorRows
      .map((row) => row.color)
      .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value)),
  };
}


===== \src\lib\coupons.ts =====
import "server-only";

import {
  Prisma,
  type Coupon,
} from "@/generated/prisma/client";

export class CouponValidationError extends Error {}

export function normalizeCouponCode(value: unknown): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new CouponValidationError("Enter a valid coupon code.");
  }

  const code = value.trim().toUpperCase();

  if (!/^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_-]{2,31}$/.test(code)) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "Coupon code must be 3 to 32 English letters, numbers, hyphens or underscores.",
    );
  }

  return code;
}

type CouponForDiscount = Pick<
  Coupon,
  | "isActive"
  | "kind"
  | "value"
  | "minimumSubtotal"
  | "maximumDiscount"
  | "usageLimit"
  | "usedCount"
  | "startsAt"
  | "expiresAt"
>;

export function calculateCouponDiscount(
  coupon: CouponForDiscount,
  subtotal: Prisma.Decimal,
  now: Date,
): Prisma.Decimal {
  if (!coupon.isActive) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "This coupon is not available.",
    );
  }

  if (coupon.startsAt && now < coupon.startsAt) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "This coupon is not active yet.",
    );
  }

  if (coupon.expiresAt && now >= coupon.expiresAt) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "This coupon has expired.",
    );
  }

  if (
    coupon.usageLimit !== null &&
    coupon.usedCount >= coupon.usageLimit
  ) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "This coupon has reached its usage limit.",
    );
  }

  if (subtotal.lt(coupon.minimumSubtotal)) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      `This coupon needs a minimum subtotal of ৳${coupon.minimumSubtotal.toFixed(2)}.`,
    );
  }

  if (
    coupon.value.lte(0) ||
    (coupon.kind === "PERCENT" && coupon.value.gt(100))
  ) {
    throw new CouponValidationError(
      "This coupon is not configured correctly.",
    );
  }

  let discount =
    coupon.kind === "PERCENT"
      ? subtotal.mul(coupon.value).div(100)
      : coupon.value;

  if (
    coupon.maximumDiscount &&
    discount.gt(coupon.maximumDiscount)
  ) {
    discount = coupon.maximumDiscount;
  }

  if (discount.gt(subtotal)) {
    discount = subtotal;
  }

  return discount.toDecimalPlaces(
    2,
    Prisma.Decimal.ROUND_HALF_UP,
  );
}


===== \src\lib\customer-auth.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";

const COOKIE_NAME = "bimlay_customer_session";
const SESSION_SECONDS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60;

function hashToken(token: string) {
  return createHash("sha256").update(token).digest("hex");
}

export async function createCustomerSession(
  customerId: string,
) {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const previousToken = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (previousToken && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(previousToken)) {
    await db.customerSession.deleteMany({
      where: {
        tokenHash: hashToken(previousToken),
      },
    });
  }

  const token = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
  const expiresAt = new Date(
    Date.now() + SESSION_SECONDS * 1000,
  );

  await db.customerSession.deleteMany({
    where: {
      customerId,
      expiresAt: { lte: new Date() },
    },
  });

  await db.customerSession.create({
    data: {
      customerId,
      tokenHash: hashToken(token),
      expiresAt,
    },
  });

  cookieStore.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
    sameSite: "lax",
    path: "/",
    maxAge: SESSION_SECONDS,
    expires: expiresAt,
  });
}

export async function getCustomer() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (!token || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(token)) {
    return null;
  }

  const session = await db.customerSession.findUnique({
    where: {
      tokenHash: hashToken(token),
    },
    select: {
      expiresAt: true,
      customer: {
        select: {
          id: true,
          name: true,
          email: true,
          isActive: true,
        },
      },
    },
  });

  if (
    !session ||
    session.expiresAt.getTime() <= Date.now() ||
    !session.customer.isActive
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return session.customer;
}

export async function requireCustomer() {
  const customer = await getCustomer();

  if (!customer) {
    redirect("/account/login");
  }

  return customer;
}

export async function deleteCustomerSession() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const token = cookieStore.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value;

  if (token && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(token)) {
    await db.customerSession.deleteMany({
      where: {
        tokenHash: hashToken(token),
      },
    });
  }

  cookieStore.delete(COOKIE_NAME);
}


===== \src\lib\db.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { PrismaPg } from "@prisma/adapter-pg";
import { PrismaClient } from "@/generated/prisma/client";

function requiredEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name];

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(`Missing environment variable: ${name}`);
  }

  return value;
}

function createPrismaClient() {
  const adapter = new PrismaPg({
    host: requiredEnv("PGHOST"),
    port: Number(requiredEnv("PGPORT")),
    user: requiredEnv("PGUSER"),
    password: requiredEnv("PGPASSWORD"),
    database: requiredEnv("PGDATABASE"),
    max: 5,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 5000,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30000,
  });

  return new PrismaClient({ adapter });
}

const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined;
};

export const db =
  globalForPrisma.prisma ?? createPrismaClient();

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") {
  globalForPrisma.prisma = db;
}

===== \src\lib\media-storage.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { stat, mkdir, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { v2 as cloudinary } from "cloudinary";

export const PRODUCT_MEDIA_DIR = path.resolve(
  process.cwd(),
  "storage",
  "product-images",
);

export const MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN =
  /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}\.webp$/;

function cloudinaryEnabled(): boolean {
  const mode = process.env.MEDIA_STORAGE ?? "local";

  if (mode !== "local" && mode !== "cloudinary") {
    throw new Error("MEDIA_STORAGE must be local or cloudinary.");
  }

  return mode === "cloudinary";
}

function cloudinaryClient() {
  const cloudName = process.env.CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME;
  const apiKey = process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_KEY;
  const apiSecret = process.env.CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET;

  if (!cloudName || !apiKey || !apiSecret) {
    throw new Error("Cloudinary credentials are missing.");
  }

  cloudinary.config({
    cloud_name: cloudName,
    api_key: apiKey,
    api_secret: apiSecret,
    secure: true,
  });

  return cloudinary;
}

function publicId(filename: string): string {
  if (!MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid image filename.");
  }

  return `bimlay/${filename.slice(0, -".webp".length)}`;
}

export async function saveMediaImage(
  filename: string,
  image: Buffer,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid image filename.");
  }

  if (!cloudinaryEnabled()) {
    await mkdir(PRODUCT_MEDIA_DIR, { recursive: true });

    await writeFile(
      path.join(PRODUCT_MEDIA_DIR, filename),
      image,
      { flag: "wx" },
    );

    return;
  }

  const client = cloudinaryClient();
  const expectedId = publicId(filename);

  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const stream = client.uploader.upload_stream(
      {
        resource_type: "image",
        public_id: expectedId,
        overwrite: false,
        unique_filename: false,
        format: "webp",
      },
      (error, result) => {
        if (error) {
          reject(error);
        } else if (!result || result.public_id !== expectedId) {
          reject(new Error("Unexpected image upload response."));
        } else {
          resolve();
        }
      },
    );

    stream.on("error", reject);
    stream.end(image);
  });
}

export function cloudinaryImageUrl(
  filename: string,
): string | null {
  if (!cloudinaryEnabled()) return null;

  const client = cloudinaryClient();

  return client.url(publicId(filename), {
    secure: true,
    format: "webp",
    resource_type: "image",
    type: "upload",
  });
}

export async function mediaImageExists(
  url: string,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!url.startsWith("/media/")) return false;

  const filename = url.slice("/media/".length);

  if (!MEDIA_FILENAME_PATTERN.test(filename)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (!cloudinaryEnabled()) {
    try {
      const file = await stat(
        path.join(PRODUCT_MEDIA_DIR, filename),
      );

      return file.isFile();
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof Error &&
        "code" in error &&
        error.code === "ENOENT"
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      throw error;
    }
  }

  try {
    await cloudinaryClient().api.resource(
      publicId(filename),
      { resource_type: "image" },
    );

    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    if (
      error &&
      typeof error === "object" &&
      "http_code" in error &&
      error.http_code === 404
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    throw error;
  }
}


===== \src\lib\order-rules.ts =====
export const ORDER_STATUSES = [
  "PENDING",
  "CONFIRMED",
  "PROCESSING",
  "SHIPPED",
  "DELIVERED",
  "CANCELLED",
  "RETURNED",
] as const;

export type OrderState = typeof ORDER_STATUSES[number];

export const NEXT_ORDER_STATES: Record<
  OrderState,
  readonly OrderState[]
> = {
  PENDING: ["CONFIRMED", "CANCELLED"],
  CONFIRMED: ["PROCESSING", "CANCELLED"],
  PROCESSING: ["SHIPPED", "CANCELLED"],
  SHIPPED: ["DELIVERED", "RETURNED"],
  DELIVERED: ["RETURNED"],
  CANCELLED: [],
  RETURNED: [],
};

export function isOrderState(
  value: string,
): value is OrderState {
  return ORDER_STATUSES.some((status) => status === value);
}

export function allowedPaymentStates(
  status: OrderState,
  paymentStatus: string,
): string[] {
  if (paymentStatus === "UNPAID" && status === "DELIVERED") {
    return ["PAID"];
  }

  if (
    paymentStatus === "PAID" &&
    (status === "CANCELLED" || status === "RETURNED")
  ) {
    return ["REFUNDED"];
  }

  return [];
}

export function label(value: string): string {
  return value
    .toLowerCase()
    .split("_")
    .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1))
    .join(" ");
}


===== \src\lib\password.ts =====
import {
  randomBytes,
  scrypt,
  timingSafeEqual,
} from "node:crypto";

const COST = 131072;
const BLOCK_SIZE = 8;
const PARALLELIZATION = 1;
const KEY_LENGTH = 64;
const MAX_MEMORY = 256 * 1024 * 1024;

function deriveKey(
  password: string,
  salt: string,
): Promise<Buffer> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    scrypt(
      password,
      salt,
      KEY_LENGTH,
      {
        N: COST,
        r: BLOCK_SIZE,
        p: PARALLELIZATION,
        maxmem: MAX_MEMORY,
      },
      (error, key) => {
        if (error) {
          reject(error);
          return;
        }

        resolve(key);
      },
    );
  });
}

export async function hashPassword(
  password: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const salt = randomBytes(16).toString("hex");
  const key = await deriveKey(password, salt);

  return [
    "scrypt",
    COST,
    BLOCK_SIZE,
    PARALLELIZATION,
    salt,
    key.toString("hex"),
  ].join("$");
}

export async function verifyPassword(
  password: string,
  storedHash: string,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const parts = storedHash.split("$");

  if (parts.length !== 6) {
    return false;
  }

  const [algorithm, cost, blockSize, parallelization, salt, hash] =
    parts;

  if (
    algorithm !== "scrypt" ||
    cost !== String(COST) ||
    blockSize !== String(BLOCK_SIZE) ||
    parallelization !== String(PARALLELIZATION) ||
    !/^[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(salt) ||
    !/^[a-f0-9]{128}$/.test(hash)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const actual = await deriveKey(password, salt);
  const expected = Buffer.from(hash, "hex");

  return timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
}


===== \src\lib\wishlist-store.ts =====
"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "bimlay-wishlist-v1";
const CHANGE_EVENT = "bimlay-wishlist-change";
const MAX_ITEMS = 50;

function getSnapshot(): string {
  try {
    return window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) ?? "[]";
  } catch {
    return "[]";
  }
}

function getServerSnapshot(): string {
  return "[]";
}

export function readWishlistIds(snapshot: string): string[] {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(snapshot);

    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) {
      return [];
    }

    return [
      ...new Set(
        parsed.filter(
          (value): value is string =>
            typeof value === "string" &&
            value.length > 0 &&
            value.length <= 100,
        ),
      ),
    ].slice(0, MAX_ITEMS);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function subscribe(callback: () => void) {
  window.addEventListener("storage", callback);
  window.addEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, callback);

  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("storage", callback);
    window.removeEventListener(CHANGE_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

function saveIds(ids: string[]) {
  window.localStorage.setItem(
    STORAGE_KEY,
    JSON.stringify(ids),
  );

  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(CHANGE_EVENT));
}

export function useWishlistSnapshot(): string {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribe,
    getSnapshot,
    getServerSnapshot,
  );
}

export function toggleWishlist(productId: string) {
  if (!productId || productId.length > 100) {
    throw new Error("Invalid product.");
  }

  const ids = readWishlistIds(getSnapshot());

  if (ids.includes(productId)) {
    saveIds(ids.filter((id) => id !== productId));
    return;
  }

  if (ids.length >= MAX_ITEMS) {
    throw new Error("Your wishlist can contain up to 50 products.");
  }

  saveIds([productId, ...ids]);
}

export function removeWishlistItems(productIds: string[]) {
  const remove = new Set(productIds);

  saveIds(
    readWishlistIds(getSnapshot()).filter(
      (id) => !remove.has(id),
    ),
  );
}
